# Cycle history (archived from CLAUDE.md)

This file archives the per-cycle status snapshots that previously lived in `CLAUDE.md`'s "Status snapshot" section. Live `CLAUDE.md` retains only current cycle pointers (workshop version, mechanisms, blueprints, harness summary, landmines summary, next-TBD pointer). For current cycle state, see `CLAUDE.md`. Entries are listed in chronological close order, preserved verbatim from their original CLAUDE.md authoring.

> Backfill note: the v0.105.0 / v0.105.0.1 / v0.105.0.2 / v0.105.0.3 entries are not yet captured here; the cycles closed without an inline cycle-history append. The v0.106.0 + v0.106.0.1 entries below were authored as a partial backfill from the 2026-06-15 handoff cycle.

## v0.121.0 (closed 2026-06-16) — test-coverage arc MINOR

Four-phase test-coverage arc executed in a dedicated worktree on a long-lived feature branch. Audit + 3 risk-weighted implementation cycles closing the `installer_migration` axis on the top-3 high-blast-radius surfaces. Squash-merged via PR #11 at commit `552b272e`.

**New audit infrastructure**:
- `scripts/regen-coverage-matrix.js` — re-runnable Node.js audit; enumerates all blueprints + mechanisms, scores each on 6 axes per arc-design.md, applies risk-weighted prioritization. Preserves qualitative notes across re-runs.
- `scripts/lib/coverage-rubric.js` — per-axis pure-function scorers + composite + axisWeight + recentIncidents. spawnSync over execSync for shell-injection hardening.
- `scripts/render-coverage-audit.js` — markdown renderer from coverage-matrix.json.
- `platform/test/coverage-matrix.json` — 30 surfaces scored.
- `platform/test/blast-radius-seed.json` — hand-curated tiers (high/med/low) + rationales per surface.

**Test fixtures** (pre-migration shapes):
- `platform/test/seed-vault/spice/projects/Legacy Project/` (5 files)
- `platform/test/seed-vault/spice/finance-legacy/` (11 files)
- `platform/test/seed-vault/spice/entity-create-legacy/` (3 files)

**Harness extension** (`platform/test/run-seed-migrations.js`):
- Module-level `makeFsAdapter(root)` helper (shared by 4 migrate-family functions; refactored from the original `runMigrateFamily()` + `runProjectMigrateFamily()` duplicates).
- `runProjectMigrateFamily()` — HC-V01190-PROJ-SEED-MIGRATE-A1..G1 (16 sub-asserts across 7 sub-families: sections + sections-hub + close-repair + empty-wikilink-repair + todo-backfill + idempotency + history).
- `runFinanceMigrateFamily()` — HC-V01190-FIN-SEED-MIGRATE-A1..I2 (50 sub-asserts across 9 sub-families: hub/defaults + debt + budget + paycheck + months + nav + invoice + idempotency + history).
- `runEntityCreateMigrateFamily()` — HC-V01190-EC-SEED-MIGRATE-A1..D2 (15 sub-asserts across 4 sub-families: applyNewEntityButtons + applyEntityCreateGuardMigration + idempotency + history).
- Total: 81 new sub-asserts.

**install.js exports** (pure additive, no behavior change): 13 new `module.exports.apply*` lines (4 project + 8 finance + 1 entity-create).

**Composite lifts** (all met or exceeded the design's +0.15 target):
- blueprint/project: 0.617 → 0.755 (+0.14)
- blueprint/finance: 0.629 → 0.783 (+0.154)
- mechanism/entity-create: 0.778 → 0.983 (+0.205)

**Pattern established**: direct-invocation migrate-family recipe — now battle-tested 4 times (HC-V01174 v0.117.4 + 3 impl cycles). Recipe: synthetic manifest + tmp vault from `Legacy <surface>/` fixture + shared `makeFsAdapter` + `Notice` shim + 2-pass invocation with idempotency snapshots. Carry-forward for v1.1.0: codify in `Docs/agent-guides/migration-regression-net.md`.

**Production-relevant discoveries** (filed as v0.120.x carry-forwards):
1. `applyFinancePaycheckDefaultsDebtBackfill` phase-1 (`_pcdBackfillExistingExpenses`) lacks the `__debt_links_migrated` idempotency marker that phase-2 uses. Phase-1 re-injects `debt:` lines on every install pass against items that lost their `debt:` continuation due to phase-2's orphan-append YAML mangling. Real and reproducible on the finance-legacy fixture.
2. Latent install-order bug: real install order runs `applyProjectSectionsMigration` BEFORE `applyProjectSectionsCloseRepair`. On a project with malformed `-"[[--]]"` YAML close, `_ensureSectionsFrontmatter` regex fails to match and `sections[]` is silently never injected. Close-repair fixes the YAML on the same pass but sections-migration's idempotency guard prevents re-injection on subsequent installs.

**CodeQL findings introduced by the arc** — all fixed in the same PR:
1. HIGH `js/incomplete-url-substring-sanitization` at `run-seed-migrations.js:1132` (HC-V01190-FIN-SEED-MIGRATE-B4): substring `includes()` tightened to YAML-key-anchored regex.
2. MEDIUM `js/shell-command-injection-from-environment` × 2 at `coverage-rubric.js:15` (tryGrep) + `:319` (recentIncidents): switched from shell-interpolated `execSync` to argv-mode `spawnSync`.

**Versions**: workshop 0.120.1 → **0.121.0**; blueprints UNCHANGED; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. Harness count UNCHANGED at 36 (extended `run-seed-migrations.js` with 3 new migrate-family functions; no new harness files).

**Preflight**: Tests 191/191 green on both macOS + Ubuntu CI matrix. `version-sync ok: 0.121.0`.

**Documentation**: 13 plan docs (`arc-{design,plan,result}.md` + `audit.md` + `impl-{1,2,3}-{design,plan,result}.md`) + 5 handoff prompts (`post-{audit,impl-1,impl-2,impl-3,arc}-handoff.md`).

See:

## v0.120.1 — note-picker fix PATCH (2026-06-16)

Reported on accuris immediately after v0.120.0 deploy: "Link a note" dropdown in `+ New Task`
dialog listed every note alphabetically and was capped at 200 BEFORE the filter ran. Vaults
with >200 notes lost everything past "D"-ish; verbatim matches past the cap returned nothing.

**Fix:** `_loadNoteList()` returns ALL notes as `{name, path, mtime}` (no cap). `repopulate(filter)`:
no filter → top 50 most-recently-modified (recency-first default); filter set → full-list
substring match, "starts-with priority + alpha" sort, cap rendered options at 50, surfaces
`"… N more — refine the filter to narrow"` hint when matches exceed cap, `"(no matches)"` when
zero. Placeholder shows total: `"Filter N notes…"`.

**Bonus latent fix:** `runInstall` (`platform/test/helpers/seed-vault-helpers.js`) `maxBuffer`
bumped 1MB → 64MB. The default 1MB cap was eating large install stdout on the second-install
idempotency check and reporting it as exit-1 with empty stderr — a misleading failure mode that
masked the real `clean run — exit 0` verdict. v0.120.0 preflight passed because it ran BEFORE
the seed rebaseline; AFTER rebaseline the seed crossed the threshold. Now error message also
captures `e.signal` + `e.message` so future buffer issues surface immediately.

Versions: workshop 0.120.0 → **0.120.1** PATCH; to-do 0.8.0 → **0.8.1** PATCH; all other
mechanisms + blueprints UNCHANGED. Harness count UNCHANGED at 35. Preflight Tests: 110/110.
DLG-20 family extended: DLG-20d (mtime carried), DLG-20e (300-note input → all 300, no cap),
DLG-20f (Z-letter notes survive the alphabet past "D"). Deployed live to accuris + headspace +
ero. See `Docs/plans/2026-06-16-v0.120.1-note-picker-fix-result.md`.

## v0.120.0 — live-render recurring + 5 UX fixes MINOR (2026-06-16)

User reported six issues on the recurring-tasks surface post-v0.119.1 + explicitly opted into
the live-render approach the v0.118.1 postmortem item #5 had flagged as the right long-term
shape.

**Six fixes:**
1. Drop unrendered `created_at: "<% tp.file.creation_date(...) %>"` Templater expression from
   `Recurring Tasks.md` template frontmatter (was visible as literal text on accuris + headspace
   because the installer copies templates as plain strings — Templater never runs over them).
2. `+ Recurring` button on `ToDoLeafActions` now opens `spice/to-do/Recurring Tasks.md` directly
   (was opening the create-task dialog).
3. `ToDoCreateTask` insert path emits `\n${line}\n` (was `\n${line}\n\n`) — no more blank lines
   between consecutive task entries on the daily.
4. `Recurring Today` SectionLabel renamed to `Recurring`.
5. `insertRecurringIntoToday` legacy emit no longer carries a leading blank between SectionLabel
   block and first task.

**Architecture shift (postmortem #5 closed):** `ToDoDailyRecurring.render()` rewritten as a
live Dataview-rendered view. Reads the registry on every render, evaluates `matchesToday`,
renders matching tasks inline into `dv.container` via `_renderRow` + `_renderInlineMarkdown`.
NO writes to today's daily file. NO sentinels. NO materialization. Mid-day-added tasks appear
on next render. Legacy `materialize()` / `insertRecurringIntoToday` static helpers preserved
for harness regression coverage but not invoked from the live render path.

**Heal migration** `stripPersistedRecurringSection` retires the persisted `Recurring Today`/`Recurring`
SectionLabel blocks + `recurring_from::` task lines + `<!-- recurring-materialized-... -->`
sentinels from existing dailies. Wired after v0.119.0/v0.119.1 migrations (which become no-ops
post-strip). Idempotent. `.sauce-backup` snapshot before write.

Versions: workshop 0.119.1 → **0.120.0** MINOR; to-do 0.7.1 → **0.8.0** MINOR; project 1.22.2
UNCHANGED. Harness count UNCHANGED at 35 (+8 HC-V0120-STRIP-1..8 in `run-seed-migrations.js`).
Preflight Tests: 110/110. **Deploy caveat caught + healed:** `materialize_once: true` (added
v0.119.1) prevents the registry-overwrite, but ALSO prevents the template fix (line a above)
from propagating via install. Hand-stripped the Templater literal on accuris + ero (headspace
already clean). See `Docs/plans/2026-06-16-v0.120.0-live-render-recurring-result.md`.

## v0.119.1 — recurring-section merge + materialize_once PATCH (2026-06-16)

Two consumer bugs closed immediately post-v0.119.0 deploy on accuris:

**Bug 1 — Duplicate "Recurring Today" SectionLabel blocks.** v0.7.0's additive sentinel
correctly materialized only NEW tasks per call, but each call routed through
`insertRecurringIntoToday`'s create-new-block path → daily ended up with one block per call.
Fix: detect existing "Recurring Today" block via regex; append to its section when present;
create-new only when absent.

**Bug 2 — Pre-existing data-loss bug (since v0.116.0).** `spice/to-do/Recurring Tasks.md`
files[] entry lacked `materialize_once: true`. Every install with a non-identical source SHA
overwrote the user's accumulated registry to the empty template. v0.119.0 deploy triggered this
on accuris (lost 2 tasks) + headspace (lost 1 task); restored from `.bak`. Fix: add
`materialize_once: true` to the manifest entry.

NEW `mergeDuplicateRecurringSections` heal migration walks every daily and merges duplicate
"Recurring Today" blocks in place. Idempotent. `.sauce-backup` snapshot before write.

Versions: workshop 0.119.0 → **0.119.1** PATCH; to-do 0.7.0 → **0.7.1** PATCH. Harness count
UNCHANGED at 35 (+13 sub-asserts: HC-V0119-INSERT-1..5 + HC-V0119-MERGE-1..7). Preflight Tests:
102/102. See `Docs/plans/2026-06-16-v0.119.1-recurring-section-merge-and-materialize-once-result.md`.

## v0.119.0 — to-do hot-fixes + thrash-defenses MINOR (2026-06-16)

Closed two live consumer-visible bugs surfaced post-v0.118.1 on the accuris vault, and landed
the Cluster A "would-have-stopped-the-thrash" verification net from the v0.118.1 cycle postmortem.

**Bug fixes (consumer-facing):**

- **(b) Additive sentinel for recurring-task materialization.** Old `<!-- recurring-materialized-YYYY-MM-DD -->`
  date-only sentinel conflated "already ran today" with "nothing to do" — mid-day-added recurring
  tasks went invisible until the user hand-deleted the sentinel. v0.7.0 replaces with per-task hash
  set: `<!-- recurring-materialized-YYYY-MM-DD: hash1,hash2,... -->`. On every render: parse
  registry → compute 7-char sha1 hashes → materialize entries not in the set → append hashes.
  Idempotent (no file write if set unchanged). NEW `applyRecurringSentinelV070Migration` heals
  legacy date-only sentinels in place by rewriting to the empty-set form; next Obsidian re-render
  populates with current hashes.
- **(c) Markdown rendering in live-render to-do widgets.** `ToDoDailyUnassignedMeetings._renderTaskRow`
  + `ToDoDailyProjectGroups._renderTaskRow` previously used `txt.textContent = ...` — `[label](url)`
  and `[[wikilink]]` rendered as raw text. v0.7.0 adds private `_tokenizeInline` +
  `_renderInlineMarkdown` methods (mirroring the established pattern in
  `space-daily-dashboard.js`); emits raw `<a href>` for external URLs (with `target="_blank"
  rel="noopener noreferrer"` + scheme allowlist) and `<a class="internal-link" data-href>` for
  wikilinks. NEW DOM-render stub at `platform/test/helpers/dom-render-stub.js` (narrow, NOT the
  full shared #2 helper); NEW `platform/test/run-todo-markdown-render.js` harness with TDM-1..6
  cases.

**Verification net (Cluster A from v0.118.1 postmortem):**

- **#1 customJS static-vs-instance contract test.** NEW `platform/test/run-customjs-contract.js`
  scans `platform/customjs/` + `platform/blueprints/*/helpers/` for class definitions and
  workshop-wide for `customJS.X.member` callsites. Detect-by-shape: if a class has ANY instance
  method, customJS stores it as an instance and members called via the dispatcher must be
  non-static. Five self-test fixtures under `platform/test/fixtures/customjs-contract/`
  (CCONTRACT-1..5). Live-tree mode (CCONTRACT-LIVE) wired into preflight; future regressions
  fail the build. Would have caught v0.116.0's RecurrenceParser/TaskParser bug at preflight.
- **#4 anchor lint + PR checklist + fixture landmine.** NEW `scripts/lint-display-markers.js`
  flags regex literals + string-prefix checks anchored on `^## ` / `^### ` heading patterns.
  Baseline-aware (`scripts/lint-display-markers-baseline.json` grandfathers existing matches;
  only NEW warnings fail). Opt-out marker `// lint-display-markers:allow <reason>`. NEW
  `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` adds migration-consumer-enumeration + fixture-regeneration
  checklist. NEW `code-conventions.md` § "Stable anchors vs display markers" subsection.
- **#7 workshop self-subscription of to-do.** `ranch/platform-subscription.json` blueprints[]
  gains `to-do@0.7.0` — workshop dogfood now materializes the full to-do surface (the v0.116.0
  template Write no-op would have been caught at preflight dogfood). NEW
  `Docs/agent-guides/smoke-checklists/` directory: load-bearing `to-do.md` (with explicit
  bug-(b) and bug-(c) verification items) + 6 placeholder scaffolds for the other dogfooded
  blueprints. NEW `Docs/agent-guides/build-test-verify.md` § "Manual smoke" gate note.
- **#6 + #10 landmines.** Two new entries in `Docs/landmines.md`: #27 "Cycle scope discipline:
  smaller is faster" (v0.116.0 8-cycle thrash → cap at 3 distinct surfaces per cycle) +
  #28 "Verify dispatcher / loader contracts before designing against them" (extends the
  verify-helpers rule to customJS, Templater, installer subprocess). NEW
  `code-conventions.md` § "Dispatcher contracts" subsection catalogs the three runtime
  contracts.

**Versions:**

- workshop_version: `0.118.1` → **`0.119.0`** MINOR
- to-do blueprint: `0.6.1` → **`0.7.0`** MINOR
- project blueprint: `1.22.1` → **`1.22.2`** PATCH (defensive `dv.current()` guards on five render helpers; no behavior change in normal path)
- All other mechanisms + blueprints: UNCHANGED

**Late-cycle additions (bundled per user request 2026-06-16 17:13 UTC):**
- **Project render-guards.** `ProjectNavButtons.render`, `ProjectWorkstreamManager.render`, `ProjectWorkstreams.render`, `ProjectNotesCards.render`, `ProjectReferencedByCards.render` previously read `dv.current().file.path` (or `.name` / `.folder`) without guarding. After EntityCreate.create → openFile, Dataview hasn't indexed the new file yet so `dv.current()` returns undefined → TypeError. Surfaced live on accuris when creating a new project (spice/projects/dev-enablement/Dev-Enablement.md). Fix: optional-chain + early-return pattern at the top of each render() (mirrors the existing pattern in ProjectStatusWidget + the to-do helpers). NEW `platform/test/run-project-render-guards.js` harness exercises each widget against four dv-stub variants (undefined / null / file-less / no-current-method) — 20 PROJGUARD asserts total. Project blueprint bumped 1.22.1 → 1.22.2 PATCH.
- **C1 XSS fix.** Final code reviewer surfaced a leading-whitespace bypass in `_isSafeUrl` (both `ToDoDailyUnassignedMeetings` + `ToDoDailyProjectGroups`). Browsers strip leading whitespace from href attrs before resolution, so ` javascript:alert(1)` executes as `javascript:` — but the scheme regex `^[a-z]...:` doesn't match the leading whitespace, falling through the "relative URL — allow" path. Fix: trim before scheme detection. NEW TDM-7 + TDM-8 regression tests assert both widgets reject ` javascript:`, `\tjavascript:`, `JAVASCRIPT:` schemes, and that the render path emits escaped plain text (not an anchor) for malicious links.

**Harness count:** 32 → **35** (`run-customjs-contract.js` + `run-todo-markdown-render.js` + `run-project-render-guards.js`).
`lint-display-markers.js` is a preflight script, not a harness (matches existing convention for
`lint-schemas.js` + `check-version-sync.js`).

**Result doc:** `Docs/plans/2026-06-16-v0.119.0-todo-hotfix-thrash-defenses-result.md`.
Handoff prompt: `Docs/prompts/2026-06-16-post-v0.119.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

## v0.115.0 monthly cohesion + Months sub-area + FinanceHubSummary landing + Paycheck Template lockstep fix CODE COMPLETE 2026-06-15 (push/tag/brew/dev-sync pending)

**CODE COMPLETE 2026-06-15** (push to origin/main, tag, brew tap PR, and per-consumer dev-sync all pending user approval per `Docs/agent-guides/asking-before-acting.md` annotated-tag rule). MINOR finance cycle closing the monthly-cohesion arc that was originally designed as v0.112.0 (codename `monthly-cohesion`) but rebased to v0.115.0 after a concurrent session shipped workshop 0.112.0 (tooling sprint) + 0.113.0 (schema registry Stage A) + 0.114.0 (PaycheckDebtBand + MonthlyOverview Date filter) while this cycle was in flight. Plan content remained authoritative throughout the rebase — only version numbers swept. Commits: `d4ef749` (FinanceMath foundation + tests — commit message carries stale v0.112.0 label, code is the v0.115.0 foundation) → `dfa015d` (installer migrations S2 — same stale-label situation) → `bc6ee37` (Stage A: MonthsCards + MonthDashboard + FinanceHubSummary widgets + wire FinanceMath) → `6a35548` (Stage B: FinanceNav months modes + Months/Finance content + Paycheck Template lockstep fix) → `4f35c31` (Stage C: finance 0.9.0 + workshop 0.115.0 + Month new_entity + months rule_fragment + version sweep) → `b9bd56a` (Stage D: self-install dogfood + behavioral harness wired into release:preflight).

**Codename:** `monthly-cohesion`. Workshop 0.114.0 → **0.115.0**; finance 0.8.0 → **0.9.0**; entity-create 0.7.2 UNCHANGED; cowork 0.40.2 UNCHANGED; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. **Nine deliverables in one MINOR.** (1) **NEW `spice/finance/months/` sub-area** — on-demand `+ New Month` creates `Month-<YYYY-MM>.md` entities; `Months.md` hub lists them via NEW `MonthsCards` widget (sorted DESC by `month`, 24-card cap, +N earlier overflow; click → openLinkText). Each Month entity body: MonthDashboard + `## Notes` free-text area. (2) **NEW `FinanceMath` static-only helper class** — canonical home for shared aggregation math; methods: `readDebts`, `readPaychecksForMonth`, `readBudgetForMonth`, `monthBounds`, `debtTotals` (totalBalance / monthlyInterest / plannedAttack / weightedApr / zeroDebtDate), `monthIncome`, `monthSpending`, `monthExpensesTotal`, `monthDebtPaid`, `debtPaidByDebt`, `measuredMovement` (nearest-at-or-before boundary snapshots per debt — strictly better than MonthlyOverview's intra-month `_mom`), `reconcile` (paydown applied vs measured drop → derived interest/charges), `fmtMoney`. Existing `MonthlyOverview` + `DebtsHubSummary` NOT refactored this cycle (convergence is a carry-forward). (3) **NEW `MonthDashboard` widget** — three-section dashboard on every `Month-<YYYY-MM>.md`; Debt Changes: paydown applied (Σ paid debt-linked expenses) + measured drop (per-debt nearest-boundary balance_history snapshots; "snapshot pending" when fewer than 2 boundary snapshots) + interest/charges (derived gap); sharpens as balance_history[] densifies. (4) **NEW `MonthsCards` widget** — hub grid DESC by month, 24-card window. (5) **NEW `FinanceHubSummary` widget** — debt-forward landing on `Finance.md` (path-guarded); hero = TOTAL DEBT + ZERO-DEBT DATE + weighted APR / monthly attack / monthly interest; three secondary tiles (this-month budget progress bar + days-in-month sub-line / latest paycheck / open invoices via FinanceStatus.derive); tile click-throughs; no-debt fallback hero. (6) **FinanceNav extended** — `hub-months` + `entity-month` modes; Months cross-hub entry with `calendar` icon; entity-month prev/next sibling nav sorted `month` DESC. (7) **2 new installer migrations** wired into `applyFinanceMigrations` orchestrator: `applyFinanceMonthsScaffolding` (create-if-absent months sub-area + Months.md hub; mirrors `applyFinanceDebtScaffolding` posture) + `applyFinancePaycheckDebtBandInjection` (heals pre-v0.114.0 paychecks missing PaycheckDebtBand; idempotency marker `<!-- paycheck-debt-band-v0.8.0 -->`; anchor priority: marker → skip / PaycheckExpensesEditor block → before / PaycheckSummary block → after / frontmatter close → after); `applyFinanceHubsRepair` extended with months/Months.md hub entry + FinanceHubSummary injection for existing Finance.md. (8) **Manifest additions**: customjs_classes[] += FinanceMath, MonthsCards, MonthDashboard, FinanceHubSummary; files[] += 4 new helpers + content/Months.md; new_entity_buttons[] += month (icon calendar-plus, prompts month, flat destination, no seed_from_defaults, inline_body = SpaceNavButtons + FinanceNav + MonthDashboard + `## Notes\n\n`); rule_fragments[] += months fragment (path glob `spice/finance/months/Month-*.md`; type=month; month `^\d{4}-\d{2}$`; naming `^Month-\d{4}-\d{2}\.md$`); post_install[] += v0.9.0 notice. (9) **Lockstep fix** (incidental, fixes v0.114.0 gap): `templates/Paycheck Template.md` was missing the PaycheckDebtBand block that v0.114.0 added to the manifest paycheck inline_body AND still referenced legacy FinanceNavRow; both corrected; template body now matches manifest inline_body block-for-block.

**Test deltas.** helper-cases 3389 → **3538 / 0** (+149 sub-asserts; new HC families V01120 (FinanceMath + S2 migrations + idempotency) + V01150 (3 new widgets + FinanceMath registration + months content + Finance landing + Paycheck Template alignment + manifests bumped + month new_entity_button + months rule_fragment); 13 occurrences of 0.114.0 → 0.115.0 swept; 6 finance regex windows widened to include 0.9.x; V01080-NEB-count 4 → 5; several workshop range windows widened to include 115). Behavioral `run-v01120-monthly-cohesion.js` **52 / 0** (new harness covering FinanceMath + MonthsCards + MonthDashboard + FinanceHubSummary fixtures; wired into `release:preflight` in Stage D). `run-v01103-monthly-overview` 91 / 0 (unchanged, still green). Preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.115.0`. Dogfood (workshop self-install): `applyFinanceMonthsScaffolding` created `spice/finance/months/Months.md`; `applyFinancePaycheckDebtBandInjection` scanned 0 paychecks (workshop has no paychecks — expected); 80 new history entries; no warnings. **3 lessons:** (1) Build the shared-math foundation before widgets when fan-out is real — FinanceMath scoped first let Stage A widget implementer focus purely on DOM shape with zero arithmetic debt; brainstorm's "5 copies of zero-debt projection" risk turned into zero copies. (2) Concurrent-session collision recovery cost only a version rebaseline — plan content stayed authoritative; lockstep fix (Paycheck Template.md) bundled into Stage B at negligible cost. (3) `balance_history[]` boundary-snapshot semantics matter — FinanceMath.measuredMovement (nearest-at-or-before boundary) yields signal on ANY pre-month snapshot, vs MonthlyOverview's intra-month _mom which requires two snapshots within the same month; precision improves automatically as cadence densifies. **Carry-forwards:** MonthlyOverview + DebtsHubSummary → FinanceMath convergence (low-risk; v0.115.1 candidate); FinanceHubActions + FinanceNavRow deletion after soak (v0.116.0+); `gather-cc-debt-snapshot` cowork skill (feeds balance_history[] from Copilot Money MCP); subprocess-race investigation (sauce update --bump-pins cross-machine hazard). **Action required post-deploy:** brew tap PR bump 0.114.0 → 0.115.0 → merge → `brew upgrade sauce` → per consumer vault (headspace + accuris LOCAL): `scripts/dev-sync.sh` (or `sauce update --bump-pins && sauce install`) → Cmd+R Obsidian → smoke: Finance.md (hero + tiles), `+ New Month` (MonthDashboard + Notes), pre-v0.114.0 paycheck (PaycheckDebtBand visible between summary + expenses). See `Docs/plans/2026-06-15-v0.115.0-monthly-cohesion-result.md` + `Docs/prompts/2026-06-15-post-v0.115.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

## v0.109.0 projects visual overhaul CODE COMPLETE 2026-06-15 (brew + accuris pending)

**CODE COMPLETE 2026-06-15** (brew deploy + accuris install pending user approval per `Docs/agent-guides/asking-before-acting.md` annotated-tag rule). MAJOR project-blueprint UX cycle. ## v0.106.0.1 section hub UX cleanup CLOSED 2026-06-14

**SHIPPED 2026-06-14** (same day as v0.106.0). Same-day PATCH addressing three user-surfaced issues from post-v0.106.0 dogfood on the section-hub render. Commit: `87df13f`. Tap PR #165 merged. Brew @ 0.106.0.1.

**Codename:** `section-hub-ux-cleanup`. Workshop 0.106.0 → 0.106.0.1 PATCH; project blueprint 1.20.0 UNCHANGED; meetings 0.8.0 UNCHANGED; entity-create 0.6.0 UNCHANGED; platform-claude 0.1.3 UNCHANGED; cowork 0.40.0 UNCHANGED; scheduled-job-contract 0.35.1 UNCHANGED; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. **Three pure visual / nav-label fixes in one PATCH.** (1) **Sub-sections / docs visual separator** — when a section hub renders BOTH a sub-sections row AND a docs row, the two appeared adjacent without a clear visual break (v0.106.0 Issue 7 had removed the standalone `Docs` header). Fix: re-emit `dv.header(3, 'Docs')` ONLY when the section ALSO has sub-sections; pure-docs sections stay header-less per Issue 7 intent. (3) **Empty-state callout removal** — `No docs in <section> yet — use + New Doc above` rendered as user-visible noise on every freshly-created section + sub-section; fix returns early on `docs.length === 0` with no rendered output. Pure additive removal — `+ New Doc` button still renders via the section-hub's separate button row.

**Pure additive / pure-removal — no migration required.** No schema changes, no installer step delta, no contract bump. helper-cases retained at **2782 / 0** (v0.106.0 + S2/S3 widget exercises updated 3 existing sub-asserts in-place but added no new ones); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 217 / 0; bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.106.0.1`. Post-deploy user smoke confirmed all three fixes: separator visible only on mixed-content sections; nav labels updated everywhere; empty sections render cleanly without noise. See `Docs/prompts/2026-06-15-post-v0.106.0.1-next-cycle-handoff.md` for the v0.107.0 next-cycle handoff that surfaced from this PATCH's user-feedback cycle (projects-visual-overhaul brainstorm seed).

## v0.106.0 install posture + filter persistence + debounce + dashboard widgets CLOSED 2026-06-14

**SHIPPED 2026-06-14.** Four-item bundled MINOR closing three multi-cycle carry-forwards (helper-script `cp` workaround pattern; persistent filter state; dashboard widget expansion) plus a small in-cycle helper. Commits: `42e30af` (S1 helper-script content-overwrite posture) → `79de445` (S2 DocSearch persistent filter state + 150ms debounce) → `4543130` (S3 ProjectDocsIndex dashboard widgets expansion — task count + 7-day activity + top tags) → `d8705cb` (S4 workshop 0.106.0 + project 1.20.0 bumps + VERSION-pin sweep) → `8c034e3` (S5 dogfood self-install materialized artifacts). Tap PR #164 merged.

**Codename:** `install-posture-and-widgets`. Workshop 0.105.0.3 → 0.106.0; project blueprint 1.19.0 → 1.20.0; meetings 0.8.0 UNCHANGED; entity-create 0.6.0 UNCHANGED; platform-claude 0.1.3 UNCHANGED; cowork 0.40.0 UNCHANGED; scheduled-job-contract 0.35.1 UNCHANGED; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. **Four moves in one MINOR.** (S1) **Helper-script content-overwrite posture.** For `ranch/scripts/.+\.js$` dests (CustomJS class bodies), the installer now bypasses the default Option B `.bak`-backup overwrite path: when the substituted source differs from the current dest, write cleanly + emit `content_overwrite_helper_script` history event. Closes a three-cycle pattern where the user had to hand-`cp` materialized helpers after each `sauce install` because the `.bak` ritual was treating them as user-authored content. (S2) **DocSearch persistent filter state + 150ms debounce.** Filter state (text + selected tags) persists to localStorage keyed by `scopePath`, so `Docs.md` (cross-section) and each Section Hub (within-section) remember their own filter across reloads. Restoration runs AFTER all listeners are wired so chip-styling restore + initial filter render flow cleanly. 150ms input debounce stops keystroke-rate re-renders on >500-doc projects (v0.104.0 lesson 3 — "defer the optimization until a real-world scope hits regression" — fired here). (S3) **ProjectDocsIndex dashboard widgets expansion.** `_renderChips()` extends with three new chips sharing the existing `chipStyle`: (a) task count — `dv.pages spice/projects/<slug>/tasks/` filtered by `type=task-note`; chip omitted silently when zero so projects without task-notes don't show a misleading "0 tasks"; (b) 7-day activity window — count of doc-notes touched in the last 7 days; (c) top-3 tag chips — frequency-ranked tag pool across the project's doc-notes. (S4) **Version-pin sweep.** Workshop manifest + project manifest + subscription pins lockstep-bumped per established cycle-close ritual.

**Pure additive — no migration required.** No schema changes, no frontmatter rewrites, no installer step delta beyond the posture extension, no contract bump. helper-cases 2713 → **2782 / 0** (+69 sub-asserts; new HC families for the installer-posture overwrite + DocSearch persistence/debounce + dashboard-widgets chip rendering); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 217 / 0 (UNCHANGED — no new `claude_surface[]` entries); bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.106.0`. **Install-posture carry-forward.** The S1 content-overwrite posture worked correctly during the v0.106.0.1 same-day deploy but a race between brew-tarball propagation and `sauce update --bump-pins` invocation meant the install briefly read STALE source (overwrite events logged the old content's SHA). Hand `cp` fix-up resolved it. v0.107.x earmark: either add a `sleep` after `brew upgrade` in deploy chains OR force `require.cache` invalidation in the workshop dispatcher. **Action required post-deploy.** `brew upgrade sauce` → per consumer vault: `sauce update --bump-pins` → `sauce install`. Idempotent. No cloud-sync needed. See `Docs/prompts/2026-06-15-post-v0.106.0.1-next-cycle-handoff.md` for next-cycle handoff (projects-visual-overhaul scope).

## v0.104.0 search & filter CLOSED 2026-06-13

**SHIPPED 2026-06-13.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.104.0-search-and-filter-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits: `cefe2ec` (S0 design) → `6331a85` (S0 plan) → `93ff990` (S1 DocSearch helper — HC-V01040-DS-1..6 GREEN) → `8a46197` (S2.1 ProjectDocsIndex consumes DocSearch — HC-V01040-PDI-EXT-1..3 GREEN; whole-suite 2708/0) → `b0f7fa5` (S2.2 SectionHub consumes DocSearch — HC-V01040-SH-EXT-1..2 GREEN; whole-suite 2710/0) → `6d3093b` (S3 project blueprint 1.18.0 — DocSearch registered; HC-V01040-MAN-1 GREEN; FA3-PROJ-1 enum widened; whole-suite 2713/0) → `f217657` (S4 workshop 0.104.0 + project 1.18.0 bumps + VERSION-pin sweep) → `a8e8f59` (S5 dogfood self-install materialized artifacts) → S6 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `search-and-filter`. Workshop 0.103.0.1 → 0.104.0; project blueprint 1.17.0 → 1.18.0; meetings 0.8.0 UNCHANGED; entity-create 0.5.0 UNCHANGED; platform-claude 0.1.3 UNCHANGED; cowork 0.40.0 UNCHANGED; scheduled-job-contract 0.35.1 UNCHANGED; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. **This cycle closes the original v0.102.0 three-item ask** — sections + meetings link (both shipped v0.102.0) + search (deferred to v0.102.1, re-evaluated against v0.103.0's section-hub reframe, shipped HERE). The deferred-item shape adapted cleanly to the v0.103.0 reframe because the underlying user-need (in-page filter + tag chips + scoped-search escape hatch) was unchanged and the section-hub model gave us natural query scope per hub.

**ONE new helper, two integration points, pure additive.** **DocSearch** — CustomJS class. `render(dv, opts)` builds the filter UI strip (text input + dynamic top-8 tag chips + scoped-Obsidian-search button + status pill) + returns the initial `filterContext` `{text, tags, hasActiveFilter}`. Static `DocSearch.matches(page, ctx)` is the pure predicate consumed by `ProjectDocsIndex` + `SectionHub` at query time — AND-logic across text substring (`file.name` + `tags` + first 200 chars of `file.content`) + every selected tag chip. `_countTags(pages)` scans scope for chip pool (excludes `doc-note` tag itself; top-8 cap via `.slice(0, 8)`). Scoped-search button invokes Obsidian's `global-search:open` command pre-filled with `path:"<scopePath>"` — the escape hatch for full-body fuzzy search. **ProjectDocsIndex** (extended) — mounts `DocSearch.render` at `recursive: true` cross-section scope above the section card row; the `allDocs` query gates on `DocSearch.matches` so the dashboard doc-count chip + per-section meta counts both reflect the live filter. **SectionHub** (extended) — mounts `DocSearch.render` at depth-appropriate scope (depth 1 = `recursive: true` so the count covers sub-section docs; depth 2 = `recursive: false` leaf); docs query + depth-1 sub-section card meta count gate on `DocSearch.matches`. `onChange` triggers a full re-render via `dv.container.empty() + this.render(dv)` with `_currentCtx` carry-over so the active filter survives keystrokes.

**Pure additive — no migration required.** No schema changes, no frontmatter rewrites, no installer step, no contract bump. Entire surface delta is helper materialization (one new file + two refreshed files) via the existing `installItem` files[] loop. Empty filter = zero behavior change from v0.103.0.1.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2713 / 0** (+12 sub-asserts from v0.103.0.1's 2701); cowork-smoke 954 / 0 (UNCHANGED); claude-surface 217 / 0 (UNCHANGED — no new claude_surface[] entries); bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; wiki-to-docs-migration 30 / 0; preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.104.0`. New HC families: DS-1..6 / PDI-EXT-1..3 / SH-EXT-1..2 / MAN-1. **FA3-PROJ-1 enum widened** to `/^1\.(1[3-9]|[2-9]\d)\.\d+$/` so future MINOR bumps of project blueprint don't whack-a-mole the same assertion (closes v0.100.0 carry-forward).

**3 lessons.**

1. **Closing a multi-item ask across cycles is structurally sound when the items decouple cleanly.** Sections + meetings link shipped together at v0.102.0; search was deferred (v0.102.1 slot) because it could be layered on without blocking the structural moves. v0.103.0 reframed the section model (single-page → hierarchical hubs); when search came up for scheduling at v0.104.0, the original brainstorm adapted cleanly because user-need was unchanged and the surface map was composable. Re-read deferred items against the new model at scheduling time — don't auto-kill, don't auto-ship. Third confirmation in three weeks (v0.95.1 anti-echo across v0.95.0 reframe; v0.102.1 search across v0.103.0 reframe; THIS cycle).
2. **The section-hub model gave us natural query scope per hub — no new schema field needed.** Each hub note has a unique folder path (`spice/projects/<slug>/docs/` for Docs.md; `.../docs/<section>/` for depth-1; `.../docs/<section>/<sub>/` for depth-2). DocSearch's `scopePath` + `recursive` args are computed from the host hub's filesystem location at render time. Structural-hierarchy cycles often pay forward unexpectedly — surface the latent affordances they create in the design's Carry-forward section so future cycles can find them.
3. **Re-render-on-keystroke works fine at <500 docs.** Considered debouncing + DOM diffing at design time; picked full re-render because the v0.104.0 design considered three strategies and per-keystroke full re-render won on simplicity. Verified at S2.1/S2.2 by reading the existing render bodies — O(docs) with no per-doc async — sub-millisecond at 30-50 docs. Defer the optimization until a real-world scope hits the regression.

**Action required post-deploy.** `brew upgrade sauce` → per consumer vault (accuris + headspace, BOTH LOCAL): `sauce update --bump-pins` → `sauce install`. Materializes `ranch/scripts/project/doc-search.js` + refreshes `project-docs-index.js` + `section-hub.js`. In Obsidian: `customJS:reload` (Cmd+P) OR full restart so the DocSearch class registers in `window.customJS`. Filter UI appears at the top of every Docs.md + every Section Hub on next render. Idempotent. No cloud-sync needed (cowork untouched; contract 0.35.1 holds).

**Carry-forward.** v0.104.x PATCH candidates: persistent filter state across renders (per-hub `ranch/cache/filter-state.json`; demand-gated); debounce keystroke re-render (only if a >500-doc project surfaces — lesson 3); dashboard widget expansion on `ProjectDocsIndex` (richer panels beyond current chip stubs; carried from v0.103.0); `_migrateDocNote` CRLF widening (carried from v0.103.0 lesson 2); section warning chip on doc rows where folder/`section:` disagree (carried from v0.102.0); `rule_fragment` `section` field strict-wikilink form once both consumer vaults migrate. **v0.105.0 cowork:doctor** NEXT (displaced FOUR cycles by v0.101.0 + v0.102.0 + v0.103.0 + v0.104.0; shape stays locked from 2026-06-11 brainstorm; KPI is 5× richer with per-cadence satisfaction signal + 5+ days of accrual since v0.101.0 deploy). v0.102.0 carry-forwards still open: per-section `+ New` button validation; `_resolveOptionsSource` silent exception swallow; ProjectMeetingsPanel view-all pagination shape.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.104.0-search-and-filter-{design,plan,result}.md` + `Docs/prompts/2026-06-13-post-v0.104.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

## v0.103.0 section hubs CLOSED 2026-06-13

**SHIPPED 2026-06-13.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.103.0-section-hubs-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits: `2be65e6` (S0 design) → `f75dde9` (S0 plan) → `d3816d4` (S1 Breadcrumb helper) → `f36dc99` (S2.1 ProjectDocsIndex helper) → `dc256f6` (S2.2 SectionHub helper) → `22b9369` (S3 project blueprint 1.17.0 — section-hub schema + templates + Docs Hub rewrite + Doc Note breadcrumb; 4 supersession test rewrites) → `213188e` (S3.2 ProjectNavButtons section-hub depth 1 + 2 contexts) → `4a6a74e` (S4 applyProjectSectionsHubMigration) → `1d6159d` (S5 workshop 0.103.0 + project 1.17.0 bumps + VERSION-pin sweep) → `36824de` (S6 dogfood self-install materialized artifacts) → S7 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `section-hubs`. Workshop 0.102.0 → 0.103.0; project blueprint 1.16.0 → 1.17.0; meetings blueprint UNCHANGED at 0.8.0; entity-create mechanism UNCHANGED at 0.5.0; platform-claude mechanism UNCHANGED at 0.1.3; cowork blueprint UNCHANGED at 0.40.0; scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17.

**Origin.** User observation on accuris one day after v0.102.0 deploy: "how am I to know what is in which section within Docs?" The v0.102.0 Confluence-style Docs Hub renders every section as a row-list bucket on one note — buckets scaled, hierarchy did not. With 31 docs across 5+ sections on `global-k8s`, the section identity got lost in the bucket density. Reframe: each section becomes a first-class entity with its own hub note. `Docs.md` becomes a sections INDEX (lists sections + dashboard widgets — does NOT list docs). Each section folder grows a `<Section Name>.md` hub that lists THIS section's docs + sub-sections + has its own `+ New Doc` and `+ New Sub-Section` buttons. File system mirrors the hierarchy, capped at 2 nesting levels (project → section → sub-section → docs). Breadcrumb on every node — clickable wikilink trail rendered at the top of `Docs.md`, every Section Hub, every Sub-Section Hub, and every doc-note. Displaced cowork:doctor by ONE more cycle (now v0.104.0; the brainstorm shape stays locked from 2026-06-11); v0.102.1 search fast-follow re-evaluated against the new model (the underlying need is unchanged but the surface map shifted).

**Three new helpers + two new templates + one new installer step.** (1) **Breadcrumb** — CustomJS class reads `dv.current()`'s `project` / `section` / `sub_section` frontmatter; emits clickable wikilink trail; branches on `type` (project / docs-hub / section-hub / doc-note). (2) **ProjectDocsIndex** — replaces `ProjectDocsSections` on `Docs.md`; renders section cards (BeaconCards `layout: "row"`) + dashboard chips (doc count / open meetings / project status) + `+ New Section` / `+ New Doc` shortcut buttons via `EntityCreate.render({presetPrompts})`. Defaults to `[[Knowledge]]` / `[[Notes]]` when `sections[]` absent. (3) **SectionHub** — depth-aware render (depth 1 vs depth 2); shows breadcrumb + doc cards + `+ New Doc` (presetPrompts: section + optional sub_section); depth-1 hubs additionally show sub-section cards + `+ New Sub-Section` button. Depth-2 hubs suppress sub-section UI per the 2-level cap. **Section Hub.md** — NEW canonical template for both depth levels. **Doc Note.md** — extended with `<!-- breadcrumb-v1.17.0 -->` marker + Breadcrumb dataviewjs block prepended above SpaceNavButtons. **Docs Hub.md** — REWRITTEN to invoke Breadcrumb + ProjectDocsIndex (the standalone `entity-create:doc-note` block is gone; `+ New Doc` moves into the index helper).

**`ProjectNavButtons` section-hub contexts.** `detectContext` dispatches `type: section-hub` notes via frontmatter (depth 1 or 2) instead of falling through to unknown. Two new render branches: depth 1 = `Project · Docs · Sibling Sections`; depth 2 = `Project · Docs · Section · Sibling Sub-Sections`. Sibling lists target the parent index (Docs.md for depth 1, the depth-1 Section Hub for depth 2) — those hubs ARE the sibling indexes per the hierarchical-tree design. Parent-section URL for depth 2 is composed from `ctx.parentSlug` (path-derived) + the `parent_section` frontmatter label (stripped via NEW `_stripLinkBrackets` helper).

**Migration.** NEW `applyProjectSectionsHubMigration` (runs after `applyProjectSectionsMigration` in the project pipeline) heals each project under `spice/projects/`. Six sub-operations per project: (1) rewrite `docs/Docs.md` body to invoke `ProjectDocsIndex` (from `ProjectDocsCards`/`ProjectDocsSections`) and strip the standalone `entity-create:doc-note` block; (2) materialize Section Hub notes for every existing `docs/<slug>/` subfolder; (3) recurse ONE level deep — sub-folders containing ≥1 doc-note get a depth-2 Sub-Section Hub; (4) migrate doc-note `section: "Knowledge"` (string) → `section: "[[Knowledge]]"` (wikilink); add `sub_section: "[[X]]"` when applicable; inject a breadcrumb dataviewjs block at the top of every doc-note body (guarded by `<!-- breadcrumb-v1.17.0 -->` marker for idempotency); (5) migrate the project's `sections[]` frontmatter from strings to wikilinks; (6) default-section guarantee — every project always has Knowledge + Notes hubs after migration. Idempotent per-project (skip if Docs.md already invokes `ProjectDocsIndex`). Failure-loud per-project: try/catch around each project body, emits warning event with `step: "project_sections_hub_migration"`; never throws.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2694 / 0** (+121 sub-asserts from v0.102.0's 2573); cowork-smoke 954 / 0 (UNCHANGED); claude-surface 217 / 0 (UNCHANGED — no new claude_surface[] entries); bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; wiki-to-docs-migration 30 / 0; preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.103.0`. New HC families: BC-1..4 / PDI-1..6 / SH-1..7 / PROJ-MAN-1..4 / PROJ-TPL-1..2 / PNB-CTX-1 / PSHM-1..5.

**3 lessons.**

1. **Reframe-driven cycles need a "what observation broke the previous model?" headline.** v0.102.0 shipped two days ago; ONE day of accuris dogfood inverted the call. The brainstorm framing was a single sentence ("how am I to know what is in which section?") and that sentence drove the whole reframe. Codify the observation verbatim in design TL;DR + result doc + cycle-history entry so future readers know WHY the model changed and don't re-litigate the old shape under amnesia.
2. **`_migrateDocNote` breadcrumb injection's frontmatter-end probe is LF-only.** Uses `out.indexOf("---\n", 4)` to find the frontmatter close. On CRLF files (Windows-edited content synced through Obsidian Sync), the close is `---\r\n` — the LF probe returns `-1` and the breadcrumb block is silently skipped. Both consumer vaults are macOS so the latent bug doesn't fire today; carry-forward: widen the probe to `/---\r?\n/` before any cross-platform deploy.
3. **EntityCreate `folder_prefix` double-slash safety is guaranteed by `_ensureFolder`'s `split("/").filter(Boolean)`.** doc-note `folder_prefix` composes `docs/{{prompts.section_slug}}/{{prompts.sub_section_slug}}/`. When sub_section is empty, the literal output is `docs/knowledge//`. Neither conditional templating nor post-processing was needed: `_ensureFolder(folder).split("/").filter(Boolean)` removes empty parts at the helper layer. Verified by reading entity-create.js lines 619-630 BEFORE shipping the manifest extension. v0.94.0's "verify helpers before design asserts them" rule extends to "verify helpers before manifest extensions assume them."

**Action required post-deploy.** `brew upgrade sauce` → per consumer vault (accuris + headspace, BOTH LOCAL): `sauce update --bump-pins` → `sauce install`. The install runs `applyProjectSectionsHubMigration` per project (heal Docs.md + materialize section hubs + migrate doc-note frontmatter + inject breadcrumbs + migrate project sections[] to wikilinks + default-section guarantee). Idempotent. No cloud-sync needed.

**Carry-forward.** v0.103.1 PATCH candidates: dashboard widget expansion on `ProjectDocsIndex` (richer panels beyond the current chip stubs); `_migrateDocNote` CRLF widening; section warning chip on doc rows where folder/`section:` frontmatter disagree; `rule_fragment` `section` field strict-wikilink form once consumer vaults migrate. v0.102.1 search fast-follow re-evaluated against the new model (filter on `Docs.md` across sections vs on each Section Hub within a section?). v0.104.0 cowork:doctor NEXT (displaced THREE cycles by v0.101.0 + v0.102.0 + v0.103.0; shape stays locked). v0.102.0 carry-forwards still open: per-section `+ New` button validation; `_resolveOptionsSource` silent exception swallow; ProjectMeetingsPanel view-all pagination shape.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.103.0-section-hubs-{design,plan,result}.md` + `Docs/prompts/2026-06-13-post-v0.103.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

### v0.102.0 — project workspace CLOSED 2026-06-13

**SHIPPED 2026-06-13.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.102.0-project-workspace-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits: `333a8fc` (S0 design) → `db56b09` (S0 plan) → `f4b04fb` (S1 entity-create 0.5.0 — presetPrompts + options_source: all_projects) → `2f85df0` (S2.1 ProjectDocsSections helper) → `79e1c8a` (S2.2 ProjectMeetingsPanel helper) → `b75443d` (S2.2.1 panel polish — double-click guard + alias-link match + no emoji) → `7dd173d` (S3.1 project blueprint 1.16.0 — sections schema + meetings panel) → `1ea5598` (S3.1.1 v1.16.0 polish — manifest desc + helper alias + prompt validation) → `da68448` (S3.2 meetings blueprint 0.8.0 — project link + hub pill) → `c4b44fb` (S4.1 applyProjectSectionsMigration) → `701e041` (S4.2 applyVaultDefaultPaths + spice/resources/ directory-map seed) → `fee044a` (S5 workshop 0.102.0 + per-bp bumps + VERSION-pin sweep) → `ed48e93` (S6 workshop manifest catalogue sweep — subscription-pin mismatch fix from self-install) → `5cb119c` (S6 dogfood self-install materialized artifacts) → S7 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `project-workspace`. Workshop 0.101.1 → 0.102.0; project blueprint 1.15.2 → 1.16.0; meetings blueprint 0.7.0 → 0.8.0; entity-create mechanism 0.4.0 → 0.5.0; platform-claude mechanism 0.1.2 → 0.1.3; cowork blueprint UNCHANGED at 0.40.0; scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1; mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17.

**Origin.** User direction 2026-06-13 (remote session, post-v0.101.1 deploy): "I want search inside docs, hierarchy/sections, and an easy way to connect projects to meetings." Anchored against accuris-sauce `spice/projects/global-k8s/` with 31 docs of varying kinds — the flat row layout v0.100.0 shipped had stopped scaling. Displaced the cowork:doctor cycle (was earmarked v0.102) by ONE cycle.

**Three platform moves.** (1) **Sections** — doc-notes get foldered under `docs/<section-slug>/`; two platform defaults ship (`Knowledge`, `Notes`); per-project override via `sections: []` in project frontmatter; `section:` doc-note frontmatter mirrors the folder; folder wins on conflict. Tags do orthogonal categorization. (2) **Confluence-style Docs Hub** — NEW `ProjectDocsSections` helper renders one labeled bucket per section, each as a BeaconCards row list (v0.100.0 row aesthetic preserved); one `+ New <Section>` button per bucket via the NEW EntityCreate `presetPrompts` capability — no section picker on click; trailing `Unfiled` bucket catches orphans. (3) **Project ↔ Meetings link** — singular `project: "[[ProjectName]]"` field on meeting frontmatter (optional); project note grows `## Meetings` panel above the BacklinkPanel (top 5 + view-all expander + `+ New meeting for this project` button via `presetPrompts`); NEW `ProjectMeetingsPanel` helper queries meetings where `project:` matches the current project; meeting hub rows gain a project pill when set; `meetings-hub-cards.js` subtitle callback renders the pill before the attendees chip row.

**EntityCreate 0.5.0 — capability MINOR.** `render({presetPrompts})` accepts preset key/value pairs; `create()` skips matching prompt UI and uses the preset value (validation still runs against preset values). NEW `_resolveOptionsSource("all_projects")` returns `(none) + dv.pages().where(p => p.type === "project").file.name` for select prompts. Schema gains `presetPrompts` (optional object) at button level + `options_source` (optional enum) at prompt level. Consumed by ProjectDocsSections (`presetPrompts: {section: <name>}`) and ProjectMeetingsPanel (`presetPrompts: {project: <current project basename>}`).

**Adjacent install fix.** NEW `applyVaultDefaultPaths` (vault-init prelude) creates `spice/resources/{notes,attachments}/` if absent and writes Obsidian's "Files & Links" defaults (`newFileFolderPath` + `attachmentFolderPath` in `.obsidian/app.json`) when unset. Existing user customizations preserved. Stops new notes + pasted attachments from scattering at vault root. `platform-claude` 0.1.3 PATCH adds `spice/resources/` to `DIRECTORY_MAP_SEEDS` so the namespace surfaces in CLAUDE.md's directory-map.

**Migration.** NEW `applyProjectSectionsMigration` (Strategy A auto-migration; runs after `applyDocsHubButtonRepair`): every flat `docs/<doc>.md` (except `Docs.md`) moves to `docs/knowledge/<doc>.md` + `section: "Knowledge"` frontmatter added; any pre-existing doc-note-containing subfolder (`docs/specs/`, `docs/runbooks/`, etc.) registers as a custom section in the parent project's `sections[]` (filter: at least one `*.md` inside has `type: doc-note`). Transactional per project; idempotent.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2573 / 0** (+116 sub-asserts from v0.101.1's 2457); cowork-smoke 954 / 0 (UNCHANGED); claude-surface 217 / 0 (+1 — `spice/resources/` row); bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; wiki-to-docs-migration 30 / 0; preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.102.0`. New HC families: EC-1..3 / PDS-1..9 / PMP-1..7 / PROJ-MAN-1..4 / PROJ-TPL-1..3 / MEET-1..2 / PSM-1..4 / VDP-1..3 / CLAUDE-MD-RES-1. Four distinct lockstep version-pin events across 5 commits (DOC-2, R-WIKI-1, DOCS-INT-2, EC-2, FA2-MEETINGS-1, HC-V0880-MEETINGS-D, DHB-1, several VERSION-* cases); established pattern, not scope creep.

**5 lessons.**

1. **Workshop catalogue drifts from per-mechanism manifests — self-install catches it; preflight doesn't.** `platform/manifest.json`'s `mechanisms[]` + `blueprints[]` catalogue is INDEPENDENT of the per-mechanism/per-blueprint manifests. S5 bumped per-bp manifests + subscription pins but missed the catalogue rows; S6 self-install bombed with "subscription pins X@A.B.C but workshop has D.E.F" skip notices, rejecting the whole install. Fix landed at `ed48e93`. Add catalogue cross-check to the S5 version-bump checklist. **Codified as landmine #24.**
2. **`slugify` is NOT a pipe; it's a derive primitive only.** Design Section 3 named `docs/{{prompts.section|slugify}}/` (Jinja-style). EntityCreate's substitution treats `slugify` as a derive-key transform, not a `|`-piped filter. Caught at plan-time pre-empt; verify-before-design on the substitution surface paid off (v0.94.0 lesson reinforcement).
3. **`{{this.project_slug}}` is NOT in the EntityCreate substitution catalogue.** ProjectMeetingsPanel needs the current project's basename for the `project` preset. Substitution catalogue is `{{current_file.frontmatter.*}}` + `{{prompts.*}}` only; no `this` namespace. Verified by reading `entity-create.js` before authoring the helper. Plan-time pre-empt is critical.
4. **Consumer regression is POST-release per `build-test-verify.md`.** `ranch/platform-config.json:workshop_relative_path` in each consumer points at brew sauce, NOT the dev repo, so pre-release consumer dogfood cannot reach dev code. Consumer regression runs AFTER `brew upgrade sauce`. Plan should not list it as a pre-tag gate.
5. **Lockstep version-pin updates in tests are an established pattern.** 4 distinct lockstep events across 5 commits (S3.1 project, S3.2 meetings, S5 workshop, S6 catalogue). The per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep IS the cycle close — it's part of the contract, not in-flight tax.

**Action required post-deploy.** `brew upgrade sauce` → per consumer vault (accuris + headspace, BOTH LOCAL): `sauce update --bump-pins` → `sauce install`. The install runs `applyProjectSectionsMigration` (filesystem migration of `docs/<doc>.md` → `docs/knowledge/<doc>.md` + `section: "Knowledge"` frontmatter; pre-existing doc-note subfolders register as custom sections) + `applyVaultDefaultPaths` (creates `spice/resources/{notes,attachments}/`; writes Obsidian "Files & Links" defaults when unset). Idempotent. No cloud-sync needed (cowork untouched; contract 0.35.1 holds).

**Carry-forward.** v0.102.1 search fast-follow NEXT (design Section 13: in-page filter + dynamic tag chips + scoped-search button on `ProjectDocsSections` — pure UX layer, no schema change). v0.103.0 cowork:doctor (displaced ONE cycle by v0.102.0; shape stays locked from 2026-06-11 brainstorm). Plus: lint helper render-time vs install-step (design Section 11 open question); section-frontmatter-mismatch chip; per-section ` + New` button validation; `_resolveOptionsSource` silent-exception cleanup; asymmetric fallback shapes in `ProjectDocsSections` Unfiled bucket; ProjectMeetingsPanel view-all pagination shape — earmark for v0.102.x cleanup.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-13-v0.102.0-project-workspace-{design,plan,result}.md` + `Docs/prompts/2026-06-13-post-v0.102.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

### v0.101.1 — docs-hub "+ New Doc" button repair (2026-06-13 close)

**SHIPPED 2026-06-13.** User-reported on accuris: a freshly-created project's Docs hub (`spice/projects/mcp-mesh/docs/Docs.md`) showed the "No docs yet · Click + New Doc above" empty-state but NO "+ New Doc" button. Root cause: the `Template, Docs Hub.md` entity-create:doc-note block used a broken dispatch — `await dv.view("ranch/views/customjs-guard", { class: "AccentButton", args: [{ id: "doc-note", ... }] })`. The guard calls `AccentButton.render(dv, opts)` passing `dv` (not `dv.container`) as the parent, so `parent.createEl` throws and the button never renders (no `onClick` wired either). All 6 other blueprints' entity-create blocks use the canonical `await customJS.EntityCreate.render(dv, { instance })` form; only the project Docs hub diverged (since before v0.55.0). Blast radius: 13/20 project Docs hubs in accuris, 6/7 in headspace were broken — they just never got clicked while empty until v0.100.0 made the row list the primary surface.

**Two-part fix.** (1) Template canonicalized to `customJS.EntityCreate.render(dv, { instance: "doc-note" })` — heals all NEW projects. (2) NEW installer step `applyDocsHubButtonRepair` + pure `_repairDocsHubButtonBody` (project-gated, idempotent, failure-loud) rewrites the broken block → canonical in any EXISTING `docs/Docs.md` on `sauce update` — `applyDocsBackfill` is create-if-absent and never repaired them. Wired after `applyDocsBackfill` in the project pipeline; exported for the migration harness.

**Tests:** NEW DHB-1 (template canonical-form assert, run-helper-cases) + DHBR-1..3 (repair behavioral cases, run-wiki-to-docs-migration). `R-WIKI-1` (run-renderer) REWRITTEN — it had asserted the template MUST contain the broken `AccentButton` + `id: "doc-note"` form, i.e. it locked in the bug. project blueprint 1.15.1 → 1.15.2; workshop 0.101.0 → 0.101.1; cowork 0.40.0 + contract 0.35.1 UNCHANGED; mechanism count 17. helper-cases 2455 → **2457 / 0** (+2 DHB); wiki-to-docs 24 → **30** (+6 DHBR); preflight exit 0; `version-sync ok: 0.101.1`.

**Lesson:** a static-string test that asserts a literal must be paired with a runtime check of the same surface — `R-WIKI-1` pinned the broken form as a REQUIREMENT since v0.50.0, so the bug "passed" for ~50 cycles. Carry-forward: a generic entity-create-block linter verifying EVERY blueprint's hub block uses the canonical dispatch (catches this class + prevents recurrence).

Workshop 0.100.1 → 0.101.0; cowork 0.39.0 → 0.40.0; eod-review sidecar schema enum gains `1.4.0` (additive; `feedback_capture.sentinel_version` enum gains `"v=4"`); the 4 non-EOD sidecar schemas gain enum `["1.0.0","1.1.0"]` + an OPTIONAL slim `feedback_capture {sentinel_version: ["v=4"], kinds_listed[]}` (additive); feedback-capture sentinel v=3 → v=4 on ALL FIVE cadences (tolerant parsers accept v=1..v=4); learned_weights schema 4 → 5; scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1 (ZERO new substitution tokens; landmine #20 doesn't fire); mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17. Displaces `cowork:doctor` by ONE cycle (shape stays locked; its KPI is 5× richer with five cadences feeding it).

**Origin.** The user's 2026-06-12 PM framing: "I want for you to add a mechanism where I get to provide feedback, verbal feedback on what I liked and didn't like, and then that also gets utilized within this process to get better and better every day. Should be something that is applied at the bottom of every scheduled job. [...] thinking big here." v0.99.0 made the EOD feedback section prose-first; the other FOUR cadences still emitted the v0.96.0 kind-checkbox-only rail — the user opened a morning brief and saw only checkboxes.

**Capture v=4 — one shape on all five briefs.** Cadence-neutral title `> [!todo]+ Was this useful?`; one-tap `Useful: [ ] yes [ ] no` + the free-text typing box render on top of EVERY brief; EOD keeps its exact v=3 per-item Mattered/Didn't-like lists + frequency knobs below; the 4 non-EOD cadences render ONE collapsed `> > [!summary]- Kinds — quick ticks` checklist (the old checkbox content demoted to garnish; computed from the same surfaced-kind source as `{{$rating_kind_lines}}` — zero new tokens). `composeFeedbackCapture` becomes cadence-aware (`itemMode = cadence === "eod-review"`); `_parsePrior` tolerates v=1..v=4; UPGRADE-DAY transition: a same-day prior carrying only the LEGACY rating-block marker surrenders its kind ticks into the new checklist via `parseRatingCallout` — nothing lost on upgrade day. Legacy `rating-block` EMISSION retired; the parser lives forever for the historical corpus. Slim `kinds_listed` sidecar observability on non-EOD notes.

**Either-marker write-guard.** NEW `FEEDBACK_SENTINEL = "<!-- cowork:feedback-capture v="` alongside `RATING_SENTINEL` in `write-atomic-note-helper.js`; Guard 2 passes when the body carries EITHER marker; gate condition + `missing-rating-callout` failure token unchanged; rating-marker acceptance is permanent legacy tolerance, not a deprecation window. All five orchestrators now pass `surfaced_kinds_for_rating` uniformly — resolving STRUCTURALLY the v0.99.0-era discrepancy where eod-review.md Step 8.5 said to pass the array while EOD bodies carried no rating-block marker (design Ground truth 3, confirmed at S1.0).

**Per-cadence satisfaction (schema 5).** Satisfaction entries become `{day, cadence, useful}` — up to five taps a day coexist; the doctor can grade each cadence separately ("morning trends yes, midday trends no"). `appendSatisfaction(totals, day, useful, opts)` gains `opts.cadence`; same-(day, cadence) re-log overwrites; window trims by DAY (30) with a 200-entry belt-and-suspenders cap. `normalizeLearnedWeightsV5` extends the tolerance chain (on-disk 2 / missing / `"1.1.0"` / 3 / 4 / 5; string-or-number versions `Number()`-coerced per v0.99.0 lesson 2; deep-copied returns; same block-scoped `.bak`-first write contract). The 4 → 5 step is PURELY ADDITIVE — NO skip-zeroing, NO warmup reset, NO counter changes (unlike the 3 → 4 silence-reset); schema-4-era `{day, useful}` entries normalize to `cadence: "eod-review"`. Graduation rules untouched.

**Prose ingest generalized.** The 03:00 reconciler extracts intents from up to five typing boxes per day, each tagged `source_cadence` (recorded in `feedback-deltas.md` audit lines + voice-proposal `source:` fields — data accrual for the doctor; NO per-cadence rendering machinery this cycle). Dedup: an accepted intent applies at most once per (day, intent, kind, entity) across ALL of yesterday's notes; both occurrences audit-logged (second tagged `[pending] dedup`). NEW intent `satisfaction` (global sentiment; requires NO kind) + NEW `proposed_target` value `satisfaction-series`; global sentiment moves satisfaction + engagement ONLY — kind weights move only on kind/entity-scoped prose; on disagreement with the same note's tap, PROSE WINS (the typed word is higher-intent than a tap). NEW downrank-skip symmetry: downrank prose scoped to a kind WITHOUT a named entity counts as that kind's SKIP (mirror of v0.99.0's uprank-counts-as-tick); downrank naming an entity keeps the entity-delta-only path. `applyIntentKindTicks` RENAMED `applyIntentKindObservations` (it now emits skips, not just ticks; all call sites + OI restatements updated in the same commit). `classifyEngagementDay` semantics UNCHANGED — five taps without other signal is still TAP-ONLY. `parseFeedbackCapture` learns the v=4 `kind_ticks` checklist section (ticked → `{kind, ticked: true}`; surfaced-but-unticked → `{kind, ticked: false}` — identical semantics to the legacy rating parse); mirrored-parser lockstep with `_parsePrior` maintained (v0.98.2 lesson 3).

**OI layer (single atomic commit, S1.7).** 4 non-EOD OIs rewire Step 4 from `parseRatingCallout` + `{{shared.rating_callout_template}}` to `parseFeedbackCapture` + `feedback_capture_template`; Step 8.5 either-marker prose; Step 8 slim sidecar field. `eod-review.md` v=3 → v=4 references; `_shared-clauses.md` `feedback_capture_template` → v=4 single shape, `rating_callout_template` retired from the emit path; `reconcile-cowork.md` Step 3 per-cadence sweep + v=4 detection, Step 3.6 source_cadence/dedup/downrank-skip/`satisfaction` intent, sub-step 13f cadence parameter + prose-over-tap + day-window trim; Idempotency + Failure-modes swept per the v0.99.0 lesson-3 rule (enumerate what RUNS per cadence/per note, not only what skips). S1.7.1 review fixes: item-mode kind-mapping SCOPED vs checklist `kind_ticks` (prevents double-count between an item-mode note's mattered-tick rollup and a checklist note's kind ticks); stale schema-4 normalize labels → 5.

**24 new HC-V1010 cases (+85 sub-asserts; 21 coincidental greens at S1.1, all explained per v0.99.0 lesson 1 — RED cases pin NEW fields/sections, not version strings):** CAPTURE (v=4 on all five cadences; section order; non-EOD checklist; prior tolerance; legacy upgrade-day transition; `kinds_listed`) + GUARD (either-marker; gate-closed incl. `learning_enabled: false`) + PARSE (`kind_ticks` extraction; non-leak; lockstep) + SAT (per-(day, cadence) append/overwrite; day-window trim + entry cap; prose-over-tap; v4-era normalize) + INTENT (`satisfaction` intent; `satisfaction-series` target; dedup applies-once/logs-twice; downrank-skip; source_cadence) + SCHEMA (V5 tolerance chain; non-destructive 4 → 5; deep-copy; idempotency; `.bak`) + DISPATCH (five paths emit v=4; uniform `surfaced_kinds_for_rating`; gate matrix unchanged on multi-note days). Supersedures with rationale: HC-V0990-SCHEMA-A6 (impossible-date fixture `"2026-05-32"`), HC-V0960-L-12 (retired emission pin), HC-V0970-O-7/T-3/T-14 (OI/template pins).

**Per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep:** 23 hardcoded `"0.100.1"` / `"0.39.0"` assertion values in `run-helper-cases.js` + 2 in `run-cowork-smoke.js` bumped to `"0.101.0"` / `"0.40.0"`; `ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop + cowork lockstep per v0.98.1 lesson #5.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2455 / 0** (+85 from v0.100.1's 2370); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 216 / 0; bootstrap 85 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0. Full `release:preflight` exit 0; `version-sync ok` at 0.101.0.

**5 lessons.**

1. **The fixture audit itself can have a scope gap:** S1.0's grep covered `platform/test/fixtures/` but compose goldens live under `helpers/fixtures/` — one CONSUMED golden needed regen and 5 UNCONSUMED dirs (regenerated only one cycle ago!) needed DELETION. v0.99.0 lesson-5 recurrence: fixtures without consumers rot silently; enumerate EVERY fixture root in the audit, and prefer deletion over regeneration for consumer-less dirs.
2. **Impossible fixture data is latent until code interprets the field:** HC-V0990-SCHEMA-A6's `"2026-05-32"` date survived a full cycle because the old `appendSatisfaction` never PARSED dates; the new day-window math threw on it. When a new feature starts parsing a previously-opaque field, audit every fixture that populates it.
3. **Per-task two-stage review caught 5 substantive issues across 4 stages** (unfalsifiable warmup pin; `learning_enabled: false` coverage gap; checklist kind dedup; item-mode/checklist kind-mapping collision in the reconcile OI; stale schema-4 labels) — keep the habit (4 catches at v0.99.0, 5 here).
4. **Resolve contract discrepancies structurally:** the v0.99.0-era EOD guard discrepancy (OI said pass `surfaced_kinds_for_rating`; EOD body had no rating marker) is resolved by the either-marker guard + uniform array passing — the discrepancy class is unrepresentable now, not documented-around.
5. **Pre-existing, out of scope, carry-forward:** weekly-review/monthly-review Step 7 example paths say `spice/cowork/daily/...`, contradicting their week/month-tier path headers — a latent mis-write instruction for the fire-time LLM. Parked as a PATCH candidate.

### v0.100.1 — docs-hub click hotfix (2026-06-12 close)

**SHIPPED 2026-06-12** (same day as v0.100.0). User-reported on accuris within minutes of deploy: clicking any docs-hub row threw `TypeError: e.indexOf is not a function` at `openLinkText`. Root cause: `project-docs-cards.js` passed `target: (p) => p.file.link` — a Dataview Link OBJECT — into `app.workspace.openLinkText()`, whose `getLinkpath` requires a string. The line was *deliberately preserved* from the pre-v0.100.0 helper as "in-production behavior" — but it had been silently broken on click since v0.50.0 (console-only error; the stacked grid's cards never navigated), masked because users routed around it. Fix: drop the target override; BeaconCards' default (`p.file.path`, a string) applies. NEW PDC-10 regression net asserts no Link-object reference in the helper. Project blueprint 1.15.0 → 1.15.1 PATCH; workshop 0.100.0 → 0.100.1 PATCH; helper-cases 2369 → **2370 / 0** (+1). **Lesson (extends "verify helper behavior before design asserts it"):** "intentionally UNCHANGED — in-production behavior" is only a safe preserve when the preserved line's runtime contract has been verified; in-production ≠ working. Carry-forward: BeaconCards defense-in-depth (coerce Link objects in targetFn results) as a cards-mechanism candidate.

Commits: `898f1c0` (S0 design) → `346bb31` (S1 plan) → `2d66ccc` (feat S1 docs-hub list rows) → `fca9ce4` (feat S2 accent nav buttons) → `b24042a` (chore S3 version bumps + pin sweep) → `63d7917` (chore S4 dogfood self-install) → S5 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `project-visual-polish`. Workshop 0.99.0 → 0.100.0; project blueprint 1.14.0 → 1.15.0; all other blueprints / mechanisms / contracts UNCHANGED. No cowork surface touched; no schema delta; no scheduled-job-contract bump (0.35.1 holds).

**Two helpers, project blueprint only.** `project-docs-cards.js`: Docs hub converts from a BeaconCards stacked auto-fit grid to `layout: "row"` single-column list rows (mtime-desc ordering). New `_formatCreated` reads canonical `created_at`, handles Luxon DateTime + string shapes, falls back to `file.ctime`; right-side meta `created <MMM D> · edited <relative>` per row; accent file glyph. Fixed latent bug: old code sorted/subtitled on dead key `p.created` (doc-notes carry `created_at`), so subtitle was empty and sort silently fell through. `project-nav-buttons.js`: project nav row delegates to `customJS.AccentButton.render(container, {label, icon, onClick, flex: true})` — identical accent styling to New Doc / New Note by construction; full-width row stretch; divider margin 6px → 14px. Hand-rolled `btnStyle` + hover handlers deleted. Workstream widget / action row / task tiles UNTOUCHED.

**Harness:** helper-cases 2362 → **2369 / 0** (+7: PDC-3 rewritten 1→2 asserts; +PDC-8 ×3; +PDC-9 ×1; PNB-WRAP-2/3 deleted −2; +PNB-ACC-1..3 ×4). All other harnesses GREEN + unchanged.
