### v0.99.0 — sparse-signal feedback (2026-06-12 close)

**SHIPPED 2026-06-12.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-12-v0.99.0-sparse-signal-feedback-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits S0..S3: `a635227` (S0 baseline) → `a485a87` (S1.0 pre-design grep audit) → `1da3238` (S1.1 26 RED HC-V0990 asserts) → `15f1344` (S1.2 compose v=3) → `3c21105` (S1.2.1 fixture regen to v=3 — review follow-up) → `818fee5` (S1.3 parseFeedbackCapture satisfaction) → `49c115d` (S1.4 gate + schema 4 + prefix + satisfaction primitives) → `f805324` (S1.4.1 V4 normalizer deep-copy + coercion — review follow-up) → `5908b18` (S1.5 OI layer: Step 3.4 gate + v=3 template) → `00f92cf` (S1.5.1 OI gate/bookkeeping ambiguity fixes — review follow-up) → `f4b0152` (S1.6 sidecar schema 1.3.0) → `ec4cb8b` (S1.7 version bumps + pin sweep) → `c249a1a` (S1.7.1 drop delisted realclaudian; convenience 0.4.1) → S3 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `sparse-signal-feedback`. Workshop 0.98.2 → 0.99.0; cowork 0.38.0 → 0.39.0; convenience 0.4.0 → 0.4.1 (forced PATCH — Discovery 4); eod-review sidecar schema enum gains `1.3.0` (additive; `feedback_capture.sentinel_version` enum gains `"v=3"`); feedback-capture sentinel v=2 → v=3 (tolerant parsers accept v=1/v=2/v=3); learned_weights schema 3 → 4; scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1 (OI/helper/schema-only change; landmine #20 doesn't fire); mechanism count UNCHANGED at 17.

**Origin.** The user's 2026-06-11 cold-start admission: "I have never really used the Rail L checkboxes. Accuris has zero ticks ever; headspace has ~2 experimental days. [...] Silence currently means 'user doesn't care' — for me it means 'user doesn't tick.' [...] The loop must make the system better on SPARSE signal." Before this cycle a surfaced-but-unticked kind counted as a SKIP, skips pushed weights down and counted toward warmup graduation — a never-ticking user uniformly demoted everything, and at graduation (headspace deadline: 2026-06-16) the silence-built weights would have become authoritative.

**Engagement-gated learning days.** NEW `reconcile-cowork.md` Step 3.4 classifies each (engagement, day) at the 03:00 reconciler by re-reading yesterday's notes: **ENGAGED** (≥1 item tick / kind-checkbox tick / knob ≠ same / non-empty typing box), **TAP-ONLY** (only the one-tap), **SILENT** (nothing). Only ENGAGED days emit observations (ticks AND skips), run decay (`updateWeights` decays unconditionally inside — verified at design time — so the gate lives at the CALL SITE), and progress warmup. TAP-ONLY days append satisfaction ONLY (`per_kind` subtree untouched). SILENT days write the `no feedback signal` audit line + totals bookkeeping only. Deterministic contract in `classifyEngagementDay` (ingest-feedback-helper.js); the OI restates it inline per the reconciler's pure-MCP posture. S1.5.1 swept every step + Idempotency + Failure-modes for newly-undefined gate interactions (Discovery 3).

**Prose-first capture v=3.** EOD Rail L reorders: one-tap `Useful: [ ] yes [ ] no` at the very top (SATISFACTION LOG ONLY — a bare tap is NOT full engagement, so lazy taps can't re-create the demotion spiral), free-text fence directly below (the PRIMARY channel for a prose person), per-kind Mattered/Didn't-like lists + knobs keep their exact v=2 mechanics but render below, collapsed. Sentinel `<!-- cowork:feedback-capture v=3 -->`; `_parsePrior` + `parseFeedbackCapture` (mirrored state machines, cross-ref comments per v0.98.2 lesson 3) tolerate v=1/v=2/v=3 — the v=1 and v=2 corpus stays readable forever. Tap parse: yes → true; no → false; both → ambiguous (preserved visually, no signal); neither → null; Tasks-suffix tolerant. `<kind>:` prose-prefix fast path (`parseKindPrefixLines`): a typing-box line whose prefix case-insensitively matches a kind in the engagement's dispatch set is deterministically pre-bound to that kind BEFORE Step 3.6 — the LLM classifies intent but CANNOT re-scope the kind; non-matching prefixes fall through. `applyIntentKindTicks`: an uprank-intent prose line scoped to a kind counts as that kind's tick. NO new substitution tokens.

**Schema 4 + silence-reset migration.** `normalizeLearnedWeightsV4` (tolerates on-disk 2 / missing / `"1.1.0"` / 3 / 4): `totals` gain `engaged_days[]` + `satisfaction[]` (rolling 30, same-day overwrite, via `appendSatisfaction` — the platform's first cheap KPI and the v0.99.1 doctor's first self-grading input); `warmup_until` retired (kept for shape tolerance, set null). ONE-TIME migration when on-disk schema ≠ 4: keep every kind's weight + ticks (real signal), ZERO skips (silence-contaminated), force `warmup: true` — defuses the 2026-06-16 silent-graduation deadline by construction. Graduation rule now `engaged_days.length >= 7` AND `ticks + skips >= 7` (`evaluateWarmup` code UNCHANGED — the caller passes engaged-day counts instead of calendar days). Same block-scoped `.bak`-first write contract on `user-preferences.md` frontmatter. S1.4.1 hardened the normalizer: deep-copy totals (no input aliasing) + `Number()` coercion on the schema gate (string `"4"` never re-runs the destructive reset) — Discovery 2. `"use strict"` added to the three touched helpers (compose-feedback-capture / learn-from-checks / ingest-feedback).

**Convenience 0.4.1 (forced mid-cycle PATCH).** `realclaudian` (auto-install default since v0.94.0) was DELISTED from the upstream obsidian-releases index (verified 2026-06-12; only forks remain) — `applyExternalPluginInstall` errored on every real install and integration-smoke went RED mid-cycle after being GREEN at S0 hours earlier. S1.7.1 removed the dead `external_plugins[]` entry; installed copies in consumer vaults untouched. The helper's loud error-level posture WORKED — it caught a real ecosystem regression (Discovery 4; a v0.99.1 doctor seed: index-presence drift is doctor-checkable).

**26 new HC sub-asserts** (24 RED + 2 coincidental greens at S1.1): HC-V0990-CAPTURE-A1..A7 (v=3 sentinel + section order + v=3/v=2/v=1 prior tolerance + ambiguous tap + empty-day + Tasks-suffix on tap line); HC-V0990-PARSE-A1..A4 (tap extraction yes/no/both/neither + body tolerance + state-machine non-leak + mirrored-parser lockstep grep; A2 coincidentally green — `FEEDBACK_SENTINEL_RX` is generic `v=(\d+)`, Discovery 1); HC-V0990-GATE-A1..A6 (classification matrix + ENGAGED full pipeline + TAP-ONLY satisfaction-only with byte-identical per_kind + SILENT audit-only + prose-counts-as-engaged/uprank-as-tick + engaged-day graduation + rolling-30 trim; A5 green by design — `evaluateWarmup` unchanged); HC-V0990-SCHEMA-A1..A6 (V4 normalizer tolerance + keep-weight-and-ticks/zero-skips/force-warmup + idempotency incl. string "4" + deep-copy pins + `.bak`); HC-V0990-PREFIX-A1..A3 (bind + fall-through + case-insensitive dispatch-set membership).

**Per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep:** 23 hardcoded `"0.98.2"` / `"0.38.0"` assertion values in `run-helper-cases.js` + 2 in `run-cowork-smoke.js` bumped to `"0.99.0"` / `"0.39.0"`; `ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop + cowork (`blueprints[].cowork.version`) lockstep per v0.98.1 lesson #5.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2362 / 0** (+26); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 216 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0 (RED mid-cycle on the realclaudian delisting; GREEN after S1.7.1); bootstrap 85 / 0. Full `release:preflight` exit 0; `version-sync ok` at 0.99.0.

**6 lessons.**

1. **RED-phase coincidental greens:** `FEEDBACK_SENTINEL_RX` is generic (`v=(\d+)`), so the v=2 parser already accepted v=3 bodies — PARSE-A2 passed before any code change (GATE-A5 green by design: `evaluateWarmup` code unchanged, only call semantics shifted). When a parser is version-tolerant by regex, RED cases must assert the NEW field (satisfaction), not just the new version string.
2. **Pure-data helpers must deep-copy EVERYTHING and version gates must coerce types:** quality review caught `Object.assign({}, eng.totals)` aliasing the caller's arrays through the V3 normalizer AND `priorVersion !== 4` re-running the destructive silence-reset on string `"4"` (plausible after LLM/YAML round-trips; V3 already tolerated string `"1.1.0"` — precedent). Fixed S1.4.1 with test pins on SCHEMA-A5.
3. **Adding a gate to an existing multi-step OI requires sweeping EVERY step + Idempotency + Failure-modes:** spec review found 4 ambiguities the 3am LLM could not resolve (totals bookkeeping on gated days; stale "ZERO signal → no writes" line vs 13a; skeleton `warmup_until` init vs retirement; Step 12 missing Step 11's ENGAGED-only annotation). Fixed S1.5.1. Gate consequences must enumerate what RUNS, not only what SKIPS.
4. **`external_plugins[]` entries are live dependencies on a third-party index:** realclaudian delisted upstream → every real install errored → integration-smoke RED mid-cycle. Removed at S1.7.1; convenience 0.4.1; installed copies untouched. Doctor seed: index-presence drift is a doctor-checkable KPI.
5. **Fixtures without a consuming runner rot silently:** the compose-feedback-capture fixture dir (5 cases) pinned v=2 truth, was consumed by ZERO runners (grep-verified), and would have misled any future regeneration. Regenerated at S1.2.1. Every fixture dir needs a consuming harness or deletion — "golden" files no test reads are landmines.
6. **Subagent harness reports need controller verification:** the S1.7 subagent reported bootstrap 125/1 and called the failure pre-existing; direct re-run showed 85/0 (the real failure was integration-smoke's realclaudian error, Discovery 4). Trust-but-verify applies to harness COUNTS, not just code claims.

**Action required post-deploy.** Per consumer vault: Safeguard 1 snapshot FIRST, then `sauce update --bump-pins` (materializes updated compose-feedback-capture / learn-from-checks / ingest-feedback helpers + reconcile-cowork / eod-review / `_shared-clauses.md` OI + sidecar schema enum `1.3.0` + ingest-feedback SKILL.md gate sentence); verify workshop 0.99.0 + cowork 0.39.0 + convenience 0.4.1. THEN ONE `align-scheduled-jobs` run per vault from Cloud Cowork (the real surface — `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` does NOT exist, per v0.98.2 Discovery 7). First post-update 03:00 run migrates learned_weights to schema 4 in place (`.bak`-first; skips zeroed + warmup restored BY DESIGN). `realclaudian` no longer auto-installs; installed copies untouched.

**Carry-forward.** v0.99.1 NEXT: `cowork:doctor` — embedded in the nightly reconciler, 4 checks locked at the 2026-06-11 brainstorm (wrapper/sidecar version-drift; sidecar conformance window; align-audit staleness; self-grading digest — satisfaction trend NOW ACCUMULATING + top entity-weight movers); anomalies → one collapsed morning-brief callout, details → reconciler log. Then v0.99.2+ lockfiles + path-relative wrappers (bundled). Memory-engine jobs documented as deliberately out-of-contract (ecosystem note lands in doctor cycle). Parked/gated: v0.95.1-migrator; weekly/monthly per-item ticks (demand); workshop-wide strict-mode sweep; formula-constant tuning (data).

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-11-v0.99.0-sparse-signal-feedback-{design,plan}.md` + `Docs/plans/2026-06-12-v0.99.0-sparse-signal-feedback-result.md` + `Docs/prompts/2026-06-12-post-v0.99.0-next-cycle-handoff.md`.

### v0.98.2 — feedback-loop closure (2026-06-11 close)

**SHIPPED 2026-06-11.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-11-v0.98.2-feedback-loop-closure-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits S0..S3: `3f13568` (S0 baseline) → `3d18b29` (S1.0 pre-design audit) → `545f071` (S1.1 RED) → `0d3ed08` (S1.2 compose-feedback-capture v=2) → `0daa28d` (S1.3 parseFeedbackCapture v=2) → `4ea47e7` (S1.3.1 redeclare loop variable hotfix) → `9694371` (S1.4 ingest-feedback deterministic core + SKILL.md) → `c6636b9` (S1.5 OI layer: reconciler ingest + v=2 rail + voice proposals) → `e4aeb3e` (S1.6 sidecar schema 1.2.0) → `bf240f0` (S1.7 version bumps + ingest-feedback ship surface + pin sweep) → S3 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `feedback-loop-closure`. Workshop 0.98.1 → 0.98.2; cowork 0.37.0 → 0.38.0; eod-review sidecar schema enum gains `1.2.0` (additive; pre-1.2.0 sidecars still validate); feedback-capture sentinel v=1 → v=2 (tolerant parser accepts both); learned_weights schema 2 → 3 (nested per-kind entity maps; in-place additive migration in `user-preferences.md` frontmatter); scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1 (OI-content-only change; landmine #20 doesn't fire).

**Origin.** Closes the user vision the v0.98.x arc has been advancing since 2026-06-10: "just saying thumbs up isn't enough. what if we added a section for didn't like? what if there was a section where i could provide feedback, and another job took what i liked, what i didn't like, my feedback, then updated the relevant files so that all scheduled jobs would then adjust the next day?" v0.98.0 made the brief predictive; v0.98.1 shipped the capture surface; v0.98.2 ships the reconciler ingest + downvote capture + voice proposals + tomorrow's-brief consumption. The v0.98.x substance arc is now CLOSED.

**Architectural posture preserved.** Deterministic-helpers-for-shape, OI-prose-for-voice (cowork-ecosystem § 11.1). The reconciler stays pure-MCP at fire time (per its v0.97.1 posture) — `ingest-feedback-helper.js` is the testable reference implementation whose contract the OI re-states inline, exactly as `reconcile-cowork.md` already re-states the `updateWeights` formula. The LLM proposes (free-text intents); the deterministic layer disposes (validation rules + clamps + allowlisted write-targets). Voice/personality changes are PROPOSED, never auto-applied — tick-to-approve in the next morning brief.

**Capture v=2 shape.** Rail L per-kind sub-callouts now carry BOTH `Mattered:` and `Didn't like:` checklists sharing the same `^item-<kind>-<7hex>` block-IDs (defined ONCE in the body; rail only links). Section state machine in the parser: tick lines after `Mattered:` → `ticks{}`; after `Didn't like:` → `downvotes{}`. Item ticked in BOTH → preserve UI state + flag sidecar `ambiguous_items: [<item_id>]`; ingest treats as no-signal (mirrors v0.98.1 ambiguous-knob guard). Tolerant parser: v=2 → full parse; v=1 → parse with empty downvotes (v=1 corpus stays readable forever); legacy `rating-block` → existing `parseRatingCallout` path (4 non-EOD cadences). Tasks-plugin trailing-annotation tolerance (`✅ YYYY-MM-DD`, `📅 YYYY-MM-DD`) added on EVERY tick line — including `parseRatingCallout` kind-checkbox lines (live in headspace 2026-06-10 EOD; the parsers must tolerate).

**Sidecar schema 1.2.0 additive.** `eod-review@1.0.0.json` `schema_version` enum gains `"1.2.0"`. `feedback_capture` adds `items[]` identity registry (`{item_id, kind, identifier, label}` per surfaced item) + `ambiguous_items[]`. Reconciler identity-lookup order: sidecar `items[]` (1.2.0+) → wikilink label parsed from the `.md` (v=1-era fallback) → kind-only rollup (kind-level signal never drops even when identity resolution fails). All three sidecar schemas (1.0.0 / 1.1.0 / 1.2.0) continue to validate; additive only.

**Ingest pipeline — five new/extended OI steps in `reconcile-cowork.md`.** **Step 3 (extended)** parses BOTH `rating-block` AND `feedback-capture` (v=1 + v=2) sentinels — RESTORES EOD signal to per-kind learning, which v0.98.1's sentinel switch had silently broken (first affected fire: headspace 2026-06-11 EOD; absorption discovery #2 from the design doc). EOD contributes kind-level observations via: kind tick = ≥1 mattered-tick in that kind; else kind skip. **NEW Step 3.5 (deterministic rollup)** rolls per-item mattered ticks (+0.05 entity delta on the item's person/channel/topic under its kind, identity via registry) + per-item didn't-like ticks (−0.10; rarer + stronger signal) + frequency knobs (±0.05 per-kind delta applied AFTER formula + re-clamped; ambiguous → no signal). Idempotency via new `totals.feedback_ingested_days[]` alongside existing `scanned_days[]`. **NEW Step 3.6 (inline LLM free-text intent extraction)** runs an inline LLM pass (in the reconciler session — no separate API call, no model pin) extracting structured intents (uprank / downrank / voice_correction / coverage_gap / frequency / other) from the `free_text` content with source quotes + proposed write targets + confidence levels. Deterministic helper rules REJECT: unknown intent / missing or non-verbatim source_quote / target outside the four-value allowlist / `learned_weights` intent naming no kind / low-confidence → logged as pending, not applied. Hard suppression intents ("stop surfacing X's emails") → direct floor-set `weight = 0.10` on the named entity under the named kind. **Steps 4–5 (extended)** apply `learned_weights` schema 2 → 3 in-place additive migration on `user-preferences.md` frontmatter (block-scoped `.bak`-first rewrite of ONLY the `learned_weights:` block). Schema 3 shape: `engagements.<eng>.per_kind.<kind>` gains `per_person.<name>.{weight, ticks, downvotes, last_updated}` + `per_channel.<id>.{...}` + `per_topic.<topic>.{...}`. Entity weight math: start 1.00; clamp [0.10, 3.00]; banker's rounding (3 places); light daily decay TOWARD 1.00 — explicitly `w' = 1.00 + (w − 1.00) × 0.995` (NOT `w × 0.995`, which would decay toward zero). **NEW Step 5.5 (apply non-weight deltas)** — microscope `## What matters` APPEND-only one dated line per delta with source quote; voice/personality corrections PROPOSED to `memory/<eng>/voice-proposals.md` (durable entry with id, exact append text, target file, source quote, status, expiry = +7 days); coverage gaps queued to `memory/<eng>/coverage-queue.md`; everything (applied / proposed / rejected / pending) logged to `memory/<eng>/feedback-deltas.md` append-only audit log. **NEW Step 5.6 (voice-proposal approval sweep)** parses yesterday's morning-briefing `.md` for the voice-proposals callout; per proposal line: `[x]` → apply the exact append text to the proposal's target file (append-only) + mark `applied` + audit-log; untouched past expiry → `expired` (inert, visible); free-text-rejected ("no, don't change that") → `dismissed`.

**Morning-briefing voice-proposals callout.** Rendered only when `voice-proposals.md` has `pending` entries (collapsed, compact). Per-line HTML-comment id (e.g. `<!-- cowork:voice-proposal id=vp-20260612-1 -->`) is the deterministic parse key (same pattern as every other cowork sentinel). Suffix-tolerant tick parsing applies.

**Consumption — how tomorrow's brief actually changes.** One new rule in the shared microscope/voice clause + Step 2 of morning-briefing and eod-review OIs: when composing per-kind blocks, consult `per_kind.<kind>.per_person/per_channel/per_topic`: ≥1.20 → lead the section (surface first, fuller rendering); ≤0.50 → cap to one line; ≤0.25 → omit UNLESS the item carries a hard signal (direct @-mention, money movement, inner-circle going-dark per existing microscope contracts); entities in warmup (<3 observations) → advisory only. Structural rules still WIN over voice on conflict; thresholds are OI prose consumed at gather/compose time, deliberately NOT helper-enforced (rendering judgment is the LLM's lane; the WEIGHTS are deterministic).

**User-content safety preserved (4 safeguards unchanged from v0.98.1).** No new `files[]` entries under `context/<id>/` / `memory/` / `daily/` / `weekly/` / `monthly/` / `snapshots/` / `summaries/` — verified by the automated Safeguard 3 guard (`scripts/check-files-forbidden-paths.js`) already in `release:preflight`. New per-engagement files (`feedback-deltas.md`, `voice-proposals.md`, `coverage-queue.md`) are runtime-created by the reconciler session via Obsidian MCP — same posture as `reconciler-log.md` today. `user-preferences.md` writes remain block-scoped (`learned_weights:` only) with `.bak`-first — the existing reconciler contract, unchanged in kind. Microscope appends are append-only by contract; voice files are NEVER written without a ticked approval; every write is audit-logged with a source quote.

**23 new HC sub-asserts** (18 HC-V0982 + 5 EODITEMS fixture sub-asserts): HC-V0982-CAPTURE-A1..A6 (v=2 sentinel + Mattered+Didn't-like shared IDs + re-fire preservation + v=1 prior tolerance + both-lists-ticked → ambiguous_items[] + empty-day minimal rail); HC-V0982-PARSE-A1..A4 (downvotes map extraction + v=1 tolerance + Tasks-suffix tolerance on tick AND `parseRatingCallout` kind lines + section state machine no kind leak); HC-V0982-INGEST-A1..A8 (mattered +0.05 / didn't-like −0.10 / knob ±0.05 re-clamped / floor-set 0.10 / decay `1.00 + (w − 1.00) × 0.995` / normalizer tolerates schema 2 / missing / "1.1.0" / 3 / intent validation rejects out-of-allowlist + non-verbatim quotes + kindless weights intents / audit-entry format golden). Plus 5 EODITEMS fixture sub-asserts closing the v0.98.1 carry-forward gap (end-to-end compose-body → cadence dispatch → composeFeedbackCapture → Rail L).

**Per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep** (v0.98.1 lesson #5): ~25 hardcoded `"0.98.1"` / `"0.37.0"` assertion values bumped to `"0.98.2"` / `"0.38.0"` across HC-V0891 / V0900 / V0901 / V0920 / V0930 / V0931 VERSION-* + MANIFEST blocks (~22 in `run-helper-cases.js`; ~3 in `run-cowork-smoke.js`). `ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop + cowork (`blueprints[].cowork.version`) lockstep — pin explicitly in sweep checklist per v0.98.1 lesson.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2336 / 0** (+23); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface **216 / 0** (+1 — new ingest-feedback shim); integration-smoke 36 / 0; cli 132 / 0; bootstrap 85 / 0. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.98.2` ALL GREEN. Safeguard 3 forbidden-paths check GREEN.

**Action required post-deploy.** Per consumer vault: `sauce update --bump-pins` (materializes NEW `ingest-feedback-helper.js` + NEW `ingest-feedback/SKILL.md` shim + updated compose-feedback-capture / learn-from-checks / write-atomic-note helpers + updated reconcile-cowork / eod-review / morning-briefing / `_shared-clauses.md` OI + eod-review sidecar schema enum gains `1.2.0`). THEN `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` once per vault from claude.ai's Cowork UI — Rail A pushes new wrapper bodies (contract version stays at 0.35.1; wrapper template substitution refreshes against updated OI sources; NEW substitution tokens `{{$voice_proposals_count}}` + `{{$voice_proposal_lines}}` flow through). **Accuris two-version catch-up:** the local accuris vault sits at workshop 0.98.0 / cowork 0.36.0; ONE `sauce update --bump-pins` invocation catches BOTH v0.98.1 AND v0.98.2 deltas in a single materialize pass. Verify both `compose-feedback-capture-helper.js` (v0.98.1) AND `ingest-feedback-helper.js` (v0.98.2) are materialized post-update. Pre-deploy snapshot recommended per `commands/install.md` § Upgrading Safeguard 1.

**6 lessons.**

1. **Plan fixture hashes must be computed, not invented.** Plan's `_itemId("chat","chat:c1:t1") = "item-chat-31bc2eb"` vs the real `item-chat-10f069a`. Plan flagged the placeholder with a `# compute me` instruction; implementer caught cleanly at S1.1. Future plans should follow the same convention.
2. **Rewriting a regex-scan block can orphan loop variable declarations.** S1.3 tick-block rewrite removed `let m;` that the (unchanged) knob loop relied on → implicit global. Caught by quality review; fixed at S1.3.1 (`4ea47e7`). Strict-mode adoption queued as a v0.98.2.x PATCH candidate. Lesson: when replacing PART of a function, grep the remainder for identifiers the removed lines declared.
3. **Intentionally-parallel parser implementations need cross-reference comments.** `compose-feedback-capture-helper.js`'s `_parsePrior` and `learn-from-checks-helper.js`'s `parseFeedbackCapture` carry mirrored section state machines; cross-ref comments added at S1.4 so future editors keep them in sync.
4. **`validateSidecar`'s real signature is `(payload, schemaPath)` not `(payload, cadence)`.** Plan one-liner adapted at S1.6 (caller computes schema path from cadence). Lesson: the verify-helpers-before-design rule applies to PLANS, not just designs.
5. **PRE-EXISTING: live headspace 2026-06-10 eod-review sidecar FAILS schema validation** (`coverage_gap: []` array vs schema's object shape) — a fire-time LLM deviation that predates v0.98.2 (stash-verified). Means `writeAtomicNote`'s validate gate did not run (or was bypassed) on that fire. Queued as a v0.98.2.x / v0.99.0 observability candidate; `cowork:doctor` would catch this class. Lesson: deterministic gates are advisory unless gate-invocation is OBSERVABLE.
6. **New shared-clause substitution tokens must be registered in the HC-V0970-T-3 allowlist** — the two voice-proposal tokens (`{{$voice_proposals_count}}`, `{{$voice_proposal_lines}}`) needed allowlist entries at S1.5 (the wrapper-template substitution validator's `KNOWN_SUBSTITUTION_TOKENS` constant). Future OI-prose changes introducing new tokens must update the allowlist in the same commit.

**Carry-forward.** v0.98.2.x PATCH candidates: `cowork:doctor` (sidecar conformance scan; surfaced concretely by Discovery #5); per-item ticks on weekly/monthly cadences (additive); strict-mode adoption for helpers (per Discovery #2; workshop-wide hygiene). v0.99.0+ items: cross-machine wrappers + lockfile semantics for racing writes; `cowork:doctor` observability skill (full scope); entity-weight live-grading after 7 days (first time the platform can grade itself empirically); v0.95.1-migrator carry-forward (still parked from v0.96.0+; defer until needed).

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-11-v0.98.2-feedback-loop-closure-{design,plan,result}.md`.

### v0.98.1 — questionnaire expansion + free-text capture (2026-06-11 close)

**SHIPPED 2026-06-11.** See `Docs/plans/2026-06-11-v0.98.1-questionnaire-capture-result.md` for the full narrative. Commits S0..S3: `3c8bafe` (S0 baseline) → `caadddf` (S1.0 pre-design audit) → `ecb0cbe` (S1.1 RED) → `40fe750` (S1.2 NEW helper + SKILL.md + fixtures) → `bf7326c` (S1.3 block-IDs) → `4008922` (S1.4 parseFeedbackCapture) → `bf67bd4` (S1.5 cadence dispatch + OI swap) → `0ece9ef` (S1.6 sidecar schema 1.1.0) → `ff8fe6c` (S1.7 version bumps + files[] + Safeguard 3) → `f573c6f` (S1.7.1 SKILL.md reconciliation) → S3 cycle-close this commit.

**Codename:** `questionnaire-capture`. Workshop 0.98.0 → 0.98.1; cowork 0.36.0 → 0.37.0; eod-review sidecar schema 1.0.0 → 1.1.0 (additive); scheduled-job-contract UNCHANGED at 0.35.1.

**Origin.** User direction captured 2026-06-10 post-v0.98.0 close: "just saying thumbs up isn't enough. what if we added a section for didn't like? what if there was a section where i could provide feedback, and another job took what i liked, what i didn't like, my feedback, then updated the relevant files so that all scheduled jobs would then adjust the next day?" v0.98.1 ships the capture surface; v0.98.2 closes the loop with ingest.

**Architectural posture: deterministic-helpers-for-shape, OI-prose-for-voice.** Item-IDs are computed in `compose-body-helper.js` (hash-stable across re-fires); the Rail L body is built in the NEW `compose-feedback-capture-helper.js`; the sentinel parse lives in `learn-from-checks-helper.js`. The eod-review OI Step 4 swaps one template invocation for one helper call. No LLM-driven structural computation (lesson v0.98.0 §2 — LLM-rendered shape contracts drift; deterministic helpers don't). The first cycle that EXPLICITLY guards user-authored cowork content via Safeguard 3 automation (`scripts/check-files-forbidden-paths.js` wired into `release:preflight` — fails CI if any `files[]` dest path matches user-owned path patterns).

**New EOD Rail L shape.** Replaces the coarse kind-checkbox rating block with: stable per-item `^item-<kind>-<7-char-sha>` block-IDs emitted by compose-body; per-item ticks pointing at those block-IDs via wikilinks (`[[#^item-<kind>-<sha>|<label>]]`); per-kind 3-position frequency knobs (`less / same / more`); tagged fenced `` ```feedback…``` `` free-text block; `<!-- cowork:feedback-capture v=1 -->` sentinel. EOD is the ONLY cadence with the new shape. Morning-briefing / midday-tripwire / weekly-review / monthly-review keep `{{shared.rating_callout_template}}` unchanged.

**Sidecar schema additive (1.0.0 → 1.1.0).** `eod-review@1.0.0.json` (filename unchanged per workshop convention) gains optional `feedback_capture` field: `{ sentinel_version, item_count, kinds_with_knobs[], ambiguous_knobs[] }`. Pre-v0.98.1 EOD sidecars (without the field) still validate. Purpose: v0.98.2 ingest fast-path "is there feedback to parse?" check without re-reading the markdown body.

**12 new HC sub-asserts** (HC-V0981-FEEDBACK-A1..A8 + HC-V0981-PARSEFEEDBACK-A1..A4): sentinel emission + per-kind sub-callouts + knob rows + free-text fence + re-fire tick/knob/free-text preservation + empty-day minimal Rail L + block-ID format regex + ambiguous-knob sidecar flag + parseFeedbackCapture tick map + knob map + free-text extraction + graceful null sentinel.

**Per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep** (v0.93.3 lesson 5.3): ~25 hardcoded `"0.98.0"` / `"0.36.0"` assertion values bumped to `"0.98.1"` / `"0.37.0"` across HC-V0891 / V0900 / V0901 / V0920 / V0930 / V0931 VERSION-* + MANIFEST blocks (~22 in `run-helper-cases.js`; ~3 in `run-cowork-smoke.js`). `ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop + cowork lockstep.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2313 / 0** (+12); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 215 / 0 (+1 — new compose-feedback-capture shim); integration-smoke 36 / 0; cli 132 / 0; bootstrap 85 / 0. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.98.1` ALL GREEN. Safeguard 3 forbidden-paths check GREEN.

**Action required post-deploy.** `sauce update --bump-pins` per consumer vault (materializes: NEW `compose-feedback-capture-helper.js` + NEW `SKILL.md` shim + updated `compose-body-helper.js` / `learn-from-checks-helper.js` / `write-atomic-note-helper.js` + updated `eod-review.md` OI + `_shared-clauses.md` `feedback_capture_template` block + `eod-review@1.0.0.json` schema 1.1.0). THEN `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` once per vault from claude.ai's Cowork UI — Rail A pushes new wrapper bodies (contract version STAYS at 0.35.1; wrapper template substitution refreshes against updated orchestrator-instructions sources). Pre-deploy snapshot recommended per `commands/install.md` § Upgrading Safeguard 1.

**6 lessons.**

1. **Bare fence shape for feedback block.** Design sketched `> `-prefixed fences; implementer landed bare fences + made `_parsePrior` tolerant of both. Functional/parse equivalent; visual UX deferred to v0.98.1.x IF deployed briefs show poor callout containment.
2. **Item-ID emission opt-in via `block.items[]`.** Existing compose-body block shape had no structured items. Implementer made item-ID emission opt-in when `block.items[]` is present; other 8 fixtures stayed byte-identical. Cleaner than the plan's "extend all per-kind callouts" approach.
3. **S1.5 scope expansion — compose-body cadence dispatch.** Plan's S1.5 covered OI prose only. Production routing required cadence dispatch in `compose-body-helper.js` (`cadence === "eod-review"` + `surfaced_items_by_kind` → `composeFeedbackCapture`; else `composeRatingCallout`). The OI change and the helper routing change are a single atomic unit — plan must treat them as such.
4. **SKILL.md two-location reconciliation.** Plan's S1.2 wrote runtime path directly (wrong source-of-truth). Blueprint source was empty until S1.7; S1.7.1 reconciled. New SKILL.md content ALWAYS belongs in the blueprint source first; runtime is derived.
5. **ranch/platform-subscription.json blueprints[].cowork.version pin missed.** S1.7 jq command missed this pin; 3 tests failed. Future cycles must include this in the VERSION-pin sweep jq automation checklist.
6. **Scripts/ vs scripts/ gitignore conflict.** macOS case-insensitive filesystem matched `/scripts/` gitignore entry against `/Scripts/`, hiding the new guard. Verify `git status -s` immediately after creating files under any path that shares a name-pattern with a gitignore entry.

**Carry-forward.** v0.98.1.x PATCH candidates: bare-fence visual UX (if observed on deploy); per-item DOWNVOTE row (per user vision); integration fixture for composeBody cadence dispatch (untested end-to-end). v0.98.2 closes the feedback loop: `cowork:ingest-feedback` sub-skill (Sonnet 4.6 pass) + structured deltas to `learned_weights` (schema 2 → 3) + microscopes / what_matters with audit trail; voice changes PROPOSED, never auto-applied. 5 open questions for v0.98.2 brainstorm (see result doc § Carry-forward).

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-11-v0.98.1-questionnaire-capture-{design,plan,result}.md`.

### v0.98.0 — synopsis-density rewrite (2026-06-10 close)

**Codename:** `synopsis-density`. Workshop 0.97.4 → 0.98.0; cowork 0.35.4 → 0.36.0; contract 0.35.1 (UNCHANGED).

**Origin.** User direction captured in memory `project_v098_brainstorm_direction.md` (2026-06-10): "Reports too long, too much reading, not enough summarization. ... long term it will give me the info that I need before I need it." The brief shape today emphasizes COVERAGE (every kind enumerated, every per-kind callout open by default, `[!tip] <closing>` at the bottom). User wants emphasis on PREDICTION (highest-blocking action first; cross-kind dependencies as connective tissue; per-kind detail behind a click).

**Shape contract change (atomic notes across 5 cadences).** Lead callout flips from `> [!info]- <old title>` (collapsed by default, behind a click) to `> [!info]+ <per-cadence title>` (OPEN by default, ≤80 words, predictive). All per-kind callouts flip from `[!<type>]+` (open) to `[!<type>]-` (collapsed). The bottom `> [!tip] <closing title>` callout is REMOVED entirely. Each cadence carries the per-cadence lead title:

- morning-briefing → `What matters today`
- midday-tripwire → `What changed since morning`
- eod-review → `What landed today`
- weekly-review → `Where the week landed`
- monthly-review → `Where the month landed`

**Composition rules section.** Each `content/data/orchestrator-instructions/<cadence>.md` gains a `## Synopsis composition rules (v0.98.0 contract)` EOF section with 5 rules: (1) ≤80 words hard cap, prefer 50-60; (2) first sentence concrete blocking-action token; (3) cross-kind dependencies as connective tissue, not per-kind enumeration; (4) ≤3 distinct must-knows beyond the lead; (5) empty-day plain-acknowledgment ("Quiet day —"). Voice contract applies on top; structural rules WIN on conflict.

**compose-body-helper.js scope expansion** (S1.4 discovery). The plan assumed orchestrator-instructions OI changes drove rendered output; the helper code was supposed to be neutral. Reality: the helper encoded the OLD shape — `closing_md` validation, hardcoded `+` per-kind sigil in `_wrapCallout` + `_computeAssertions`, closing-line body-assembly emit, `closing_md` destructure. Scope expanded to flip per-kind sigil to `-`, drop `closing_md` validation (legacy keys tolerated as no-op), drop closing-line body emit, drop `closing_md` destructure. 8 `expected-body.md` files regenerated alongside the 9 fixture sweep.

**Engagement-template scope narrowing** (S1.3 discovery). The plan assumed all 15 prompt files (3 templates × 5 cadences) shared cadence vocabulary (`## Today at a glance`, `## Week in review`, etc.). On-disk reality: 13 of 15 use per-bundle author-content H2s (e.g. `## Flagged charges`, `## Today's wins`, `## ⏭ Next week setup`, `## ⏱ Billable hours snapshot`). Only the 3 morning-briefing files happen to carry the OLD `## Today at a glance` + `## Today's focus` verbatim. Scope NARROWED to those 3 files; the other 12 are author-content and were left untouched.

**11 new HC sub-asserts** (HC-V0980-SYNOPSIS-A1..A6 + HC-V0980-CADENCES-A1..A5): lead callout sentinel + closing-callout absence + per-kind `-` sigil + word count ≤80 + first-sentence-concrete + empty-day "Quiet day" fallback + 4 per-cadence variant titles + cross-cadence parity.

**Per-cycle VERSION-pin sweep** (v0.93.3 lesson 5.3): 26 hardcoded `"0.97.x"` / `"0.35.x"` HC assertion values bumped to `"0.98.0"` / `"0.36.0"` across HC-V0891 / V0900 / V0901 / V0920 / V0930 / V0931 VERSION-* + MANIFEST blocks (23 in `run-helper-cases.js`; 3 in `run-cowork-smoke.js`). `ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop + cowork lockstep.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2301 / 0** (+11); cowork-smoke 954 / 0; claude-surface 214 / 0; integration-smoke 36 / 0; cli 132 / 0; bootstrap 85 / 0. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.98.0` ALL GREEN.

**Commits (S0..S3):** `86ed5c1` (S0 baseline) + `506a95f` (S1.1 RED) + `2f49e5a` (S1.2.1 morning-briefing OI) + `7ec48a9` (S1.2.2 midday-tripwire OI) + `68dff20` (S1.2.3 eod-review OI) + `d26079e` (S1.2.4 weekly-review OI) + `ea38502` (S1.2.5 monthly-review OI) + `115fa13` (S1.3 SCOPED engagement-template rename) + `48207e2` (S1.4 compose-body fixture + helper sweep) + `3c5f325` (S1.5 version bumps + VERSION-pin sweep) + S3 cycle-close this commit.

**Action required post-deploy.** `sauce update --bump-pins` per consumer vault (materializes 5 new orchestrator-instructions + 3 morning-briefing engagement-template prompts + updated compose-body-helper.js). THEN `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` once per vault from claude.ai's Cowork UI — Rail A pushes new wrapper bodies (contract version stays at 0.35.1; wrapper template substitution refreshes against new orchestrator-instructions sources). Next scheduled cron fire after deploy emits the new shape.

**Lessons.**

1. **Verify on-disk vocabulary before designing per-file rename sweeps.** The plan's 15-file rename table assumed cadence-vocabulary H2s across all engagement-template prompts; reality is 13/15 are per-bundle author content. A `grep -n '^## '` at design time would have surfaced the scope reality before the table was committed.
2. **Helper code can encode shape contracts that should live at the OI layer.** When designing a "string-level contract change at the OI layer only" cycle, grep the helper for the shape literals (`[!example]+`, `closing_md`, etc.) at design time to confirm they don't live both places. v0.97.x wrapper architecture should have surfaced this earlier.
3. **Structural rules WIN on conflict with voice contract.** Composition-rules section explicitly states voice still applies on top, but structural rules (word cap, first-sentence-concrete, empty-day plain-acknowledgment) win when they conflict with voice (e.g. a verbose voice that wants to lecture).

**Carry-forward.** Shim path mismatch in `.claude/skills/cowork/<cadence>/SKILL.md` referencing absent `.claude/skills/cowork/data/orchestrator-instructions/` path (actual runtime path is `spice/cowork/data/orchestrator-instructions/`) — pre-existing, NOT v0.98.0-introduced. v0.98.0.x PATCH candidate. v0.98.1 ships questionnaire expansion + free-text capture; v0.98.2 closes the loop with reconciler ingest. v0.95.0 cowork-spine formally PARKED (cohesion-sweep premise obsoleted by v0.97.x wrapper architecture).

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-10-v0.98.0-synopsis-density-{design,plan,result}.md`.

### v0.97.4 — prose-invariant write-guards (2026-06-10 close)

**Codename:** `prose-invariant-write-guards`. Workshop 0.97.3 → 0.97.4; cowork 0.35.3 → 0.35.4; contract 0.35.1 (UNCHANGED).

**Origin.** v0.97.3 cloud-sync-parity shipped and today's accuris morning briefing was the first clean fire in 4 cycles — structural contract held (correct path, correct frontmatter, all callouts, correct sidecar schema). But multi-lens review identified three blockers + two high-severity gaps the deterministic write-helper currently CANNOT catch because they're prose-level invariants the LLM owns:

1. **Rating callout missing.** Sidecar said `surfaced_kinds_for_rating.length=3` + `learned_weights` initialized — Rail L should have emitted `> [!todo]+ Was today useful?` with the `cowork:rating-block` sentinel. Body had no rating block. Without the sentinel, next-day reconciler can't parse prior_rating_state — Rail L is dead for that engagement.
2. **Anti-echo callout missing despite sidecar saying it applied.** Sidecar `render_aspects_applied: [anti_echo:include]` but body had no `> [!question] Outside yesterday's frame`. Sidecar and body contradicted each other.
3. **Priority loop dispatched only 3 of 5 kinds.** Accuris's priorities `[chat, ado, github, calendar, email]`; body had callouts for chat/calendar/email only. ado + github silently skipped. GitHub deployment-approval notifications leaked into the Email callout.
4. **Chat microscope `## Output shape` ignored** — chat rendered as flat reverse-chronological list instead of Utilization snapshot + urgency tiers (REPLY OWED - DIRECT / GROUP / TIME-SENSITIVE / FYI).
5. **Calendar used bullets instead of mandated chronological table.**

**Migration.** Architectural shift: where v0.97.3 made the SHAPE of the brief contractually correct, v0.97.4 makes the BEHAVIOR contractually correct.

**Three new write-guards in `write-atomic-note-helper.js`** (guard order: missing-input → rating → anti-echo → coverage-gap → schema → write):

1. **Rating-callout sentinel.** Gate: `surfaced_kinds_for_rating.length>0 && learning_enabled !== false`. Body MUST contain `<!-- cowork:rating-block schema=`. On miss → `failed:contract-violation:missing-rating-callout`; no file written.
2. **Anti-echo callout.** Gate: `sidecar.render_aspects_applied.includes("anti_echo:include")`. Body MUST contain `Outside yesterday's frame`. On miss → `failed:contract-violation:missing-anti-echo-callout`; no file written. Sidecar-driven — no new param needed.
3. **Coverage-gap inject + sidecar mirror.** Gate: `expected_kinds.length > surfaced_kinds.length`. Injects `> [!warning]+ Coverage gap` callout BEFORE the first per-kind block AND mirrors `coverage_gap: { expected, surfaced, skipped }` into the sidecar. NON-failing — some kinds legitimately have no data — but the gap is now VISIBLE in the rendered brief AND machine-readable for `reconcile-cowork`'s cross-day monitoring.

**6 sidecar schemas** (morning-briefing / midday-tripwire / eod-review / weekly-review / monthly-review / morning-briefing-cold) grow an optional `coverage_gap` property: `{ expected: string[], surfaced: string[], skipped: string[] }`.

**`gather-from-served-by/SKILL.md` inline output-shape contract.** New section `## Microscope output-shape contract (INLINE — do not delegate to a separate read)` lists the FOUR mandatory structural elements verbatim:

- Utilization snapshot 1-line lead-in (NOT a generic "Recent activity:" placeholder)
- Urgency-tiered subsections with bold subheads (REPLY OWED - DIRECT / GROUP / TIME-SENSITIVE / FYI for chat; REPLY OWED / TIME-SENSITIVE / FYI for email; MINE - OPEN / BLOCKED / REVIEW REQUESTED / FYI for ado/github)
- Inner-circle hits wrapped as `**[[Person Basename]]**`
- Calendar: MARKDOWN TABLE (Time / Event / Organizer / Status), NOT bullets

Plus a verbal-commitment line directing the LLM to READ the microscope file in full BEFORE composing AND echo back the section headers as a Notice. Plus a pre-render self-check checklist (4 points). Plus a `do NOT improvise a flat list` fallback prohibition for the missing-microscope case.

**5 orchestrator-instructions** (morning-briefing / midday-tripwire / eod-review / weekly-review / monthly-review) update Step 8.5 with v0.97.4 guidance documenting the three new `writeAtomicNote` params (`surfaced_kinds_for_rating`, `learning_enabled`, `expected_kinds`).

**plan-dispatch investigation.** HC-V0974-PD-1..5 prove the helper contract is already correct: 5-priority engagements with `custom_kind: true` + UUID-shaped `served_by` + warn-on-unreachable all produce 5-entry `dispatch_plan`. The accuris fire's "only 3 of 5 priorities surfaced" was an LLM-side priority-loop skip, NOT a planDispatch filtering bug. The coverage-gap write-guard now makes that exact class of failure VISIBLE in the brief.

**23 new HC sub-asserts** (HC-V0974-WG-1..12 write-guards + HC-V0974-PD-1..5 plan-dispatch contract + HC-V0974-GS-1..6 gather-from-served-by inline contract).

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2290 / 0** (+23); cowork-smoke **954 / 0**; claude-surface **214 / 0**; integration-smoke **36 / 0**; cli **132 / 0**; bootstrap **85 / 0**. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.97.4` ALL GREEN.

**Action required post-deploy.** `sauce update --bump-pins` per consumer vault. Cloud-sync prompt UNCHANGED from v0.97.3 — source files now produce a stricter wrapper (rating/anti-echo/coverage-gap guidance flows through Steps 4/6/8.5; gather-from-served-by inline contract loaded on every priority-loop iteration); sync mechanics unchanged.

**Lessons.** (1) Deterministic backstops must cover both shape AND behavior — v0.97.3's regex/sidecar-schema backstops caught structural drift but couldn't see prose-level invariants. v0.97.4 closes that gap by letting writeAtomicNote ITSELF refuse the write when an invariant is violated. (2) Visibility beats silent dropping — the coverage-gap guard does NOT fail the write (some kinds legitimately have no data) but makes the gap LOUD in the rendered brief AND machine-readable in the sidecar. (3) Inline the contract when the LLM is under wrapper-load — same fix pattern as v0.97.2's inline write contract. (4) HC tests as forensic instruments — HC-V0974-PD-1..5 were written to prove or disprove the planDispatch filtering bug; they proved the helper is correct and redirected the cycle to the right fix.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-10-v0.97.4-prose-invariant-write-guards-{design,plan,result}.md`.

### v0.97.3 — cloud-sync-parity (2026-06-10 close)

**Codename:** `cloud-sync-parity`. Workshop 0.97.2 → 0.97.3; cowork 0.35.2 → 0.35.3; contract 0.35.1 (UNCHANGED).

**Origin.** v0.97.2 added an `ANTI-DELEGATION (NON-NEGOTIABLE)` directive and a fire-time `PRELUDE` block via `compose-scheduled-job-wrappers-helper.js` literal strings — prepended at compose time inside the JS function. This worked when the sync ran locally via the sauce CLI: Node executes the helper, the directive lands in every composed wrapper. But when the sync ran inside claude.ai's Cowork UI (cloud-side), there was no JS runtime — the cloud LLM read `orchestrator-instructions/*.md` + `_shared-clauses.md` from the vault directly. Today the cloud guardrail-verify step searched for `ANTI-DELEGATION (NON-NEGOTIABLE)` in the vault sources, found it nowhere, and correctly aborted the push (since from its perspective the wrappers were missing v0.97.2's guardrail).

**Fix.** Move the three load-bearing blocks OUT of JS literals and INTO `_shared-clauses.md` as three new clause heading blocks: `## anti_delegation_clause`, `## prelude_block`, `## done_block`. The PRELUDE was reparameterized from hardcoded `America/Denver` to the `{{$timezone}}` token so each engagement's timezone resolves correctly. The DONE block was parameterized via `{{$cadence}}` + `{{$engagement_label}}` + `{{$workshop_version}}` + `{{$cowork_version}}` + `{{$contract_version}}`. All six orchestrator-instructions cadence files (morning-briefing/midday-tripwire/eod-review/weekly-review/monthly-review/reconcile-cowork) now reference all three clauses at top + bottom via `{{shared.*}}`.

**JS composer simplified.** `composeFromOrchestratorInstructions(...)` no longer prepends ANTI-DELEGATION text or appends DONE text. The wrap-with-PRELUDE-and-DONE block at the bottom of the function is stripped; the function returns `_substituteStaticTokens(template, tokens)` directly. The composer is now a pure substituter — same as the cloud LLM's substituter, modulo runtime.

**Parity invariant.** Both rails (local Node, cloud LLM) now resolve the same vault sources and produce byte-identical composed wrappers. Same `{{shared.*}}` substitutions, same `{{$<token>}}` substitutions, same per-cadence body. The cloud LLM no longer needs special "prepend the directive" knowledge — it just reads the vault sources and substitutes.

**Tests.** 11 new HC-V0973-CSP sub-asserts:

- **CSP-1..6** (6) — each cadence file contains `{{shared.anti_delegation_clause}}` + `{{shared.prelude_block}}` + `{{shared.done_block}}` references.
- **CSP-7..9** (3) — `_shared-clauses.md` defines `## anti_delegation_clause` / `## prelude_block` / `## done_block` heading blocks.
- **CSP-10..11** (2) — `compose-scheduled-job-wrappers-helper.js` source no longer contains `ANTI-DELEGATION (NON-NEGOTIABLE)` or `PRELUDE — fire-time setup` as literals.

Pre-existing HC-V0972-AD-1..7 + HC-V0972-DP-1..4 stayed GREEN — the strings are still in the composed output, just sourced from `_shared-clauses.md` now.

**Final harness.** helper-cases **2267 / 0** (+11); cowork-smoke **954 / 0**; claude-surface **214 / 0**; integration-smoke **36 / 0**; cli **132 / 0**; bootstrap **85 / 0**. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.97.3` ALL GREEN.

**Files touched.** `platform/blueprints/cowork/content/data/orchestrator-instructions/_shared-clauses.md` (3 new clauses); 6 cadence files (top + bottom refs); `platform/blueprints/cowork/helpers/compose-scheduled-job-wrappers-helper.js` (strip prepending/appending logic); `platform/test/run-helper-cases.js` (+11 new HC); plus standard cycle-bump version pins.

**Action required post-deploy.** Run `sauce update --bump-pins` per consumer vault (materializes v0.97.3 `_shared-clauses.md` + cadence files into `spice/cowork/data/orchestrator-instructions/`); THEN run `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` from claude.ai's Cowork UI — cloud LLM now finds all three guardrail substrings in vault sources, passes verify, pushes new wrappers to live scheduled tasks via `update_scheduled_task` MCP. Schedule preservation invariant holds.

**Lessons.** (1) Source of truth must be readable by every rail; JS-only guardrails fail silently in non-Node execution paths. (2) Cloud sync's guardrail-verify is the right safety net — it correctly aborted today rather than pushing a wrapper without the v0.97.2 directive. (3) The cloud LLM is a capable token-substituter; once text lives in markdown, `{{shared.*}}` and `{{$<token>}}` substitute reliably with no special handling.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-10-v0.97.3-cloud-sync-parity-{design,plan,result}.md`.

### v0.97.2 — wrapper-delegation-proof (2026-06-10 close)

**Codename:** `wrapper-delegation-proof`. Workshop 0.97.1 → 0.97.2; cowork 0.35.1 → 0.35.2; contract 0.35.1 (UNCHANGED).

**Origin:** Accuris's 2026-06-10 morning fire produced structurally-wrong output (wrong path `Tuesday-2026-06-10.md` instead of `2026-06-10/morning-briefing.md`; copied verbatim "Tuesday" from PRELUDE example value on a Wednesday; wrong frontmatter type; missing callout structure; fabricated sidecar with `cadence: "morning"` instead of `type: cowork-morning-briefing`). Headspace's morning fire at the same fire-time with the same wrapper was structurally perfect. Root cause: accuris's cron LLM delegated to a Task() subagent which then improvised everything. Same input → wrong output because nothing in the wrapper PROHIBITED delegation.

**Three knobs:**
- **Anti-delegation directive** prepended as the first non-blank paragraph of every composed wrapper, ahead of the PRELUDE. Bans Task()/Agent()/subagent invocations + summarize-and-hand-off pattern. Coverage: all 6 cadences. Lives in `compose-scheduled-job-wrappers-helper.js` (~line 593).
- **Inline write contract** — replaces Step 7's `READ <vault>/.claude/skills/cowork/skills/write-run-note-<cadence>/SKILL.md ... follow its Steps section` (delegation pattern) in ALL 5 atomic-note orchestrator-instructions files (morning-briefing, midday-tripwire, eod-review, weekly-review, monthly-review) with a literal 5-substep inline contract: 7a path / 7b frontmatter YAML / 7c DataviewJS / 7d callout body skeleton / 7e obsidian_put_content invocation. Path now a literal `spice/cowork/daily/{{$today_dirpath}}/{{$cadence}}.md`. Frontmatter shape carries explicit FORBIDDEN-keys list (`cadence`, `date`, `engagement`, `generated_at`, `week`, `month`, `year`, `schema_version`).
- **PRELUDE date fix** — replaces example-based PRELUDE (`e.g. "Tuesday"`, `e.g. "June"` — which accuris's LLM literally copied into the title on a Wednesday) with a Bash-command-driven PRELUDE: `TZ='{{$timezone}}' date '+%A'` etc. + verbatim `do NOT use any example value` instruction. Lives in helper (~line 594-601).

**Why contract_version stays at 0.35.1:** Per cycle prompt instruction. The contract shape itself (`cadence_order`, `substitution_tokens`, `shared_clauses`, per-cadence schema) didn't change — only the per-cadence orchestrator-instructions bodies + helper PRELUDE. Landmine #20 lockstep applies when contract DATA changes; byte-identical contract data doesn't require a bump.

**Files touched:** 6 implementation files (helper + 5 orchestrator-instructions) + 3 test files (run-helper-cases + run-cowork-smoke + run-bootstrap pin sweeps and reroutes) + 4 version/manifest files (package.json, platform/manifest.json, cowork/manifest.json, ranch/platform-subscription.json).

**HC sub-asserts added:** 26 (HC-V0972-AD-1..7 + HC-V0972-IW-1..15 + HC-V0972-DP-1..4). 5+1 pre-existing legacy delegation-pattern asserts rerouted (HC-V0920-ORCH-{MB,MT,EOD,WR,MR}-A4 + S6 + v065-S3 in run-cowork-smoke).

**Action required post-deploy:** run `/cowork sync-scheduled-jobs` once from claude.ai's Cowork UI **PER VAULT** (headspace + accuris). Rail A pushes new wrapper bodies (anti-delegation + inline contract + new PRELUDE) into the live 11 cadence tasks per vault. Schedule preservation invariant holds. Headspace's accidentally-correct fire today doesn't mean it's delegation-proof — directive needs to land in both vaults because the bug is non-deterministic.

**Final harness:** helper-cases **2256 / 0** (+26); cowork-smoke **954 / 0**; claude-surface **214 / 0**; integration-smoke **36 / 0**; cli **132 / 0**; bootstrap **85 / 0**. Preflight `version-sync ok: 0.97.2` ALL GREEN.

**Parked for v0.98.0+:** v0.97.1 launchd grace removal; full retirement of `write-run-note-<cadence>` SKILL.md files (orchestrator-instructions stopped delegating to them — could retire fully or keep as legacy manual paths); per-item L2R + Thompson sampling, memory salience/decay, per-skill SemVer, v0.95.1-migrator.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-10-v0.97.2-wrapper-delegation-proof-{design,plan,result}.md`.

## v0.96.2 — daily-dashboard-tskeys-hotfix (2026-06-09 close)

**Codename:** `daily-dashboard-tskeys-hotfix`. Workshop 0.96.1 → 0.96.2; daily 0.13.5 → 0.13.6. Cowork blueprint + contract UNCHANGED at 0.34.1 (landmine #20 only pins scheduled-job-contract to cowork, not daily).

**Fix shipped:**
- `SpaceDailyDashboard`'s `tsKeys` arg passed to `ActivityFeed.render` now includes `"day"` as the FIRST element, so cowork atomic notes are bucketed by their semantic `day:` frontmatter field (canonical day-of-action) before falling back to wall-clock `created_at` / `status_changed_at` timestamps.
- Local `inWindow` predicate inside `_getActivityCount` rewritten to consult `day` → `created_at` → `status_changed_at` in order. Key semantic decision: when `day` is PRESENT, it is fully authoritative — no OR-fallback to `created_at` / `status_changed_at`. Without this, a page would double-surface on both its canonical day (per `day:`) AND on the wall-clock day that `created_at` landed on. The OR-fallback path remains for pages with no `day:` set (legacy + non-cowork blueprints).

**Closes:** timestamp-drift class affecting any cowork cron whose `created_at` rolls past midnight. Diagnosed via Workflow run wzr3g3uad — Monday 2026-06-08 EOD note stamped `created_at: 2026-06-09T04:10:...` (cron fired 4am Tuesday) dropped from Monday daily-note Activity panel.

**HC added:** 3 sub-asserts (HC-V0962-DASH-1..3) — tsKeys ordering (DASH-1), `day:` YYYY-MM-DD parse on Monday window (DASH-2), no-double-surface invariant when `day` takes precedence over a later `created_at` (DASH-3).

**Open carry-forward to v0.97:** the activity-feed mechanism's own `inWindow` predicate (`platform/mechanisms/activity-feed/activity-feed.js` line ~349) still uses pure OR-semantics across all tsKeys — at render time it may still double-surface a page on both Monday and Tuesday in the cron-overrun scenario, because the dashboard pre-count fix only gates the `hasContent` boolean. Authoritative-day semantics in activity-feed itself is a follow-up. Two other findings from the same Workflow run (sidecars-not-written + skill-not-loaded) also remain open for v0.97.

**Verification:** Tomorrow (2026-06-09), the Monday EOD note (after the inline created_at hotfix on the consumer vault) appears on Monday's daily; will NOT double-surface on Tuesday's daily because `day:` takes precedence in the dashboard pre-count.

**Harness:** helper-cases 2150 → 2153 (3 new sub-asserts, GREEN). cowork-smoke 952/0; claude-surface 213/0; integration-smoke 36/0; activity-feed 120/0; bootstrap 85/0.

## v0.95.1 (closed 2026-06-08) — cowork-anti-echo MINOR

Workshop 0.95.0 → **0.95.1** (MINOR). cowork blueprint 0.32.0 → **0.33.0** (MINOR). scheduled-job-contract `contract_version` 0.32.0 → **0.33.0** (lockstep mirror, per v0.93.2 landmine #20 convention). Mechanism count unchanged at 17.

**Codename: cowork-anti-echo.** Three composable knobs breaking the cowork memory echo loop — the v0.84.0 memory layer caused gather output to converge on yesterday's carry-forward bullets, suppressing genuinely-new items. v0.95.1 ships three independent levers, each opt-in everywhere (Approach B), bundled into one MINOR cycle.

**Knob 1 (anti_echo render aspect).** NEW `render_aspects.anti_echo` engagement-type default `"skip"` (opt-in `"include"`). When set to `"include"` for a given engagement: plan-dispatch invokes NEW `dispatch-plan-helper.js` export `deriveExcludedThemes(yesterdayMemory)` → returns yesterday's carry-forward bullet strings verbatim (no semantic extraction). NEW `compose-body-helper.js` export `injectAntiEchoCallout(body, excluded_themes, voice_contract)` + module-private const `ANTI_ECHO_ELIGIBLE_CADENCES = ['morning-briefing', 'midday-tripwire', 'eod-review']`. `composeBody` wires the eligibility guard + injection. Per-fire `[!question] Outside yesterday's frame` callout names ONE item from today's dispatch that doesn't relate to yesterday's bullets (LLM-fill placeholder resolves at runtime). Plan-dispatch **contract grows 12 keys → 13 keys** (NEW `excluded_themes: string[]` always-present, empty when not opted in). 3 orchestrator SKILL.md (MB / midday / EOD) wire `plan.excluded_themes` through to compose-body.

**Knob 2 (capture-frame-drift tripwire).** NEW sub-skill `cowork:capture-frame-drift` at `platform/blueprints/cowork/skills/skills/capture-frame-drift/SKILL.md` — background tripwire invoked post-synthesize-day, gated by `plan.tripwire_aspects.includes("frame_drift")`. Requires ≥5 days of memory.md syntheses (fast-path skip if fewer). NEW `capture-frame-drift-helper.js` with 3 exports: `extractThemes(syntheses)` async LLM call to `claude-haiku-4-5` returning ThemeBundle[] of `{day, themes: string[], top_subject: string}`; `evaluateDrift({themes, atomicNotes})` deterministic 3-flag DriftReport (`frame_repeat ≥4/5 days`, `subject_dominance ≥3/5 days`, `explicit_null 3 consecutive "today's gather largely continued yesterday's threads" lines`); `composeDriftCallout(report, voice_contract)` returns voice-contract-applied markdown callout body. On any flag firing, sub-skill appends `[!warning]- Frame may be stuck` callout to today's memory.md (between synthesis section and `## Ticks`) + frontmatter additions `frame_drift_flagged: true` + `frame_drift_at: <ISO>`. LLM failure → emits `warnings:` frontmatter entry; doesn't crash. read-memory sub-skill grows return shape from 3 keys to 4 keys (adds `drift_warning: string | null`). morning-briefing Yesterday-at-a-glance step injects drift_warning when non-null so the LLM composing today's MB is aware that recent days have been thematically locked. Estimated cost: ~$0.50/year/engagement at daily firing.

**Knob 3 (lens_shift cadence).** NEW cadence added to engagement-types `supported_cadences` (NOT `default_cadences` — opt-in everywhere). Default cron `0 7 * * 6` (weekly Saturday 7am cold-mode MB). morning-briefing orchestrator gains `--cadence lens_shift` branch that skips pre-flight 3a (read-memory) + 3a.5 (gather-semantic-related) — produces a fully cold MB with NO "Yesterday at a glance" callout (no memory), NO anti-echo callout (excluded_themes empty), NO semantic-echoes callout (3a.5 skipped). NEW atomic-note type `cowork-morning-briefing-cold` + rule_fragment + slug pattern `morning-briefing-cold-<engagement>.md`. scheduled-job-contract.json gains `lens_shift` cadence entry with prompt template; `contract_version` 0.32.0 → 0.33.0 (lockstep mirror per landmine #20). `compose-scheduled-job-wrappers-helper.js` emits 6th wrapper block when engagement opts in (5 → 6); existing 5-block emit preserved when not opted in. NEW CustomJS class `CoworkLensShiftCards` renders Daily Hub side-by-side warm + cold pair via slug-match (no `companion_to:` frontmatter — avoids race conditions); pair vs single rendering handles both-exist, only-warm, only-cold modes. activity-feed type-filtering allowlist registers `cowork-morning-briefing-cold` (S0.5 verified ENUMERATED mode + registered).

**Engagement-type updates.** 3 engagement-type JSONs (personal / w2-fte / consulting) gain `render_aspects.anti_echo: "skip"` default + `supported_cadences += ["lens_shift"]`. cowork:bootstrap-vault interview grows 3 new opt-in questions (anti-echo per engagement, frame-drift tripwire per engagement, lens_shift cadence per engagement).

**Migrator parking.** v0.95.1 slot was originally allocated to a migrator (`sauce update --migrate-config` dropping the one-cycle backward-compat reads for pre-v0.95.0 vault-config.md). Re-prioritized to anti-echo 2026-06-08. Migrator design + plan PARKED at v0.96.0+ (S0.6 parking memos added to both docs; 4 prior commits preserved; execution-ready when migrator resurrects).

**Harness deltas.** +39 new HC-V0951-* sub-asserts (helper-cases 2037 → 2076):
- HC-V0951-K1-A1..K1E (7 sub-asserts): deriveExcludedThemes happy/null/missing paths + plan-dispatch contract excluded_themes key on/off + compose-body callout injection on/off + cadence-eligibility guard + canonical regex match.
- HC-V0951-K2-A..K2K (11 sub-asserts): sub-skill at claude_surface + tripwire_aspects gating + <5-syntheses skip + extractThemes JSON validity + frame_repeat/subject_dominance/explicit_null flag evaluation + composeDriftCallout canonical shape + synthesize-day post-step write to memory.md + read-memory drift_warning extraction + MB drift_warning injection.
- HC-V0951-K3-A..K3L (12 sub-asserts): `--cadence lens_shift` skips 3a + 3a.5 + cold-mode write contract (type/slug/body exclusions) + scheduled-job-contract entry + contract_version bump + wrapper-helper 6th block emit/skip + CoworkLensShiftCards pair vs single rendering.
- HC-V0951-X (cross-cutting): bootstrap-vault interview cases.
- +3 cowork-smoke (924 → 927); +1 claude-surface (210 → 211) — new sub-skill + new CustomJS class claude_surface entries.

**Per-cycle VERSION pin sweep (landmine #16).** 24 hardcoded `"0.95.0"`/`"0.32.0"` assertions in run-helper-cases.js bumped to `"0.95.1"`/`"0.33.0"`; 12 hardcoded `workshop_version: "0.95.0"` in run-bootstrap.js bumped; 1 cowork manifest.version assertion in run-cowork-smoke.js bumped (V0750-VERSION). ranch/platform-subscription.json lockstep workshop_version + cowork pin bumped (v0.93.3 lesson 5.4). scheduled-job-contract.json contract_version bumped during S3.2 (already at 0.33.0 by S5).

**Dogfood.** `./install.sh` self-install clean. Workshop installed surface reflects v0.95.1: capture-frame-drift SKILL.md materialized at `.claude/skills/cowork/skills/capture-frame-drift/SKILL.md`; CoworkLensShiftCards CustomJS class registered; engagement-types personal/w2-fte/consulting JSON deployed with `anti_echo: "skip"` + `lens_shift` in `supported_cadences`; ranch reflects workshop_version 0.95.1 + cowork 0.33.0 lockstep.

**Commits (S0–S5):** `1608d00` (S0 baseline), `dedb065` (S0.5 activity-feed ENUMERATED + registered), `f87b0f9` (S0.6 migrator parking memos + brainstorm-state crosslink), `2973059` (S1.1 K1 TDD-red), `82b208b` (S1.2 helper additions), `6f081a7` (S1.3 plan-dispatch 13th key), `aff223b` (S1.4 orchestrator wire-throughs + engagement-type defaults), `01298b6` (S2.1 K2 TDD-red), `06f4fe4` (S2.2 capture-frame-drift sub-skill + helper), `6924312` (S2.3 read-memory drift_warning extraction), `20724a6` (S2.4 MB drift_warning injection), `4f61527` (S3.1 K3 TDD-red), `d6fd86d` (S3.2 lens_shift cadence + MB cold-mode), `d07351b` (S3.3 wrapper helper 6th block + CoworkLensShiftCards), `baad378` (S3.4 Daily Hub Lens-shift companions section + smoke goldens), `a76e572` (S4 bootstrap-vault interview + cross-cutting GREEN), S5 this commit.

**Lessons (5 NEW):**
1. **Bundled-MINOR with 3 distinct knobs fits comfortably in 17 stages** when stages are tight + sub-asserts are reused. The Knob 1 / Knob 2 / Knob 3 parallel structure (each: RED → helper → contract wiring → orchestrator wire-through) becomes a reliable template for future bundled-MINOR cycles.
2. **LLM theme extraction (claude-haiku-4-5) costs are negligible at daily firing** — ~$0.50/year/engagement. Don't optimize prematurely; the deterministic-helper layer (`evaluateDrift`) is the hot path. The LLM call is the cold path (5 syntheses → 1 call → cache).
3. **Visual-pairing via slug-match (no `companion_to:` frontmatter) avoids race conditions cleanly.** Warm + cold MB write independently; CoworkLensShiftCards scans for both by slug pattern. No coordination required between the two cadences.
4. **activity-feed type-discovery mode matters — verify in S0.5 BEFORE writing RED tests.** v0.95.1 found ENUMERATED mode + required adding the new type to the allowlist; if the mode had been PATTERN-MATCH, the S0.5 step would have been a no-op. Codifying this gate as Step 0.5 (not buried inside S3) saved test-time surprise.
5. **excluded_themes as 13th contract key, not embedded in render_aspects.** Initial design option was to attach the bullets list to `render_aspects.anti_echo` itself (struct-shape). Final design (top-level `excluded_themes: string[]`) is cleaner: composability with future tripwires (Knob 2's drift_warning) that ALSO need yesterday-memory access without re-coupling to render_aspects.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-08-v0.95.1-anti-echo-{design,plan}.md`.

## v0.95.0 (closed 2026-06-07) — cowork-spine MINOR

Workshop 0.94.0 → **0.95.0** (MINOR). Bundles skill structural cohesion sweep + knob ergonomics refactor into one workshop MINOR cycle. cowork blueprint 0.31.2 → **0.32.0** (MINOR). Mechanism count unchanged at 17.

**Cohesion sweep.** 5 atomic-note orchestrator SKILL.md files (morning-briefing / midday-tripwire / eod-review / weekly-review / monthly-review) carried byte-identical copies of dispatch-plan pseudocode through v0.94.x (~100 lines per file). v0.95.0 extracts the load-bearing pre-flight surface into a NEW `cowork:plan-dispatch` sub-skill at `platform/blueprints/cowork/skills/skills/plan-dispatch/SKILL.md`. The sub-skill wraps the pre-existing `dispatch-plan-helper.js` PLUS 3 NEW additive exports (`composeFinalPreferences` + `readEngagement` + `loadKindTitles`); returns a 12-key contract that orchestrators consume as their single source of truth for Gather + Write. 5 orchestrators slim by collapsing pre-flight steps 3b read-prefs + 3c dispatch + 3d microscopes + 3e inner-circle (~120 lines) into one `READ cowork:plan-dispatch` step (~20 lines). Net **−587 lines workshop-wide** (1685 → 1098 across the 5 orchestrators). morning-briefing's semantic-echoes step renamed 3b → 3a.5 (sub-step of memory phase); other 4 cadences can opt in via `engagement.overrides.render_aspects.semantic_related`.

**Knob ergonomics.** NEW `engagement.overrides` schema on vault-config.md engagements[] introduces a layered preferences tree: `bundle (engagement-type JSON defaults) ⨁ overrides (per-engagement) ⨁ ad_hoc_prefs (reserved) → final`. Composition rules: objects (render_aspects, cadence_order, voice) merge per-key with override-wins; arrays (tripwire_aspects, cadence_order entries) REPLACE bundle values when overrides present; microscopes_registry: ad_hoc || overrides || bundle || null. Engagement-types become NAMED KNOB BUNDLES, not runtime predicates — gather/write skills no longer branch on `engagement.type` directly. Backward-compat reads of pre-v0.95.0 vault-config.md preserved for one cycle (records without `overrides:` return bundle defaults verbatim); v0.95.1 ships `sauce update --migrate-config` + drops back-compat reads.

**12-key plan-dispatch contract.** `{ dispatch_plan, voice_contract, microscopes, siblings, allowlist, render_aspects, cadence_order, tripwire_aspects, kind_titles, effective_hard_rules, dispatch_mode, prefs_status }`. Mid-execution scope expansion from design's 10-key contract: `effective_hard_rules` added during S1.5.1 (needed by gather-from-served-by independently of voice_contract formatted string); `tripwire_aspects` added during S1.5.2 (needed by midday-tripwire's early-exit + per-aspect dispatch). Both surfaced explicitly in commits + sub-skill SKILL.md (per v0.93.3 lesson 5.6).

**Kind-titles extraction.** NEW `platform/blueprints/cowork/data/kind-titles.json` v1.0.0 with 7-entry canonical map (calendar=Today's calendar, email=Email triage, chat=Chat, finance=Finance, github=GitHub, ado=ADO, monitoring=Monitoring). Materialized via files[] with `tag: "platform"` into `spice/cowork/data/kind-titles.json`. Module-private `CANONICAL_TITLES` const (4 entries) stays as fallback when data file is missing or carries an incompatible version. Per-kind microscope `## Output shape` directives can override the data-file value per-engagement.

**Canonical orchestrator template.** NEW `Docs/agent-guides/cowork-orchestrator-template.md` v1.0.0 captures the structural contract every cowork atomic-note orchestrator must conform to: required H1 + `[!warning]+ CRITICAL` output-path callout, required section order (Inputs / Pre-flight / Gather / [Decide] / Write / Verify / [State] / Done / Harness testing), required Pre-flight steps (1, 1b, 2, 3, 3a, [3a.5], 3b, 4), the canonical step-3b plan-dispatch invocation contract, required body-shape markers, failure-token vocabulary, and what lives INSIDE plan-dispatch (NOT in the orchestrator). HC-V0950-COHESION-A1..A5 cohesion-automation assertions queued for v0.96.0+ (cowork:audit-cohesion candidate).

**Harness deltas.** +15 NEW HC-V0950-* sub-asserts (OVERRIDES A1..A4 = 4 covering composeFinalPreferences composition; PLAN-DISPATCH A1..A6 = 6 covering 5-key shell + render_aspects/cadence_order/voice/tripwire_aspects overrides + w2-fte bundle defaults; PLAN-DISPATCH B1..B3 = 3 covering loadKindTitles fallback/data-file/stale-version; PLAN-DISPATCH C1..C2 = 2 covering decideDispatchMode legacy fallback + composeFinalPreferences null-input defensive). ~36 pre-existing assertions across HC-V0800/V0820/V0870/V0891/V0900/V0913 re-routed through plan-dispatch (cohesion-aware corpus reads — assertions concat orchestrator body + plan-dispatch body OR accept dual patterns plan.X vs prefs.X). Final smoke **2037 / 0** (run-helper-cases); cowork-smoke **924 / 0**; claude-surface **210 / 0**; ALL 6 suites GREEN; preflight `version-sync ok: 0.95.0`.

**Per-cycle VERSION pin sweep (lesson 5.3).** 22 hardcoded `"0.94.0"` assertion values bumped to `"0.95.0"` across HC-V0891/V0900/V0920/V0930/V0931-VERSION-* blocks in run-helper-cases.js + run-bootstrap.js. 14 hardcoded `"0.31.2"` cowork-pin assertions in run-helper-cases.js bumped to `"0.32.0"`; 2 in run-cowork-smoke.js bumped. scheduled-job-contract.json contract_version 0.31.2 → 0.32.0 (per landmine #20 contract-mirror convention). 8 fixture `input.json` files: cowork_version 0.31.2 → 0.32.0 + sauce_version 0.94.0 → 0.95.0. 8 fixture `expected-file.md` goldens regen'd (contract_version + section_contract_version + warning-token + provenance line).

**Dogfood.** `node platform/install.js --vault . --auto-approve` clean exit 0 with 82 new history entries. Workshop installed surface reflects v0.95.0: plan-dispatch SKILL.md materialized at `.claude/skills/cowork/skills/plan-dispatch/SKILL.md`; kind-titles.json materialized at `spice/cowork/data/kind-titles.json` v1.0.0; morning-briefing slimmed to 237 lines in installed surface; ranch reflects workshop_version 0.95.0 + cowork 0.32.0 lockstep.

**Commits (S0–S3):** `9bcd726` (S0 baseline), `7c0c97b` (S1.1 TDD-red HC-V0950), `b548021` (S1.2 data/kind-titles.json), `d8f9c6a` (S1.3 helper exports), `29f4a8e` (S1.4 plan-dispatch sub-skill), `3ebd638` (S1.5.1 morning-briefing), `295d1b7` (S1.5.2 midday-tripwire + 12th contract key), `2b8a274` (S1.5.3 eod-review), `5dbbd87` (S1.5.4 weekly-review), `8a1220d` (S1.5.5 monthly-review), `469fc22` (S1.5 sweep — re-route HC-V0800/V0820/V0870/V0891/V0900/V0913 assertions), `a701a2e` (S1.6 orchestrator template), `d8f6eb0` (S1.7 version sweep + ranch lockstep), `5b7db1e` (S2 dogfood), S3 this commit.

**Lessons (6 NEW):**
1. Sub-skill contract evolves under refactor pressure — 10-key → 12-key during S1.5 as orchestrators consumed the result tree. Reserve a slot or two for "I forgot one" expansions during design.
2. Defense-in-depth surfaces resist slimming — verify/write-guard/verbal-commitment prose stayed load-bearing. 1685 → 1098 (−587 lines) is a net win but the absolute per-orchestrator line targets in design docs should account for the defense-in-depth floor.
3. Test-quality drift can mask cohesion regressions — S1.5 surfaced 41 pre-existing test failures asserting against load-bearing prose that the refactor legitimately MOVED. Right fix: re-route assertions to read the new structural owner, not preserve stale prose.
4. Phantom plan steps signal stale assumptions — plan's S1.4 Step 4 referenced `Scripts/regenerate-claude-surface-registry.js` (doesn't exist; registry regen happens inside platform/install.js Templater context). Verify scripts exist before authoring plan steps.
5. Mid-execution scope expansion needs explicit surfacing — 12th contract key added during S1.5.2; documented in commit + sub-skill SKILL.md per v0.93.3 lesson 5.6.
6. Workshop dogfood is a useful contract verifier — 82-history-entry install pass caught the manifest entry order + verified data file materialization to the right path. Worth running before every push.

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-07-v0.95.0-cowork-spine-{design,plan,result}.md`.

## v0.94.0 (closed 2026-06-07) — applyExternalPluginInstall MINOR

Workshop 0.93.3 → **0.94.0** (MINOR). Closes the v0.93.3 existing-consumer install gap by adding an install-time companion to `phaseFetchPlugins`. Also adds claudian as a default external plugin under convenience.

**Gap closed.** v0.93.3 shipped `applyExternalPlugins` (warning-only) and `phaseFetchPlugins` (bootstrap-only). Existing consumers running `sauce update` after a v0.93.3 manifest delta saw a Notice + had to install the plugin manually. Headspace-sauce demonstrated this: `new-tab-default-page` declared by convenience 0.3.0 was not auto-installed; user had to use Settings → Community plugins → Browse manually. v0.94.0's new `applyExternalPluginInstall` mirrors `phaseFetchPlugins`'s behavior at install time, with a warn-and-continue posture per plugin so a single fetch failure doesn't brick the wider install.

**Helper shape.** Inline in `platform/install.js`, alongside `applyExternalPlugins`. Imports the three already-standalone `bootstrap-lib/` modules (`fetch-plugin`, `community-plugins-index`, `community-plugins-merge`). Wires at install.js:1071, BEFORE the existing warning helper. Per-plugin failures are caught into a `failed[]` list; failed ids are NOT appended to community-plugins.json (so the downstream `applyExternalPlugins` warning helper retains `required:true` teeth on failed installs); successful and skipped ids ARE appended (mirroring phaseFetchPlugins:161).

**Claudian addition.** convenience 0.3.0 → 0.4.0 adds `{id: "realclaudian"}` to `external_plugins[]`. Plugin id is `realclaudian` (not `claudian`); repo is `YishenTu/claudian`; standard 3-asset release. No `community_plugin_settings[]` entry — claudian's settings (Claude provider, auth, MCP servers) are user-owned.

**Landmine #17 amendment.** Two network gateways now: `bootstrap.js:phaseFetchPlugins` (fail-loud, fresh-vault) and `install.js:applyExternalPluginInstall` (warn-and-continue, existing-vault). Same defense-in-depth via shared `bootstrap-lib/fetch-plugin.js`.

**Versioning.** workshop 0.93.3 → 0.94.0 MINOR; convenience 0.3.0 → 0.4.0 MINOR. New install-time behavior is semver-respectful MINOR.

**HC delta:** +6 cases in run-bootstrap.js (HC-EPI-1..6). HC-EPI-6 exercises the chain end-to-end (lesson 5.2). Per-cycle VERSION pin sweep landed in S1.4 (lesson 5.3).

**Status (post-deploy verification):**
- accuris-sauce: `sauce update --bump-pins` ran clean. Pins bumped. realclaudian auto-installed (or skipped-if-present per current state).
- headspace-sauce: `sauce update --bump-pins` ran clean. **The load-bearing demo**: `.obsidian/plugins/new-tab-default-page/` and `.obsidian/plugins/realclaudian/` both now present; `community-plugins.json` includes both; new-tab-default-page's `data.json` scaffolded by the chain. The v0.93.3 manual-install workflow becomes a historical artifact.

**Commits:** S0 baseline (36bc60b); S1.1 tests RED (b8daa11); S1.2 helper GREEN (7df7073); S1.3 convenience manifest (451d778); S1.4 workshop + package bump + VERSION pin sweep (0a31930); S1.5 landmine #17 (7780133); S2 dogfood + ranch (34d4f59); S2 addendum community-plugins.json dogfood (e40bcb1); S3 cycle-close (b6d1c10).

See `Docs/plans/2026-06-07-v0.94.0-applyExternalPluginInstall-{design,plan,result}.md`.
