BudgetsCards/PaychecksCards/InvoicesCards opt in. **Cycle shape:** S1 (helper + nav-buttons + hub polish) + S2 (4 widgets + template integration + NewXButton lockstep) + **FIVE in-cycle CFs** (CF-1 visual cohesion + Pending sub-section bug fix [latent v0.16.0 dv-shim missing pages]; CF-2 empty pending sections + NewX outlined-accent restyle + cards outline-pill propagation; CF-3 drop redundant H1 from area hub content; CF-4 paycheck-some-paid-as-InProgress + InvoiceControls summary strip + outline-style action buttons + drop entity H1; CF-5 drop ## Notes from all entity templates). SIX in-cycle bumps finance 0.1.4 → 0.2.0 → 0.2.1 → 0.2.2 → 0.2.3 → 0.2.4 → 0.2.5 → 0.2.6. Reserved 0.17.x headroom USED extensively per gotcha 9. All S3 manual smokes user-confirmed PASS. **NEW lessons (v0.17.0):** (a) **dv-shim composition discipline (latent bug class)** — when a hub renderer hands a sub-container to another customJS class, the shim MUST proxy every dv method that sub-class calls (`pages` + `current` + `el` minimum); a `{container: sub}`-only shim breaks any sub-renderer that calls `dv.pages(...)`; CF-1 surfaced as v0.16.0 latent bug masked by empty Pending sections; (b) **`badges[].style: "outline"` on BeaconCards** — cards@0.2.3 PATCH ADDITIVE; default "fill" preserves prior behavior; future status-bearing blueprints can opt in for the polished aesthetic; (c) **outline-accent action button pattern** — repeated across NewBudget/Paycheck/InvoiceButton + 3 widget "+ Add X" buttons + InvoiceControls Save/Submit; mirrors project `_renderActionButton`; candidate for `BeaconButton` mechanism promotion in a future cycle; (d) **`FinanceFrontmatter.update(file, mutator)` API** — generic enough to be a candidate for promotion to a shared mechanism (e.g., `frontmatter-mutator`) once a second blueprint surfaces the same need; (e) **first-of-its-kind UI cycle pulls 5 CFs** — 6-data-point precedent now (v0.5.0/v0.11.0/v0.12.0/v0.6.0/v0.16.0/v0.17.0); plan reserved Y.Z.x headroom upfront; (f) **visual cohesion + UX consistency are CF-heavy work** — three of five CFs were primarily styling; lock-the-pattern up front via design quoting prevents widget-to-widget drift but cannot eliminate "feels off" smoke-time feedback; plan accordingly. Workshop_version 0.16.0 → 0.17.0 MINOR; tag `v0.17.0` annotated and pushed. Helper-cases harness UNCHANGED at 255 (zero installer surface). Renderer harness 9 → 17 cases (+8 FF1-FF8 under new `finance` selector). Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.17.0-finance-widgets-result.md`.

## v0.18.0 BeaconButton mechanism CLOSED 2026-05-05

First new mechanism in many cycles. Promotes the duplicated outline-accent action button pattern (12 call sites across project + finance with subtle drift) into a shared `beacon-button@0.1.0` mechanism. Single class `BeaconButton` with one method `render(parent, opts) → HTMLButtonElement`; opts `{label, icon, onClick, flex?, disabled?, tone?}`; `tone` reserved for future additive tones (only "accent" valid at v0.18.0; silently falls back on unknown). Hover handlers honor `btn.disabled`. **S1 — project migration:** `project-nav-buttons.js` 4 `_renderActionButton` call sites (lines 717/733/737/765) migrated to `customJS.BeaconButton.render(...)`; private `_renderActionButton` method DELETED entirely; project 1.3.2 → 1.3.3 PATCH. **S2 — finance migration:** 8 sites migrated (NewBudget/Paycheck/InvoiceButton + BudgetCategoriesEditor/PaycheckExpensesEditor/InvoiceTimeLogEditor "+ Add X" + InvoiceControls Save+Submit); finance 0.2.6 → 0.2.7 PATCH. **CF-1 (in-cycle, surfaced in Smoke E):** InvoiceControls Save now also recomputes `fm.amount = round(rate * hours, 2)` so rate changes immediately propagate to the summary strip without requiring a Time-Log re-edit; `refreshSaveEnabled` extended to consider amount drift (Save enabled when stored amount != rate × hours, allowing re-sync of stale amounts at the saved rate); misleading "Amount recomputes on next Time-Log edit" caveat note removed. CF-1 root cause was pre-existing v0.16.0/v0.17.0 UX gap (Save only wrote rate; cross-file amount propagation only fired from Time-Log entry mutations). Three in-cycle finance bumps: 0.2.6 → 0.2.7 (S2 atomic) → 0.2.8 (CF-1 first-pass) → 0.2.9 (CF-1 refinement after stale install surfaced gotcha 9 in-cycle re-process bump rule at scale). **Harness deltas:** renderer harness 17 → 23 cases (+6 BB1-BB6 under new `beacon-button` selector; TDD-first per FF1-FF8 / BC1-BC7 / DA1-DA2 precedent); `makeFinanceCustomJsStub` extended with BeaconButton stub (unblocks FF4-FF8 after migration — finance helpers now reach for customJS.BeaconButton at render time). Helper-cases UNCHANGED at 255. Workshop_version 0.17.0 → 0.18.0 MINOR (new mechanism). Tag `v0.18.0` annotated and pushed. **NEW lessons (v0.18.0):** (a) **In-cycle re-process bump rule at scale** — the gotcha 9 pattern (when in-cycle revisions reuse the same version, the installer's `subscribed.version === installed.version` short-circuit skips the new content) hits hard during CF iteration; pattern: any in-cycle revision after first install MUST bump version; the 3-bump stack (S2 atomic → CF-1 first → CF-1 refinement) is the load-bearing example; promotion to landmine #15 is a v0.19.0 candidate. (b) **Mechanism-promotion-from-duplicated-pattern as a reliable cycle shape** — non-UI dedupe cycles are reliably-clean; v0.18.0's only CF was UX-driven (Smoke E) not migration-regression; subagents can be parallelized when migrating independent files (T2.1-T2.7 dispatched in one batch). (c) **Harness stub maintenance** — when a mechanism becomes a dep of existing helpers, the harness stub map needs the new entry at the same time as the first cross-class consumer (added BeaconButton stub to `makeFinanceCustomJsStub` at the moment the finance helpers migrated). (d) **Reserved-headroom-UNUSED precedent extended to 3+ data points** (v0.13.0 + v0.14.0 + v0.18.0) — mechanism promotion + non-UI dedupe cycles reliably clean; visual + UX cycles still pull 2-6 CFs. (e) **Tone palette slot reservation as future-proofing pattern** — reserving an opt slot at API launch (BeaconButton's `tone`) is cheaper than additive opts later; future tones (neutral / danger) become additive without API break. (f) **Cross-file UX coupling discovered late** — even pure dedupe cycles can surface latent UX bugs in the migrated surface (CF-1 was pre-existing v0.16.0/v0.17.0 design gap exposed by Smoke E user testing); plan for one UX-CF when migrating user-visible widgets. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.18.0-beacon-button-result.md`.

## v0.18.1 BeaconButton adoption sweep + nav-button label cleanup CLOSED 2026-05-05

Closes the v0.17.0 + v0.18.0 carry-forward (new-meeting-button parity + workstream Add/Remove migration) plus an in-session-surfaced label cleanup. Single PATCH cycle, single stage S1, 4 surfaces + 1 in-cycle CF. **Migration sites (3):** (a) `new-meeting-button.js` button → `customJS.BeaconButton.render(container, {label, icon, onClick})` (user-plus SVG preserved verbatim; visual change from neutral browser-default to outline-accent — intended per `blueprints i want.md` line 64); (b) `project-workstream-manager.js` `addBtn` → `customJS.BeaconButton.render(btnRow, {label: "Add", icon: plusIcon, onClick: addWorkstream})` with new Lucide plus SVG; (c) `project-workstream-manager.js` `rmBtn` → `customJS.BeaconButton.render(btnRow, {label: "Remove", icon: minusIcon, onClick: removeWorkstream})` with new Lucide minus SVG (`+` / `−` chars dropped from labels — icon renders before label). **Label edits (2):** to-do nav-button `"+ Today's To-Do"` → `"To Do"` (icon unchanged); journal nav-button `"+ Today's Journal"` → `"Journal"` (icon unchanged). **CF-1 (in-cycle, surfaced in Smoke C):** `Today To-Do.md` template's first task line `- [ ] ` (trailing space) → `- [ ]` (no trailing space) — the trailing space caused a blank placeholder task to register as an open task in the daily dashboard's tasks panel queries; trim keeps the line as a usable empty checkbox without making it count. Pre-existing latent defect from v0.4.0 to-do authoring; trim forced an in-cycle re-process bump (to-do 0.1.3 → 0.1.4). **Cycle shape:** S1 (4 surfaces, T1.1 + T1.2 subagent-dispatched in 1 turn each with no rework — first cycle to land both subagent migrations clean; T1.3-T1.5 master-driven manifest mechanics + lockstep; T1.6-T1.7 harness verification + barebones install; T1.8 commit + push) + CF-1 (template body + version bump + lockstep + barebones reinstall + commit + push). NO mechanism changes. NO installer changes. NO schema additions. NO new harness cases (BB1-BB6 cover the BeaconButton contract; pure adoption requires no new cases). Workshop_version 0.18.0 → 0.18.1 PATCH; tag `v0.18.1` annotated and pushed. Helper-cases UNCHANGED at 255. Renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 cases. **NEW lessons (v0.18.1):** (a) **Pure adoption migrations land in single-task subagent dispatches with zero rework** — API contract was QUOTED literally in the design doc (v0.6.0 lesson) → both T1.1 + T1.2 subagent dispatches landed clean first-pass. Pattern reusable for any future BeaconButton adoption. (b) **Trailing-whitespace template defect class** — invisible defects in template bodies can survive multiple cycles undetected; future blueprint authoring should grep for trailing whitespace before merge (`grep -n " $" templates/*.md`); v0.19.0 candidate: extend helper-cases harness to lint blueprint template bodies. (c) **Reserved-headroom-UNUSED precedent extended to 4 data points** (v0.13.0 + v0.14.0 + v0.18.0 + v0.18.1) — pure adoption / pure dedupe / pure label edit cycles reliably land clean; visual + UX cycles still pull 2-6 CFs. (d) **Combined cycles (BeaconButton sweep + label cleanup) are bundle-friendly** — when two unrelated micro-tasks share a release window, single PATCH with single commit + single tag + single harness sweep + single smoke matrix is cheaper than two separate cycles. Decision was made mid-brainstorm when user surfaced the label cleanup as additional scope. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.18.1-beacon-button-adoption-sweep-result.md`.

## v0.18.2 Project Map sub-tasks bug fix CLOSED 2026-05-05

Closes the `blueprints i want.md` line 68-69 carry-forward bug from v0.5.0 project port. Sub-task cards (Task Board Cards under `<projectDir>/tasks/<Task>/board/<Card>`) were leaking into the Project Map's "Unassigned" bucket because the recursive `dv.pages(projectDir)` query in `project-workstreams.js:50` lacked sub-* exclusion. Two new predicates added to the `where(...)` clause: `!p.task_parent` (frontmatter signal — Task Board Card.md + Task Note.md set it; top-level Kanban Card.md does not) AND `!p.file.path.includes("/board/")` (layout signal — sub-task cards land at `<Task>/board/<Card>`). Belt-and-suspenders: either alone closes the bug; together they survive future template authoring slips OR layout reorgs that drop one signal. Single-stage S1, **zero in-cycle CFs** (5th data point in reserved-headroom-UNUSED precedent: v0.13.0 + v0.14.0 + v0.18.0 + v0.18.1 + v0.18.2). Pure master-driven cycle (1-line filter edit + 3 manifest version bumps + harness runs); no subagent dispatches needed because every edit was mechanical and the design quoted both the failing-state query and the fix shape literally. NO mechanism changes. NO installer changes. NO schema additions. NO new harness cases. All 5 manual smokes A-E user-confirmed PASS first-pass (existing project Map sub-tasks vanish from Unassigned + workstream-assigned tasks still render + top-level Unassigned still renders with Move button + Move dialog + assignment still functional + fresh sub-task creation does NOT appear in Map). Workshop_version 0.18.1 → 0.18.2 PATCH; project 1.3.4 → 1.3.5 PATCH; tag `v0.18.2` annotated and pushed. Helper-cases UNCHANGED at 255. Renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 cases. **NEW lessons (v0.18.2):** (a) **Belt-and-suspenders predicate stacking is cheap insurance** — when a filter has multiple independent signals to choose between, stacking 2 of them is zero-cost. Either alone would close the bug; together they survive a future template authoring slip dropping `task_parent` OR a layout reorg dropping `/board/`. Pattern: prefer 2 cheap independent predicates over 1 cleaner predicate when the surfaces are independent and cost is one extra `&&`. (b) **Reserved-headroom-UNUSED precedent extended to 5 data points** — bug fixes scoped to one existing helper class with quoted failing-state + quoted fix-shape in the design land single-stage clean. Decision rule reinforced: bug fixes scoped to one existing helper → expect clean; new mechanism / new UI surface → expect CFs. (c) **Master-driven cycles can be very fast** — entire S1 was master-driven (1-line edit + 3 manifest bumps + harness runs + commit) and took fewer turns than any prior cycle. No subagent dispatches needed because edits were mechanical AND design specificity removed all judgment-call ambiguity at execution time. Pattern: master-driven shape is right when (1) edits are mechanical, (2) version-bump-lockstep is the only multi-file orchestration, (3) no novel CustomJS class or template authoring. (d) **Carry-forward bugs: queue them with line numbers** — `blueprints i want.md` line 68-69 had clear repro + clear scope; brainstorm started at "which one?" instead of "what was the bug we talked about?" Pattern: file mid-cycle-surfaced bugs in `blueprints i want.md` with line + clear repro instead of carrying as conversation memory. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.18.2-project-map-subtasks-fix-result.md`.

## v0.19.0 styling mechanism CLOSED 2026-05-05

First new mechanism since v0.18.0 BeaconButton. Vendors the Baseline community theme (manifest.json + theme.css copied verbatim from `/Users/willfell/Documents/obsidian/sync/accuris/.obsidian/themes/Baseline/`) + ships a canonical 38-key Style Settings JSON (rose-pine-light light / melange-dark dark + Inter font + blockquote-edge + 1.8em h1 etc.) under `platform/mechanisms/styling/{assets/themes/Baseline/, data/style-settings-default.json}`. Style Settings community plugin declared as `external_plugins[]` prerequisite (NOT vendored — same posture as Templater + Slash Commander). Three new installer helpers (`applyVendoredThemes`, `applyAppearance`, `applyStyleSettings`) wired into `installItem` after `applyCorePluginSettings`; helper count 4 → 7. Mechanism count 5 → 6. Landmine #12 allowlist 3 → 6 paths (adds `.obsidian/themes/<Name>/` overwrite-with-backup + `.obsidian/appearance.json` cssTheme-override-with-snippet-union + `.obsidian/plugins/obsidian-style-settings/data.json` per-key first-wins). NEW landmine **#15**: vendored theme is mechanism-owned; never hand-edit `.obsidian/themes/<Name>/`. **Cycle shape:** S1 (vendor + manifest scaffold) + S2 TDD-first (15 failing harness cases VT/AP/SS, then 3 parallel subagents implementing helpers, then installItem wiring) + S3 dogfood + barebones + smokes A-F + S4 docs/version + S5 close. **ONE in-cycle CF** (CF-1) surfaced at S3 barebones smoke A: helpers wrote despite Style Settings plugin being absent in barebones because the existing `applyExternalPlugins` only emits a warning + continues. Per design Section 4, when prereq is absent NOTHING should be written. Fix: NEW shared helper `_externalPluginsSatisfied(tp, manifest)` reads `.obsidian/community-plugins.json`, returns `{ok, missingIds}`; all three new helpers call the gate at the top and emit `info/{theme_overwrite,appearance,style_settings}` + `action: "skipped_missing_prereq"` + zero writes when prereq missing. styling@0.1.0 → 0.1.1 PATCH per gotcha 9 in-cycle re-process bump rule (lockstep edits to 4 files: workshop manifest, mechanism manifest, workshop subscription, barebones subscription). Helper-cases harness 255 → 312 (+57 sub-asserts via 15 new VT1-VT5 / AP1-AP5 / SS1-SS5 cases). Renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 (styling has no CustomJS class). Workshop_version 0.18.2 → 0.19.0 MINOR (mirrors v0.11.0 cards + v0.18.0 beacon-button precedent). Tag `v0.19.0` annotated and pushed. **NEW lessons (v0.19.0):** (a) **Helpers that materialize NEW state need stronger prereq contracts than helpers that READ existing state.** `applyTemplaterHotkeys` etc. degrade gracefully via missing-target Notices because they READ existing files. The styling helpers ACTIVELY MATERIALIZE state from nothing (theme dir + appearance.json + plugin data.json), so a downstream prereq miss isn't recoverable post-hoc — must short-circuit at the top. Pattern: any helper that mkdirs/writes-fresh-from-source needs an explicit prereq check, not just upstream warnings. (b) **Shared prereq gate `_externalPluginsSatisfied`** is now reusable across any future helper that materializes state and depends on a community-plugin prereq. Returns `{ok, missingIds}` shape; conservative on read/parse failure (ok=false). (c) **First-of-its-kind UI mechanism cycle pulled exactly 1 CF** (vs the 2-6 the v0.5.0/v0.11.0/v0.12.0/v0.6.0/v0.16.0/v0.17.0 precedent suggested). Stronger than expected because: helpers were structurally analogous to existing v0.1.3/v0.3.0 helpers (3-precedent posture mirror); 15 TDD-first harness cases caught all functional bugs pre-smoke; the only surface that smoke surfaced was the cross-helper prereq contract, which the plan T3.2 explicitly anticipated as a CF candidate. Pattern: when the design quotes the helper posture pattern AND the harness covers the spec literally, even first-of-its-kind cycles can land near-clean. (d) **`require("fs")` + `require("path")` per-helper local imports** is the canonical pattern for helpers that read from outside the vault adapter (workshop). Don't promote these to module-top — keep them local for clarity that THIS helper reaches outside `.obsidian/`. (e) **Two backup-suffix conventions** now live in the codebase and the landmine doc: `.bak` for file-content overwrite (vendored themes; v0.2.0 boards Option B precedent), `.beacon-backup` for plugin-data additive merge (Templater/Slash Commander/core daily-notes/appearance/Style Settings). Mixing them would be confusing — codified in landmine #12 mechanic #2. (f) **Three parallel subagents implementing independent helpers in one batched message** worked cleanly: applyVendoredThemes / applyAppearance / applyStyleSettings each implemented in isolation against the same harness target; master committed the combined work in a single S2 atomic commit. Pattern: when N independent helpers share posture + the harness pre-locks behavior + each subagent has a stable insertion point, parallel dispatch saves wall-time vs sequential. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.19.0-styling-mechanism-result.md`.

## v0.20.0 docs polish + T4.0 + landmine #16 + trailing-whitespace lint CLOSED 2026-05-05

Pure docs + harness cycle closing 12 cycles of compounding maintenance debt in a single MINOR commit window. **Three master-driven stages, no manual smokes (harnesses ARE the smoke).** S1 harness hardening (commit `0053fb8`): T4.0 fatal eliminated (12 cycles old since v0.2.0) by inlining synthetic kanban body in `run-renderer.js` + sentinel `template_source: 'synthetic://kanban-board'` + adapter override match — `node platform/test/run-renderer.js` (no selector) exits 0 for the first time since v0.2.0; trailing-whitespace lint case `caseTW1TemplatesNoTrailingWhitespace` added to `run-helper-cases.js` walking `platform/blueprints/<bp>/{content,templates}/*.md` two-level layout, failure-loud with `<file>:<line>` + JSON-quoted line on violation; **34 templates scanned, 0 violations on first run** (v0.18.1 CF-1 trim was the only outstanding defect; corpus is clean). Helper-cases 312 → 346 (+34 sub-asserts via TW1 case). S2 docs polish (commit `662f21c` + S2 polish `2028927`): NEW landmine **#16** (in-cycle re-process bump rule) promoting the 5-data-point precedent across v0.6.0 / v0.17.0 / v0.18.0 / v0.18.1 / v0.19.0 with literal `install.js:223` short-circuit quote (`if (installedEntry && installedEntry.version === node.sub.version) continue;`) + 4-file lockstep rule for mechanism cycles per v0.19.0 CF-1 reinforcement; `Docs/how.md` "Gotchas / Lessons learned (v0.11.0–v0.19.0)" section appended with 10 load-bearing entries (in-cycle bump rule, API-contract quoting, dv-shim composition, mobile-aware design, embed dedup, YAML date auto-parsing, NBSP gotcha, two backup-suffix conventions, prereq gates for state-materializing helpers, bounded cohesion); `blueprints i want.md` cleaned of three closures (styling DONE v0.19.0, new-meeting-button parity SATISFIED v0.18.1, project sub-task on map FIXED v0.18.2) — only finance personal/business expansion remains as open carry. S2 polish caught 2 cross-doc cosmetics surfaced by quality reviewer: lead-paragraph "Eleven cycles" count fix + landmine #10 install.js line drift fix (`:111` → `:223`; matched `installedEntry.version === node.sub.version` ordering with #16; added cross-link "see landmine #16 for the literal call-site quote"). S3 close: workshop_version 0.19.0 → 0.20.0 MINOR (matches v0.13.0/v0.14.0 docs-+-harness-substantive precedent for new-landmine + new-harness-behavior cycles). NO installer changes; NO mechanism / blueprint version bumps; NO manifest schema changes; NO renderer harness count change (T4.0 + 7 selectors PASS as before, just whole-suite green now). **Reserved-headroom-UNUSED 6th data point** (precedent: v0.13.0 + v0.14.0 + v0.18.0 + v0.18.1 + v0.18.2 + v0.20.0 — pure docs + harness cycles reliably land clean; one S2 polish commit for cosmetic count + cross-doc line drift, but no in-cycle CF). Subagent execution shape: per-stage subagent dispatch (S1 implementer + spec/quality reviewers; S2 implementer + spec/quality reviewers; S3 master-driven). Both implementer subagents landed clean first-pass (zero re-review cycles) — API-contract quoting + master-provided pre-captures (install.js:223 verbatim, two-level glob correction) gated subagent ambiguity to zero. Landmines list **15 → 16**. **NEW lessons (v0.20.0):** (a) **Test isolation via inline synthetic bodies decouples harness tests from blueprint churn** — T4.0 root cause was a 12-cycle-old `fs.readFileSync` coupling to a deleted blueprint file; the `'synthetic://kanban-board'` sentinel reads as test-only at a glance and is reusable for any future test fixture decoupled from on-disk state. (b) **Helper-cases as a generic lint host** — adding a per-file violation walker (`caseTW1`) without new selector machinery extends harness reach cheaply; pattern reusable for any future blueprint-asset content lint (e.g., frontmatter-shape, embed-link audit). The two-level `readdir` walk + extension filter + `assertTrue`-per-file + JSON-quoted violation dump skeleton can be promoted to a shared helper if a second consumer surfaces. (c) **Cross-doc line-citation drift is a real maintenance class** — landmine #10's stale `install.js:111` citation (drifted to :223 over 12 cycles) was caught by the S2 quality reviewer; future cycles that cite specific source lines should mark them as drift candidates AND any cycle that touches the cited file should grep all docs for `<filename>:<lineno>` references and update in lockstep. (d) **Two-stage subagent review (spec then quality) yields different signal at low cost** — spec compliance found ZERO issues; quality reviewer found 2 real cross-doc nits + 2 cosmetic nits. The two-stage flow is cheap insurance; spec-only review would have shipped the count bug + landmine #10 drift. (e) **Master-driven S3 (cycle close) is the right shape** — version bumps + status-snapshot updates + result writeups + tag creation are judgment-heavy + approval-gated; subagent dispatch adds friction without value. Pattern reaffirms v0.18.2 lesson c (master-driven cycles can be very fast for mechanical edits). Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-05-v0.20.0-docs-polish-result.md`.

## v0.21.0 consumer bootstrap CLOSED 2026-05-06

First new platform layer since v0.19.0. Ships `platform/bootstrap.js` + 4 lib modules + new `_https.js` (CF-1 redirect-following) for a Node-based interactive bootstrap orchestrator. Fresh consumer goes from `git clone` to fully-loaded with one command (after a one-time `cd <workshop> && npm install`). **First npm dep in the workshop:** `@inquirer/prompts` (also adds `package.json` + `package-lock.json` + `node_modules/` to `.gitignore`). New `foundational_plugins[]` schema field at workshop manifest level (templater-obsidian + customjs + dataview). Allowlist 6 → 9 paths (USER APPROVED): `.obsidian/plugins/<id>/{main.js,manifest.json,styles.css}` for vendored plugin files. New landmine **#17** — bootstrap network posture (failure-loud + idempotent skip-if-present + path-traversal validator + `.beacon-backup` on overwrite + GITHUB_TOKEN honored + redirect-following + GitHub-only host + no mid-fetch cleanup). Landmine #8 amended (install.js is filesystem-only; bootstrap.js is the network gateway). **Five in-cycle CFs:** CF-1 (`_https.getText` follows 302 redirects — surfaced at first real GitHub fetch in Phase A; harness mocks returned 200 directly so 8 BS cases passed pre-CF-1; new BS9 `caseBS9FollowsRedirects` locks behavior, harness 8 → 9 cases / 34 → 36 sub-asserts), CF-2 (`runInstall` output simplification: 1983-line JSON dumps replaced with ~30-80 line condensed summary [Notice lines + Verdict + section counts] + tee'd to `<vault>/Docs/Meta/bootstrap-last-install.log`; required also fixing `run-install.js` `process.exit(N)` → `process.exitCode = N` pre-existing pipe-truncation bug + switching wrapper to async `spawn` so child `close` event fires after stdout EOF), CF-3 (defer workshop-manifest discovery into the wizard so first-run works from any depth — Phase C from `/workshop/scratch/1` needed `../../beacon` not the hardcoded default `../beacon`; wizard now prompts + validates path, then loads manifest after path validates; `path.resolve` not `path.join` so absolute paths restart resolution correctly), CF-4 (vendor v0.1.2 thin-stub on first-run + defensively on every run — `<vault>/Docs/Meta/Templater/platformInstall.js` was missing on fresh `scratch/1`, causing run-install.js to fail "bootstrap installer missing"; landmine #13 content-static md5 invariant `a39257da1dd49ae4481e5cd0a42bdac4` honored — write only if missing), CF-5 (canonical platform path variables in config — wizard wrote `variables: { workshop }` only but every blueprint's manifest uses `{{templates_path}}/Template, X.md` etc.; new CANONICAL_VARIABLES constant in wizard.js writes 6 path defaults + workshop + vault_identity_tag on first-run, AND defensive augment-if-missing on every run for older configs). **Plus styling 0.1.1 → 0.1.2 PATCH unstick** at CF-2-prereq time: barebones had a no-op-due-to-missing-prereq install of styling@0.1.1 from a prior run; install.js short-circuit at line 223 then skipped re-processing on every subsequent run, so styling helpers never fired even after Phase A added obsidian-style-settings to community-plugins.json. Canonical landmine #16 unstick: 4-file PATCH lockstep bump forces re-process. Architectural root cause (DEFERRED to v0.22.0+ candidate): helpers that no-op-due-to-missing-prereq should signal to the orchestrator NOT to write installed_at; install.js writes installed_at unconditionally based on "did the helpers throw" rather than "did anything actually materialize." **Subagent dispatch shape:** S2 master-driven (TDD-first 8 failing harness cases + schema + npm scaffold); S3 4 parallel subagents (1 per lib module: community-plugins-index / fetch-plugin / community-plugins-merge / wizard) + master-driven entry + master-driven harness iteration; S3 two-stage review (spec PASS / quality CHANGES-REQUESTED with 14 findings → applied 7 [path traversal validator + atomic write + backup-failure throws + body drain + dedupe _httpsGetText into _https.js + trim wizard inputs + force-redl filter] / re-review APPROVED). 7 BS cases → 9 BS cases (BS9 added in CF-1). Helper-cases harness UNCHANGED at 346 (zero installer-schema additions); renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 cases. **NEW lessons (v0.21.0):** (a) **First-of-its-kind orchestration cycles draw 4-6 in-cycle CFs at integration boundaries** — harness mocks can't fully simulate real-world vendor behavior (302 redirects, missing config files, depth-mismatched relative paths, missing thin-stub, schema-default expectations). Plan reserved Y.Z.x headroom for first-of-its-kind even when scope is "infrastructure" not "UI" (precedent now extends from 7-data-point UI to 8-data-point first-of-kind). (b) **`process.exit` truncation when piped is a real footgun in CLI tools** — children using `console.log` + `process.exit(N)` lose buffered stdout when stdio is a pipe; canonical Node fix is `process.exitCode = N` + let main return naturally. Worth grepping platform/test for other `process.exit` callsites if a future CLI wrapper needs full output capture. (c) **Async `spawn` over `spawnSync` when capturing child stdout** — sync capture hits race conditions on child exit; the close event fires AFTER stdout EOF only on async spawn. (d) **Atomic write + backup-on-overwrite + path-traversal validator are the canonical posture for any new state-materializing helper** — quality-review #1/#3/#4 distilled the v0.1.3 helper posture into 3 reusable invariants; future bootstrap-lib additions (or any helper that vendors files outside `.obsidian/plugins/<id>/data.json`) should adopt this stack from the start. (e) **Defer-then-validate-then-load is the right wizard architecture for chicken-and-egg path resolution** — bootstrap.js used to pre-read manifest before wizard ran (wrong default → fail before wizard); wizard now owns the path prompt + validation + manifest load. Pattern: when a config field gates downstream prompts/data loads, gate the load behind the field's prompt+validator. (f) **Defensive augment-on-every-run** for new schema fields makes patches retroactive — older config files missing canonical_variables get them added on next bootstrap run, no manual hand-edit, no version migration needed. Pattern reusable for any future schema extension where backward compatibility is desired. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-06-v0.21.0-consumer-bootstrap-result.md`.

## v0.21.1 consumer-convenience CLOSED 2026-05-06

Pure-additive PATCH cycle closing the Phase C user requests from v0.21.0. NEW mechanism `convenience@0.1.0` ships zero files; declares two new shared manifest fields: `hotkeys[]` (workspace:copy-full-path Cmd+- + workspace:copy-path Cmd+=) + `community_plugin_settings[]` (dataview enableDataviewJs + enableInlineDataviewJs) + `external_plugins:[{id:"dataview"}]`. Daily blueprint 0.2.1 → 0.2.2 PATCH adds `autorun:true` to existing `core_plugin_settings.daily-notes` entry + new `hotkeys[]` field with Cmd+[ → daily-notes binding. **Two new installer helpers:** `applyHotkeys` (additive per-`command_id` first-wins; pre-existing user bindings always preserved) + `applyCommunityPluginData` (additive shallow merge; `substituteLenient` round-trip; path-traversal validator on `id`; plugin-dir-absent skip; broadened prereq gate that short-circuits on ANY declared `external_plugins[]` id absent — divergent from v0.19.0 styling helpers' `required:true` interpretation, justified because materializing into a missing plugin's data.json is a wasted write). Helper count **7 → 9** (allowlist-touching). Allowlist (landmine #12) **9 → 11** (USER APPROVED at design close): `.obsidian/plugins/dataview/data.json` + `.obsidian/hotkeys.json`. **Cycle shape:** master-driven S0 + S1 TDD-first 10 failing harness cases (HK1-HK5 + CP1-CP5; commit `1820044`) + **2 parallel subagents in S2** (subagent A applyHotkeys 1634-1805 + subagent B applyCommunityPluginData 2472-2734; both landed clean first-pass with single combined commit `f197a6a`) + S3 barebones install + idempotency check + **all 4 manual smokes A-D PASS first-try** + S4 docs + close. **Reserved-headroom UNUSED 7th data point** extending the precedent (v0.13.0 + v0.14.0 + v0.18.0 + v0.18.1 + v0.18.2 + v0.20.0 + v0.21.1 — pure-additive helper-pair / pure docs / pure adoption / scoped-bug-fix cycles reliably land clean). Subagent A wired `applyHotkeys` after `applyStyleSettings` (line 620); subagent B wired `applyCommunityPluginData` between `applyCorePluginSettings` and `applyVendoredThemes` (line 616) — no collision because the insertion points are 800+ lines apart. Helper-cases harness 346 → 387 sub-asserts (+41 net via 10 new HK1-HK5 + CP1-CP5 cases). Bootstrap 36/36 + renderer green; whole-suite green preserved. Workshop_version 0.21.0 → 0.21.1 PATCH; tag `v0.21.1` annotated and pushed. **NEW lessons (v0.21.1):** (a) **Parallel-subagent dispatch with literal-contract prompts works cleanly when insertion points are far apart** — both subagents landed clean first-pass; the API-contract quoting (v0.6.0 lesson) plus literal "insert immediately AFTER X function; don't touch any other line of installItem" gating eliminated collision risk. Pattern: when N independent helpers share posture and the implementation files are 800+ lines apart, parallel dispatch with literal insertion-point pinning saves wall-time vs sequential. (b) **Broadened prereq gate is a defensible per-helper choice** — the styling helpers (v0.19.0) honor `_externalPluginsSatisfied`'s `required:true` semantic; `applyCommunityPluginData` (v0.21.1) broadens to ANY declared `external_plugins[]` id. The divergence is intentional and documented in landmine #12 + how.md; future helpers should pick the semantic that fits their failure mode rather than forcing a single platform-wide interpretation. (c) **First-wins on hotkeys is non-negotiable for consumer trust** — never modify a binding the user has already set. The skipped_existing history entry is the canonical signal; authors who want to FORCE a binding need to instruct the consumer to delete the prior binding first. There's no override knob and there shouldn't be one. (d) **Cmd+= is the literal physical key for "Cmd++"** on US keyboards — the unmodified key is `=`; users say "Cmd++" because that's what's printed on the keycap with shift. The hotkeys.json shape stores the unmodified key. Future hotkey authoring docs need to call this out (already in how.md). Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-06-v0.21.1-consumer-convenience-result.md`.

## v0.22.0 inside-vault bootstrap CLOSED 2026-05-06

Flips beacon's consumer-entry surface from hand-typed `node ../beacon/platform/bootstrap.js` to a remote curl one-liner that clones beacon into `<vault>/Beacon/`, npm-installs, runs the existing first-run wizard, writes activation + CLI artifacts. Ships a Lean MVP `beacon` CLI with four verbs (`bootstrap` / `update` / `status` / `wizard`) activated per-shell via `source Beacon/Scripts/activate.sh` (zero shell-rc edits; mirrors Python venv / nvm / pyenv idiom). NEW `install.sh` (repo root, chmod 0755) — bash orchestrator pulled by curl; preflight (node + git) + vault validation + Beacon/ overwrite handling (timestamped `Beacon.bak.YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS` preserves prior backups; non-tty stdin refuse-loud per S3 CF defensive hardening) + clone --depth=1 + npm install --omit=dev + exec hand-off to node CLI. NEW `platform/cli/{beacon-cli, cmd-bootstrap, cmd-update, cmd-status, cmd-wizard}.js` — dispatcher walks cwd ancestors for `Docs/Meta/platform-config.json` (BEACON_VAULT env var fallback); failure-loud on dirty tree without --force; visual `[N/M] step... OK` posture across all verbs. NEW `platform/visual/{banner, section, colors}.js` — ANSI escape primitives with TTY fallback (no new dep — `@inquirer/prompts` remains the only npm dep). REFACTORED `platform/bootstrap.js` — extracted `phaseFirstRunWizard / phaseFetchPlugins / phaseRunInstaller` from `runBootstrap` (back-compat preserved); NEW `phaseWriteActivation` generates `Beacon/Scripts/{activate.sh, beacon}` with absolute paths baked at install-time + atomic write + `.beacon-backup` on overwrite (matches landmine #12 mechanic #2). CHANGED `platform/bootstrap-lib/wizard.js` first-run default `workshop_relative_path` `"../beacon"` → `"Beacon"` (both interactive + nonInteractive paths; sibling-of-workshop layout still works for legacy POC vaults). **Cycle shape:** master-driven across all 5 stages with sequential implementer-then-spec-then-quality reviewer dispatch per stage. S1 TDD-first failing-baseline (commit `9a55e1e` + S1 review fix `f85d0ac` — `withTempVault` setup type-guard + per-case try/catch + harness hygiene); S2 CLI surface (commit `7d1a3b4` — phase refactor + 4 cmd verbs + visual primitives, all 13 sub-asserts in run-cli.js GREEN); S3 install.sh (commit `96c4a91`) + S3 CF defensive hardening (commit `f56c7fc` — IMP-1 non-tty stdin refuse-loud + IMP-2 timestamped Beacon.bak preservation + IMP-3 tilde/relative path canonicalization + IMP-4 trap-on-EXIT cleanup of half-installed Beacon/); S4 docs (commit `e4a67d2` — NEW `Docs/install.md` user-facing reference with curl one-liner + sync-exclusion guides for Obsidian Sync / iCloud / Dropbox + 4-scenario troubleshooting + uninstall; landmine #18 codifies `Beacon/` is git-managed; CLAUDE ask-before-acting amendment for `Beacon/` capital top-level; how.md CLI surface section; use.md curl-onboarding subsection); S5 manual smoke (real curl-pulled bootstrap against `/Users/willfell/scratch/2/` empty dir USER-CONFIRMED clean across all 4 verbs `status / update --force / wizard / cwd-walk-from-subdir`) + cycle close. **TWO new harnesses + ONE existing extension:** `run-bootstrap.js` 36 → 45 sub-asserts (+9 net via BS10-BS13 phaseWriteActivation + wizard default + sibling fallback + atomic-write/backup); NEW `run-cli.js` 13 sub-asserts under 10 cases C1-C10 covering dispatcher cwd-walk + BEACON_VAULT fallback + verb-routing + status/update/wizard with default-with-override DI pattern (`_gitExec`, `_npmInstall`, `_runInstaller`, `_runReRunWizard`); NEW `run-install-sh.js` 10 sub-asserts under 6 cases I1-I6 using bash-shim posture (PATH-prefixed git/npm/node shim scripts) — the install-sh harness was the FIRST bash-driver harness in the codebase, with shim posture extending the v0.20.0 "test isolation via inline synthetic bodies" lesson to the bash-driver layer. Helper-cases harness UNCHANGED at 387 (no new schema fields or installer helpers; CLI is its own surface). Renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 cases. Allowlist (landmine #12) UNCHANGED at 11 paths (CLI does not touch `.obsidian/`). Helper count UNCHANGED at 9. **Architectural changes:** dispatcher `bootstrapCtxFromArgs(rest)` helper synthesizes minimal ctx for the `bootstrap` verb (parses `--vault PATH` from argv; pre-populates workshopPath = vault/Beacon + workshopManifest from just-cloned manifest.json). Bootstrap is the only verb that creates platform-config.json; the dispatcher must tolerate config absence for THIS verb only. Other verbs go straight to `resolveContext()` and fail-loud on missing config. Test-hook DI pattern (underscore-prefixed methods on ctx) documented in each cmd-*.js file's header comment. **Reserved-headroom-USED 1 CF (8th data point):** S3 CF defensive hardening surfaced 4 IMPORTANT issues that would have broken real-user flows (curl-pipe stdin hang, .bak silent destruction, half-install on npm fail, tilde/relative path mishandling) — caught by code-quality reviewer pre-close, fixed in-cycle. First-of-its-kind orchestration cycles continue to draw 1-6 in-cycle CFs at integration boundaries (precedent: v0.21.0 5 CFs, v0.22.0 1 CF, both first-of-kind orchestration). Workshop_version 0.21.1 → 0.22.0 MINOR (matches v0.21.0 bootstrap-MINOR precedent; new top-level UX surface + new CLI binary + new bin artifacts justify MINOR not PATCH). Tag `v0.22.0` annotated and pushed. **NEW lessons (v0.22.0):** (a) **Per-shell source-activation matches Python venv / nvm / pyenv conventions and avoids shell-rc edit risk** — design's lowest-blast-radius choice; each new shell needs `source Beacon/Scripts/activate.sh`; no global PATH pollution; vault is fully portable. Trade-off: one extra command per shell. (b) **Inside-vault `<vault>/Beacon/` (capital B) coexists cleanly with the lowercase `beacon/<module>/` blueprint-content namespace** — codified as second sanctioned top-level vault dir in CLAUDE ask-before-acting + landmine #18. (c) **`bootstrapCtxFromArgs` is the right shape for the one verb that creates state** — bootstrap is special because it MUST tolerate missing platform-config.json (it CREATES it); a synthetic minimal ctx + `--vault` arg + bypass of `resolveContext` cleanly handles this without polluting other verbs' resolution. (d) **Bash-driver harnesses use PATH-prefixed shim scripts as the inline-synthetic-body posture** — extends v0.20.0 lesson (a) to the bash layer; `withShimEnv(setup, fn)` mirrors `withTempVault(setup, fn)` shape. Shim heredocs MUST be column-zero in source. `${process.execPath}` for node passthrough avoids shimDir-vs-system-PATH recursion. macOS `/bin/bash` (3.2) vs Linux `/usr/bin/bash` (5.x) — PATH must include `/bin` for spawnSync to find macOS bash. (e) **`install.sh --non-interactive` is bash-side only** — does NOT propagate to the node wizard; first-run wizard prompts still fire. v0.22.x carry-forward: either propagate the flag through to node CLI + wizard auto-accept-defaults mode (enables CI/headless installs), OR document the constraint clearly. (f) **Fresh-clone `Beacon/` reports git status `dirty` immediately after install.sh** — likely because `npm install --omit=dev` reconciles `package-lock.json` against the just-cloned tree; `beacon update --force` handles it. v0.22.x carry-forward: investigate whether `package-lock.json` can be made deterministic OR add post-install untracked files to a workshop-side `.gitignore`. (g) **Banner version reflects live manifest at clone time** — install.sh shows the manifest.json `workshop_version` at clone time. (h) **TDD baseline harnesses can ship plan-quoted bugs that surface only at S2/S3** — S1 baseline shipped `withTempVault({}, ...)` per plan-verbatim; helper-side fix in S1-fix preserved plan-of-record without retroactively rewriting case bodies. (i) **`set -euo pipefail` + `trap cleanup_partial EXIT` + `INSTALL_OK=1` immediately before exec** is the canonical bash posture for orchestrators that may leave half-installed state. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-06-v0.22.0-inside-vault-bootstrap-result.md`.

## v0.22.1 --non-interactive propagation + dirty-tree fix CLOSED 2026-05-06

Pure-additive PATCH closing the two highest-leverage v0.22.0 carries (NEW lessons e + f) plus one pre-flight-surfaced harness bug (I2 PATH-includes-system-git on macOS) plus IMP-5 from v0.22.0 quality review (`--vault=PATH` equals form). install.sh now forwards `--non-interactive`, `--mechanisms=<csv|all|none>`, `--blueprints=<csv|all|none>` to the node CLI via NODE_ARGS bash-array (conditional appends gated on `HAS_*=1`). cmd-bootstrap.js gains exported `_parseFlags` + `_resolveListArg` helpers; `run` builds `wizardDefaults` + threads through `runBootstrap`. wizard.js nonInteractive branch raises default `selectedMechs` from `["customjs-guard"]` to `DEFAULT_MECHANISMS_CHECKED.slice()` (5 mechs — same as interactive checkbox default); NEW `_resolveMechs` / `_resolveBlueprints` helpers honor `"all"` literal (resolves to manifest entry names) + array passthrough + default fallback. Workshop-side `.gitignore` adds 9 root-anchored module-dir patterns (`/Scripts/`, `/boards/`, `/daily/`, `/finance/`, `/journal/`, `/meetings/`, `/projects/`, `/to-do/`, `/trips/`) masking the macOS APFS case-collision dirty-tree symptom (Beacon/ aliases to lowercase beacon/<module>/ on case-insensitive FS — blueprint module-dir mkdirs land INSIDE Beacon/ instead of vault root). Linux/case-sensitive volumes unaffected. Docs/install.md gains `[!warning]` callout cross-referencing landmines #11 + #18. **Cycle shape:** 3 stages, master-driven on implementation per plan analysis; two-stage subagent review (spec then quality) at S1 + S2 close per v0.20.0 lesson d. **S1 (commit `ed1a656`)** TDD-first failing baseline — I2 PATH narrowing fix (drop /usr/bin so system git can't be found; latent host-dependent bug since v0.22.0) + I7 (--non-interactive propagation through node argv) + I8 (--vault=PATH equals form) + C11-C14 (cmd-bootstrap parses --non-interactive / --mechanisms=all / --blueprints CSV / wizard NI defaults match DEFAULT_MECHANISMS_CHECKED). **S2 (commit `f88eab8` + CF-1 `8711e91`)** implementation — install.sh + cmd-bootstrap + wizard.js + .gitignore + install.md callout. **CF-1**: code-quality reviewer flagged that bare-name .gitignore patterns (`Scripts/`, `boards/`, etc.) match at any depth, risking silent drop of future workshop content under nested paths like `Docs/Meta/Scripts/<mech>/...` or `platform/blueprints/<bp>/<bare-name>/...`. Root-anchored fix (`/Scripts/` etc.) preserves dirty-tree masking at workshop root only (where the case-collision materializes); nested content tracks normally on Linux/case-sensitive FS. Verified live via `git check-ignore` probes (nested → exit non-zero; root → exit zero). **S3** smoke against scratch/3 (NI-default install: 4 phases green, ZERO prompts, 9 history entries, clean Beacon/ git status, NI subscription = 5 default mechs) + scratch/4 (NI-all install: 43 history entries, 8 mechs + 8 blueprints subscribed, all 9 module dirs materialize INSIDE Beacon/ confirming case-collision, root-anchored .gitignore masks correctly, `beacon status` reports "8 mechanisms · 8 blueprints · Drift: none"). Smoke used `BEACON_REPO_URL=file://...` env override because the workshop GitHub remote needs personal-account auth not available in the smoke environment — the `file://` transport exercises the full bash → node → wizard flow. **Reserved-headroom-USED 1 CF (9th data point) — first MIXED case (pure-additive PATCH that pulled exactly 1 CF from the review-loop).** Decision rule refinement: expected-clean cycles can still pull 1 review-surfaced CF; the two-stage spec-then-quality reviewer dispatch is the load-bearing surface (spec-only review would have shipped the latent silent-ignore bug). **Two harness extensions:** `run-install-sh.js` 10 → 14 sub-asserts (+4 from I7 + I8; I2 fix is sub-assert-neutral but flips a prior FAIL into PASS); `run-cli.js` 13 → 20 sub-asserts (+7 from C11-C14). Helper-cases / bootstrap / renderer UNCHANGED. Workshop_version 0.22.0 → 0.22.1 PATCH; tag `v0.22.1` annotated and pushed. Landmines list UNCHANGED at 18; allowlist UNCHANGED at 11; helper count UNCHANGED at 9. **NEW lessons (v0.22.1):** (a) **`.gitignore` patterns must be root-anchored when masking install-time-generated artifacts that share names with content namespaces elsewhere in the tree.** Bare-name patterns match at any depth; future mechanism / blueprint cycles landing nested files would silently lose tracking. Root-anchoring (`/boards/`) preserves the masking at workshop root without collateral. Pattern reusable for any future workshop-side `.gitignore` extension. (b) **`phaseFirstRunWizard` short-circuits on existing config** — `--overwrite` to install.sh wipes Beacon/ but preserves consumer-side platform-{config,subscription}.json. The wizard only runs when both files are absent; on re-bootstrap, existing config is read verbatim and any new flags are ignored. Implication: testing the new --mechanisms=all / --blueprints=all overrides requires a fresh-vault smoke target, not a --overwrite re-bootstrap. v0.22.x candidate: `install.sh --reset-config` flag. (c) **`BEACON_REPO_URL=file:///<path>` is a serviceable smoke transport** when the workshop dev environment lacks anonymous-read access to the workshop's GitHub remote. install.sh line 7 already supports the env var; no install.sh change needed. v0.22.x candidate: document `file://` URL pattern explicitly in install.md "smoke testing" section. (d) **Reserved-headroom precedent now 9 data points; first MIXED case introduced (S2 CF-1).** Pure-additive PATCH cycles that ship the two-stage subagent review can still pull 1 review-surfaced CF; cycles that skip quality review would have shipped this latent bug. (e) **Two-stage subagent review (v0.20.0 lesson d) reaffirmed at scale.** S2 spec reviewer found ZERO issues; S2 quality reviewer found one IMP (root-anchoring) + one NIT. Pattern: spec compliance gates "did you do what the plan said," quality gates "is what you did sound." Cheap insurance. (f) **Plan baseline-count drift is harmless when commit-message reports actual reality.** Plan T1.9 predicted "install-sh 13/2" but actual was 11/3 (I7 marker-exists assertion correctly PASSES; only flag-includes assert FAILS). Commit message used the actual count. Pattern: when TDD harness predicted counts diverge from observed, prefer reality in the commit message; the plan stays as-of-design-time. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-06-v0.22.1-non-interactive-and-dirty-tree-result.md`.

## v0.23.0 Sauce rebrand + workshop clone rename CLOSED 2026-05-06

Resolves the macOS APFS case-collision RUNTIME breakage from v0.22.1 by renaming the workshop clone dir from `Beacon/` to `pantry/` (non-aliasing with the lowercase `beacon/<module>/` namespace per landmine #11). Bundles full Sauce platform rebrand: project brand "Beacon" → "Sauce", CLI binary `beacon` → `sauce` (file rename `platform/cli/beacon-cli.js` → `platform/cli/sauce-cli.js`; binary `<workshop>/Scripts/beacon` → `<workshop>/Scripts/sauce`), env vars `BEACON_VAULT` / `BEACON_REPO_URL` / `BEACON_REPO_BRANCH` / `BEACON_TEST_MODE` → `SAUCE_*`, ASCII banner, GitHub repo rename `github.com/willfell/beacon` → `github.com/willfell/sauce` (auto-redirect preserved). **Multi-cycle naming destination locked at brainstorm:** workshop clone = `pantry/` (v0.23.0); modules = `spice/<module>/` (v0.25.0); runtime = `ranch/` (v0.24.0); future mechanism additions inherit sauce/kitchen/ranch aesthetic. Removes v0.22.1 `.gitignore` root-anchored module-dir block (case-collision gone). install.md `[!warning]` callout flipped to `[!success]`; new "Upgrading from v0.22.x" section (one-shot `mv Beacon pantry` + sed on platform-config.json). `.beacon-backup` suffix PRESERVED across rename per landmine #12 mechanic #2 (renaming would silently miss prior backups in consumer vaults). `beacon-button` mechanism queued for v0.24.0 rename alongside runtime rename. **Cycle shape:** 3 stages, master-driven on the rename mechanics + parallel subagents for docs (4 files) per CLAUDE.md cadence; two-stage subagent review (spec then quality) at S2 close per v0.20.0 lesson d + v0.22.1 reaffirmation. **S1 (commit `4c86b36`)** TDD-first failing baseline — 3 harnesses (run-install-sh + run-bootstrap + run-cli) updated to expect post-rename state (vault path, binary, env var, function name `caseBS11WizardDefaultsBeacon` → `caseBS11WizardDefaultsPantry`, `caseC2BeaconVaultEnv` → `caseC2SauceVaultEnv`). RED at S1 commit. **S2 (commit `<hash>`)** mass rename atomic — `git mv platform/cli/beacon-cli.js platform/cli/sauce-cli.js`; install.sh + bootstrap.js + wizard.js + cmd-bootstrap/status/update + .gitignore + 4 docs files + CLAUDE.md. ASCII banner width preserved with trailing-space adjustment ("Sauce " = 6 chars same as "Beacon"). All 5 harnesses GREEN: install-sh 14/0, bootstrap 45/0, cli 20/0, helper-cases 387/0, renderer exit 0. **S3** smoke against fresh `scratch/5` (`SAUCE_REPO_URL=file:///path/to/workshop` curl-bootstrap with `--non-interactive --mechanisms=all --blueprints=all`); manual mv + sed on `barebones-beacon-poc` + `accuris-beacon-poc` consumer vaults; ACCEPTANCE GATE — Daily Notes "Open today's daily note" SUCCEEDS on macOS APFS (the v0.22.1 RUNTIME breakage is fixed at the root). GitHub repo rename + `git remote set-url` + tag annotation pending USER APPROVAL at S3 close. Workshop_version 0.22.1 → 0.23.0 MINOR (mirrors v0.11.0 / v0.18.0 / v0.19.0 new-mechanism-or-substantial-surface MINOR-bump precedent). Helper-cases harness UNCHANGED at 387 (zero installer-schema additions); renderer harness UNCHANGED at 23 cases. **NEW lessons (v0.23.0):** (a) **`.beacon-backup` suffix preserved across rename** — landmine #12 mechanic #2 backup-suffix is the canonical convention; renaming to `.sauce-backup` would silently miss prior backups in consumer vaults. Pattern: backup-suffix conventions are platform-version-stable; brand-rename does NOT cascade to backup-file names. (b) **sed regex word-boundary discipline for brand-rename** — bare `s|beacon|sauce|g` would have corrupted `beacon-button` mechanism name. Mitigation: scope sed to specific anchored patterns (`s|`Beacon/|`pantry/|g`, `s|beacon status|sauce status|g`, etc.); never bare-substitute generic words. (c) **GitHub repo rename + permanent redirect** — the rename auto-redirects from old URL forever; tag history preserved; no migration tooling needed beyond `git remote set-url` + user shell-rc updates. (d) **Multi-cycle naming destination locked at brainstorm time** — gravity → magma → Sauce pivots happened in brainstorm (cheap); each subsequent cycle (v0.24.0, v0.25.0) marches toward the locked destination instead of revisiting naming each cycle. Pattern: brainstorm full naming sequence even if shipping in stages. (e) **Reserved-headroom: USED 3 review-surfaced CFs (10th data point; second MIXED case after v0.22.1).** Two-stage subagent review caught all three pre-commit, no S3-smoke regressions, no in-cycle bumps. **CF-1 spec:** `platform/visual/banner.js:9` runtime CLI banner still emitted "Beacon" — design + plan covered the install.sh ASCII banner but missed the runtime-CLI banner module. User-visible regression every time `sauce status`/`update`/`wizard` invoked. **CF-2 spec:** `Docs/how.md:338,370` documentation references `platform/cli/beacon-cli.js` after the file was git-mv'd to `sauce-cli.js`. **CF-3 quality:** `install.sh:3` Usage-comment curl URL pointed at `willfell/beacon` while `REPO_URL` default on L7 already updated to `willfell/sauce`. Spec-only review would have shipped CF-3; quality reviewer caught it. **Pattern reaffirms v0.22.1 lesson:** the two-stage spec-then-quality review is the load-bearing surface for wide-touching mechanical-rename cycles; cycles that skip quality review would ship latent inconsistencies. (f) **`platform/visual/banner.js` is a runtime-CLI brand surface.** Future brand-prose audits must include both `install.sh` ASCII banner AND `platform/visual/banner.js` runtime banner. Pattern: any brand-name string emitted to user-facing stdout from CLI verbs lives in this file. (g) **Pre-existing working-tree drift should be excluded from v0.X.Y atomic commits.** `Docs/Meta/Scripts/cards/beacon-cards.js` had cards@0.2.2 → 0.2.3 outline-pill content drift in the tree at session start (pre-v0.23.0). Quality reviewer caught it; deferred to a separate v0.23.x dogfood-resync commit. Pattern: when staging an atomic-rename commit, only stage files that the cycle touches; leave dogfood drift for a separate housekeeping commit. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-06-v0.23.0-sauce-rebrand-result.md`.

## v0.24.0 Tree 3 runtime rename + `beacon-button` → `accent-button` CLOSED 2026-05-07

Second of three multi-cycle Sauce rebrand renames. Tree 3 runtime plumbing rename `<vault>/Docs/Meta/` → `<vault>/ranch/` (path variables in every consumer's `platform-config.json:variables`; stub LOCATION moves; mechanism + blueprint sources + harness fixtures + infrastructure code all sed'd). `beacon-button` mechanism → `accent-button` (`BeaconButton` class → `AccentButton`; mechanism dir `git mv`'d; class file renamed; **15 `customJS.BeaconButton.render` call-sites** across project (6) + finance (8) + meetings (1) helpers migrated; 3 blueprint manifest depends_on entries renamed; workshop manifest mechanisms[] entry renamed; 2 consumer subscriptions renamed). Stub body line 11 hardcode-rewrite: `"Docs/Meta/platform-config.json"` → `"ranch/platform-config.json"`; **NEW md5 invariant `ea23aa812503bfca66359d3b2b239ba8`** (was `a39257da1dd49ae4481e5cd0a42bdac4` from v0.1.2 → v0.23.0); landmine #13 wording amended (stub body changes ONLY on Tree N rename cycles; rare). **Cycle shape:** 3 stages, master-driven on rename mechanics + harness updates; subagent dispatch for docs (4 files: install.md/use.md/how.md/landmines.md); two-stage spec/quality review pre-S2-commit. **THREE in-cycle CFs (11th data point; third MIXED case):** **CF-1 (S2 master sweep):** `run-bootstrap.js:82` + `install.js:3926` had multi-arg `path.join(dir, "Docs", "Meta")` — bare `s|Docs/Meta|ranch|g` literal sed misses these. Fixed by Edit calls. **CF-2 (S2 master sweep):** T2.11 sed scope was platform/install.js + bootstrap + cli + install.sh only. Did NOT include `platform/mechanisms/` or `platform/blueprints/`. Critical runtime paths in `validator/hook.js`, `audit-walker.js`, `validate.js` + jsdoc examples + `Project Map.md` template's `dv.view("Docs/Meta/Views/customjs-guard")` calls remained stale. Fixed by extending sed to mechanism + blueprint sources. **CF-3 (S3 acceptance gate):** `.obsidian/plugins/customjs/data.json:jsFolder` had stale `Docs/Meta/Scripts` value (pre-Tree-3) or absent (fresh install). After Tree 3 rename, every consumer's stale jsFolder no longer resolves → CustomJS scans nothing → all classes report "unavailable" on Cmd+R. **NEW `applyCustomJsSettings` helper** (~190 LOC; mirrors v0.21.1 applyHotkeys posture): runs ONCE per install run before per-item iteration; surgical migration (only overwrites legacy `Docs/Meta/Scripts` value or absent settings; preserves user-customized values via `info/skipped_user_customized` history); plugin-dir-absent skip via `skipped_missing_prereq`; backup-on-edit `.beacon-backup`; failure-loud history; path-traversal validator on `scripts_path`. **Allowlist #12 expanded 11 → 12 paths** (USER APPROVED at CF-3 surface) for `.obsidian/plugins/customjs/data.json`; helper count **9 → 10**. **Two-stage subagent review** caught 2 quality fixes pre-S2 commit (stale jsdoc citation in `accent-button.js:8` reworded; tone-policy version anchor in manifest description reworded). **Two S2 commits** (`494d29e` rename + `b2bdada` content fixup; git rename detection swallowed initial staging step → second commit captures the actual sed modifications — codified as v0.24.0 NEW lesson e). All 5 harnesses GREEN: bootstrap 45/0, cli 20/0, install-sh 14/0, helper-cases 387/0, renderer (no selector) exit 0. Manual acceptance gate (Daily Notes "Open today's daily note" on macOS APFS fresh `/Users/willfell/scratch/6/` + post-applyCustomJsSettings Cmd+R reload) PASSED. Workshop_version 0.23.0 → 0.24.0 MINOR. **NEW lessons (v0.24.0):** (a) **Tree-rename cycles must grep `path.join` multi-arg patterns explicitly** — bare literal sed misses `path.join(dir, "Docs", "Meta")`; design pre-flight should include `grep -rE 'path\.join.*"<oldpart>"\s*,\s*"<oldsubpart>"'` scan. (b) **Tree-rename sed scope must include source dirs, not just infrastructure code** — mechanism + blueprint sources + templates + jsdoc examples ALL hold runtime paths; design must enumerate. (c) **Foundational plugin data.json migrations are a Tree-rename concern** — every `.obsidian/plugins/<id>/data.json:<field>` that holds a path value needs migration audit at design-time. (d) **Acceptance-gate-surfaced CF distinct from review-surfaced CF** — review can't catch user-environment-dependent issues like stale plugin config; acceptance gate is load-bearing for Tree-rename cycles even after harness GREEN. (e) **git rename detection swallowed staging step** — `git add -A` post-`git mv` of wide-touching rename stages ONLY rename moves (100% similarity); content modifications need separate `git add -u` + verify `git diff --cached --stat` before committing; v0.24.0 split S2 into 2 commits. (f) **Surgical helper migration preserves user customization** — applyCustomJsSettings only overwrites legacy or absent values; pattern reusable for any future migration helper. (g) **Helper-once-per-install-run vs helper-per-item** — when setting is platform-wide (driven by `variables.*`), put helper at top-level install entry, not inside installItem. (h) **Stub body changes are rare events; new md5 must be captured durably** — first stub body change since v0.1.2 (~22 cycles); md5 captured in landmine #13 + install.md + CLAUDE.md atomically. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-07-v0.24.0-tree3-and-accent-button-result.md`.

## v0.25.0 Tree 2 module-namespace rename + `.beacon-backup` → `.sauce-backup` CLOSED 2026-05-07

Final of three multi-cycle Sauce rebrand renames. Tree 1 (workshop clone `Beacon/` → `pantry/`) shipped in v0.23.0; Tree 3 (runtime plumbing `Docs/Meta/` → `ranch/`) shipped in v0.24.0; **Tree 2 (consumer module namespace `beacon/<module>/` → `spice/<module>/`) ships here.** The `pantry/` + `ranch/` + `spice/` namespace tripod is now the canonical layout. Single source-of-truth at `install.js:250` (one-char change `` `beacon/${...}` `` → `` `spice/${...}` ``) controls the namespace prefix; blueprint manifests UNCHANGED (already declare bare `module_directory: "<name>"`). Bundles `.beacon-backup` → `.sauce-backup` suffix rename under user's fresh-start posture (no legacy consumer state to preserve). v0.23.0 + v0.24.0 "preserve `.beacon-backup` across rename" lessons retired. **Cycle shape:** 3 stages, master-driven on rename mechanics + harness updates. **S1** (`e379436`) TDD-RED baseline updates 3 harnesses (run-helper-cases / run-bootstrap / run-renderer) to expect post-rename state — fixture module names + .sauce-backup expectations + 9 fixture paths in renderer. Internal harness tmpdir labels (`mkdtempSync("beacon-caseXX-")`) + cards-mechanism filename `beacon-cards.js` + `BEACON_CARDS_FILE` constant + `'beacon-cards'` selector PRESERVED (not renamed per user choice). **S2** (`88fc46c`) atomic mass rename across 39 files / +184 / -169: install.js (single L250 prefix + 6 jsdoc/comments + 17 `.beacon-backup` → `.sauce-backup` sites), bootstrap.js + bootstrap-lib (6 .beacon-backup sites), 20 helper .js files across 5 blueprints (daily/project/finance/meetings/trips), 4 templates (Kanban Card / Project Board / Task Board Card / Trip Board), 8 blueprint manifest description + post-install message strings, install.md (Upgrading-from-v0.24.x section + .sauce-backup), how.md (.sauce-backup), landmines.md #11 final wording (drop forecast block; codify spice/<module>/; success block; legacy-violations marked RESOLVED + historical) + #12 history block (allowlist UNCHANGED at 12; backup-suffix renamed). **Two-stage subagent review** (spec ✅ APPROVED no gaps; quality APPROVED with 1 NIT applied — landmine #11 legacy-violations historical context). **Pre-existing dogfood drift** (`.obsidian/*` + `blueprints i want.md`) excluded from atomic commit per v0.23.0 lesson g. **All 5 harnesses GREEN** post-S2: bootstrap 45/0, cli 20/0, install-sh 14/0, helper-cases 387/0, renderer exit 0. **Manual acceptance gate PASSED** — fresh `<workshop>/scratch/7/` curl-bootstrap (file:// transport) installs 8 mechanisms + 8 blueprints under `spice/<module>/`; daily-notes.json:folder = `spice/daily`; stub md5 invariant `ea23aa812503bfca66359d3b2b239ba8` HOLDS (Tree 2 doesn't change stub body); `sauce status` reports "Drift: none"; **Daily Notes "Open today's daily note" SUCCEEDS on macOS APFS** (PRIMARY user-environment validation, user-confirmed PASS). **Reserved-headroom: 1 review-surfaced NIT** (12th data point; fourth MIXED case after v0.22.1 / v0.23.0 / v0.24.0). Quality reviewer caught the legacy-violations historical-context gap pre-commit. Spec reviewer ✅ no gaps. Workshop_version 0.24.0 → 0.25.0 MINOR (mirrors v0.23.0/v0.24.0 Tree-rename precedent; BREAKING for v0.24.x consumers; one-time wipe + reinstall migration documented in install.md upgrade section). **NEW lessons (v0.25.0):** (a) **Tree 2 was the cheapest of the three rename cycles** — single source-of-truth prefix at install.js:250 + bare names in blueprint manifests meant the core code change was 1 line; rest was mass-replace across helpers/templates/docs. Lesson: when designing a multi-namespace platform, put the prefix application at ONE source-of-truth (the install pipeline), not in every consumer-facing manifest. (b) **fresh-start posture eliminates migration-helper complexity** — without legacy consumer state to preserve, no `applyTemplaterFolderTemplates` / `daily-notes.json:folder` migration helpers needed. Saved an entire CF class. Lesson: when locking namespace renames at brainstorm, ASK explicitly about consumer-state-preservation; the constraint dictates whether the cycle is mass-replace + reinstall (cheap) or mass-replace + migration-helpers (medium-cost like v0.24.0 CF-3). (c) **`.beacon-backup` retention was conditional, not permanent** — v0.23.0 + v0.24.0 lessons preserved the suffix because consumer vaults had on-disk backup files. Once user adopts fresh-start, those constraints retire. Lesson: backup-suffix conventions are not platform-version-stable forever; they're stable until the project takes a brand-rebrand-AND-consumer-reset window. (d) **Quality reviewer caught 1 NIT (landmine #11 legacy-violations historical-context wording)** — spec compliance was perfect; quality found that "Known violations (legacy, awaiting future cycles)" header was stale (violations had been RESOLVED). Lesson reaffirmed: spec review + quality review serve different functions; never skip either. (e) **Internal harness tmpdir labels (`mkdtempSync("beacon-caseXX-")`) + cards-mechanism filename + `BEACON_CARDS_FILE` constant survived the brand rename intentionally** — these are NOT user-visible namespace references; they're internal fixtures. Lesson: brand-rename sed scope discipline (v0.23.0 lesson b) extends to "what NOT to rename" — internal-only tokens that share a prefix-string with the renamed namespace must be enumerated upfront. (f) **All 8 blueprint manifest.json description + post-install message strings carried `beacon/<module>/` references** — these are consumer-visible at install time (via Notice). Plan's call-site inventory should always grep `description` + `message` strings in manifests; not just code. (g) **`Docs/install.md` upgrade section is the canonical fresh-start migration recipe** — under user's fresh-start stance, the recipe is `rm -rf <vault>/<old-namespace>` + `sauce update --force`. Mirrors v0.23.0 + v0.24.0 upgrade-section pattern. (h) **Reserved-headroom precedent extends to 12 data points** — fourth MIXED case (v0.22.1, v0.23.0, v0.24.0, v0.25.0). Pure-rename cycles draw 1-3 review-surfaced CFs; user-confirmed acceptance gate as final close-step continues to be load-bearing. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-07-v0.25.0-tree2-namespace-rename-result.md`.

## v0.26.0 first-run robustness CLOSED 2026-05-07

Closes 4 P0 foundation bugs that bricked fresh installs through v0.25.x. **P0-1** wizard "Edit subscription" double-wrap fix (root cause `bootstrap.js:344` + mirror `cmd-wizard.js:48` — `payload.mechanisms.map(name => find(x => x.name === name))` treated already-objects as bare strings → produced `{name: <object>, version: "0.0.0"}` nested-wrap entries causing 16 `[object Object]` skip notices and exit 1 on every subsequent install); fix via NEW exported `_normalizeSubscriptionFile` + private `_coerceSubscriptionEntry` helpers in `wizard.js`; production writeback rewired in BOTH `bootstrap.js` (re-run flow) AND `cmd-wizard.js` (sauce wizard verb); dead `mergeSubscription` removed. **P0-2** NEW helper `scaffoldFoundationalPluginData(tp, manifest, workshopPath, variables, history, git)` in `install.js` materializes Templater data.json defaults at install time (closes "6 silent helper-skips on fresh install" symptom — applyTemplaterHotkeys + applyTemplaterFolderTemplates skip across validator/audit/to-do/journal/meetings/finance because data.json doesn't exist yet, daily-note Templater placeholders never render). NEW `FOUNDATIONAL_PLUGIN_DEFAULTS` registry (Templater-only at v0.26.0); wired into installItem between `applyExternalPlugins` and `applyTemplaterHotkeys`. Helper count 10 → 11. **P0-3** convenience added to `DEFAULT_MECHANISMS_CHECKED` so fresh non-interactive installs get DataviewJS + copy-path hotkeys by default. **P0-4** atomic lowercase sweep ranch/{Templater,Scripts,Templates,Views,Content} → lowercase via macOS APFS `git mv X X_tmp && git mv X_tmp x` workaround + sed across 65 files (mechanisms / blueprints / harness / docs / workshop self-state) + Docs/install.md upgrade section + NEW landmine #19 (platform-managed dir names lowercase + OUT-of-scope exceptions enumerated explicitly: MM-MMMM date-routed, assets/themes/Baseline/ vendored, user-facing note filenames, Docs/plans + Docs/prompts historical, pantry/, spice/). Landmines 18 → 19. **Cycle shape:** 4 stages + 2 in-cycle CFs surfaced from S4 acceptance smoke. **S1** (`4bbfda6`) TDD-RED baseline: C15 wizard double-wrap + SF1-SF5 scaffold-foundational + C14 convenience-regression-guard + lowercase-path expectations across run-helper-cases + run-install. **S2** (`5a4d630`) atomic implementation: spec review PASS first try; quality review CHANGES-REQUESTED 1 CRITICAL (C-1 cmd-wizard.js test-hook seam — would have shipped latent test-mode TypeError) + 3 IMPORTANT (I-1 _coerceSubscriptionEntry skip-unknown mirroring _buildSubscriptionEntries; I-2 drop redundant `time` field; I-3 wrap getBasePath in try/catch) + 1 NIT all applied pre-commit. **S3** (`fb092a0`) atomic lowercase sweep + S3 follow-up (`dea7c41`) .gitignore extensions (`*.sauce-backup`, `*.bak`, `ranch/scripts/accent-button/`) + git rm --cached on 3 inadvertently-committed dogfood-drift files; spec review PASS first try (1 NIT bundled — ranch/Content); quality review APPROVED zero CRITICAL/IMPORTANT. **S4 CF-1** (`9065f5c`) acceptance smoke surfaced FOUNDATIONAL_PLUGIN_DEFAULTS missing array fields downstream helpers expect (enabled_templates_hotkeys + startup_templates + enable_system_commands); registry extended; SF1-SF5 unchanged + still GREEN. **S4 CF-2** (`c0a622c`) manual Obsidian smoke surfaced daily-note tags Type Mismatch — vault_identity_tag from path.basename("scratch/11") = "11" parsed as YAML int + invalid as Obsidian tag (numbers-only rejected); two-layer fix: NEW `_deriveVaultIdentityTag` helper prefixes numbers-only inputs with "vault-"; daily-template.md placeholder + Templater date both quoted. **All 5 harnesses GREEN:** cli 20/0 → 27/0 (+1 case C15, +6 sub-asserts), helper-cases 387/0 → 407/0 (+5 cases SF1-SF5, +20 sub-asserts), bootstrap 45/0, install-sh 14/0, renderer exit 0. **Acceptance gate PASSED on fresh `<workshop>/scratch/11/`** (curl-bootstrap file:// transport): 6 community plugins enabled, all 3 hotkeys bound (Cmd+- / Cmd+= / Cmd+[), DataviewJS on, Templater data.json scaffolded with all expected fields + folder-templates pointing at lowercase ranch/templates/, 8 blueprints materialized at spice/<module>/, ranch/{rules,scripts,templater,templates,views} all lowercase, audit script `find pantry ranch spice -type d \| grep -E '/[A-Z]'` returns empty. Manual Obsidian-runtime smoke user-confirmed PASS post-CF-2: daily Templater placeholders resolve (no `<%` literals), tags YAML valid (no Type Mismatch + no red strikethrough), all 8 blueprints' nav-buttons fire. **Reserved-headroom precedent:** 13 data points; 3rd MIXED case (v0.22.1 / v0.23.0 / v0.26.0). Workshop_version 0.25.0 → 0.26.0 MINOR (USER APPROVED; matches v0.21.0/v0.22.0/v0.23.0/v0.24.0/v0.25.0 first-of-kind precedent — new helper + new landmine + breaking-rename = MINOR). Allowlist UNCHANGED at 12 paths. Stub md5 invariant `ea23aa812503bfca66359d3b2b239ba8` UNCHANGED. **NEW lessons (v0.26.0):** (a) **scaffold helpers must include EVERY field downstream helpers read** — CF-1 root cause: design Section 3 specified 4 minimal fields; production install ran applyTemplaterHotkeys + applyTemplaterFolderTemplates which expect array fields; helpers errored on read. Generalization: scaffold registries must be authored from downstream-consumer perspective, not just helper-contract minimum. Future scaffold helpers should grep downstream helper bodies for every field they consume. (b) **numbers-only inputs need explicit handling at every layer** — CF-2 root cause: vault_identity_tag defaulted to path.basename(vaultPath).toLowerCase() without rejecting numbers-only; combined with unquoted YAML placeholder produced Type Mismatch + invalid Obsidian tag. Two-layer fix: template-body YAML quoting + input-derivation prefix logic. _deriveVaultIdentityTag helper is reusable. (c) **acceptance smoke against literally-named adversarial vault dirs surfaces edge cases** — scratch/11 numeric basename surfaced CF-2; prior cycles' scratch/2..scratch/7 numeric basenames had been latent since v0.3.0 daily template authoring. Future smoke-target naming should rotate adversarial inputs. (d) **two-stage subagent review continues to be load-bearing** — 6 data points (v0.20.0/v0.22.1/v0.23.0/v0.24.0/v0.25.0/v0.26.0). S2 quality reviewer caught CRITICAL test-hook seam pre-commit; S3 spec reviewer caught NIT F1 (ranch/Content) bundled in-cycle. (e) **pantry-clone source mutation is a separate latent bug** — investigation surfaced install.js writes back to pantry/'s daily-template.md source-of-truth file during install (workshop source remains intact; mutation self-heals on next sauce update). Carry to v0.26.x. (f) **in-cycle re-process bump rule (#16) didn't fire on this cycle** — workshop_version stayed at 0.25.0 across S2/S3/S4 until cycle close; rule applies for cycles that bump version mid-stream, not when version is static across all CFs. (g) **reserved-headroom precedent extends to 13 data points; 3rd MIXED case** — pure-additive cycles draw zero CFs; mixed cycles draw 1-3 review-surfaced or smoke-surfaced CFs caught + fixed pre-close. (h) **explicit `git add <specific-files>` preferred over `git add -A`** for staging atomic commits to honor cycle-time-accuracy posture (v0.23.0 lesson g reaffirmed). v0.26.0's `git add -A` in S3 picked up untracked drift; `dea7c41` follow-up commit recovered via .gitignore + git rm --cached. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-07-v0.26.0-first-run-robustness-result.md`.

Workshop_version 0.26.0 → 0.26.1 PATCH (USER APPROVED; matches v0.21.1/v0.18.x precedent — additive helper + additive schema + additive plugin baseline + new CLI verb without breaking flows = PATCH). **Reserved-headroom precedent extends to 14 data points; 4th MIXED case** (v0.22.1 / v0.23.0 / v0.26.0 / v0.26.1). **NEW lessons (v0.26.1):** (a) **plugin id pre-flight probe** is reusable for any future `foundational_plugins[]` expansion. (b) **applyAppSettings** is the canonical workshop-level helper template for any future cycle that needs a declared knob in `.obsidian/<file>.json`. (d) **parallel-session git race during S1 commit** — when user uses `/remote-control` orchestration with multiple sessions, git lock contention is real; multi-session workflow + git-lock recovery is a v0.26.x candidate doc. (g) **wizard auto-add scope question resolved by user** during quality review surface — pattern: ambiguous design wording should be surfaced as user-resolution question rather than implementer judgment call. (h) **bundled multi-feature PATCH cycles where each feature has clear precedent reliably MIXED, not first-of-kind** — small reserved headroom. Two-stage S2.10 subagent review surfaced 9 Important + 10 Minor issues; I-8 (`[ ]` whitespace tolerance) + I-9 (stable-sort violations within blueprint bucket by file/rule for cross-machine reproducibility) applied inline before S3; remaining 7 Important + 10 Minor issues deferred as v0.29.1 carries (I-1 glob `?` escape; I-2/I-3 BOM/CRLF doc nits; I-4 BLUEPRINT_MODULE_DIRS → `module_directory` field on each blueprint entry in `ranch/platform-installed.json` populated by install.js at install time; I-5 `_runForTest` mixes process.stdout side-effects with return value; I-6 `when.frontmatter` scalar-only equality; I-7 rule-runner WeakMap regex caching; M-1..M-10 polish). Mechanism count UNCHANGED at 9; blueprint count UNCHANGED at 9; helper count UNCHANGED at 12; allowlist UNCHANGED at 13; landmines `20 → 21`; stub md5 invariant `ea23aa812503bfca66359d3b2b239ba8` UNCHANGED. **9 v0.29.1 carries:** I-1 + I-2 + I-3 + I-4 + I-5 + I-6 + I-7 (S2.10 review-deferred) plus DRY frontmatter parser extraction (`platform/lib/frontmatter.js` — v0.28.0 NIT-1 carry continuation), shared rule-runner core extraction between in-vault validator + CLI audit (deferred from v0.29.0 design § 5), sub-section + task-note rule_fragments for trips/project, per-vault audit allowlist (`ranch/audit-allowlist.json` — deferred design § 2), `rule_fragments[]` for journal / to-do / boards / finance blueprints. Cycle-shape: 6 stages (S0 brainstorm → S1 design+plan → S2 TDD-RED+GREEN+S2.10 review → S3 dogfood+barebones smokes → S4 docs+version → S5 close). Single bundled S2 commit (`7df0394`) for atomic CF-1 through CF-4 + lockstep edits; S2.10 review fixes in `57e0c99`; CF-5 in `ddb8d6f`. Workshop_version 0.28.0 → 0.29.0 MINOR. **NEW lessons (v0.29.0):** (a) **hardcoded blueprint→module_directory map (`BLUEPRINT_MODULE_DIRS` at walker.js:58) is a v0.29.1 carry** — cleaner posture is `module_directory` field per blueprint entry in `ranch/platform-installed.json` populated by install.js at install time; the singular-vs-plural mismatch (`project` blueprint → `spice/projects/` dir) is the only current entry but the lookup table will grow as more blueprints diverge. (b) **install.js applyRuleFragment array-support fix surfaced FIRST CYCLE to ship multiple rule_fragments per blueprint** — pre-existing overwrite bug never hit because no blueprint had non-empty `rule_fragments` before v0.29.0; latent-bug-detection-on-first-real-use pattern echoes v0.6.0's API-contract-quoting lesson. (c) **validator vs audit walker dual-implementation is intentional for v0.29.0** — shared rule-runner core extraction deferred to v0.29.1 once both pipelines have run against real data; premature extraction without observed shared call patterns risks the wrong abstraction. (d) **`readNote` helper in run-audit.js had to special-case empty-array `[]`** because YAML's `key:` (no value) parses as null, not empty list — Sauce templates emit `key: []` so the parser must distinguish; codifies the v0.16.0 frontmatter-parser-variance lesson at audit-walker layer. (e) **CF-5 (report.js blueprint name normalization) wasn't caught in S2.10 review** because tests use string arrays exclusively; only real-vault smoke against accuris-sauce surfaced the object-shape gap — affirms **S3 dogfood is non-optional even after harness GREEN + two-stage subagent review GREEN**; harness fixtures are scaffolding, not reality. (f) **5 in-cycle CFs hits the upper bound of the 3-5 design-time estimate** — first-of-its-kind cycles (NEW verb + NEW core dir + NEW additive schema fields applied across 5 blueprints) reliably draw 3-5 CFs; reserved-headroom precedent extends to 16 data points. Result writeup at `Docs/plans/2026-05-08-v0.29.0-vault-audit-result.md`.

Workshop CLAUDE.md gains three marker pairs (`directory-map`, `resolvers`, `skills-index`). NEW harness `platform/test/run-claude-surface.js` (171 sub-asserts).
