Three highest-traffic blueprints adopt `claude_surface[]`. `project@1.4.1 → 1.5.0` MINOR — NEW `/project` Claude Code slash command at `platform/blueprints/project/commands/project.md` (~120 lines documenting `spice/projects/<slug>/` layout, hub navigation, workstream tagging, audit/refresh CLI) + NEW `new-project` programmatic SKILL.md at `platform/blueprints/project/skills/new-project/SKILL.md` (~60 lines: inputs/steps/outputs/failure-modes/audit-receipt) + `claude_md_row` resolvers row (Projects → /project) + `skills_dir: ".claude/skills/project"`. `daily@0.2.6 → 0.3.0` MINOR — same shape with `/daily` + `open-today` SKILL.md + Daily resolvers row. `meetings@0.3.1 → 0.4.0` MINOR — same shape with `/meetings` + `new-meeting` SKILL.md + Meetings resolvers row. **S1 foundation fixes folded in:** workshop subscription drift reconciled (`ranch/platform-subscription.json` workshop_version 0.21.1 → 0.32.0; validator 0.1.1 → 0.1.2; nav-buttons 2.5.3 → 2.6.0); CS-SUB-3 verb-existence guard generalized — extracted `walkClaudeSurfaceBodies(workshopRoot, manifestEntries)` + `scanBodyForBadVerbs(bodies, verbs, documentedNonVerb)` helpers; `caseCSSUB3VerbsExist` body now walks every mechanism + blueprint manifest with `claude_surface[]` (was hard-scanned to platform-claude only); NEW `CS-SUB-3b` sentinel sub-case proves out-of-platform-claude drift is caught; drive-by drift fix in cowork's `bootstrap-vault/SKILL.md` line 34 (`sauce install cowork` → `sauce update` since `install` isn't a real CLI verb); workshop subscription gains project + daily + meetings entries for end-to-end dogfood. **Workshop materialization housekeeping commits per stage** captured first-time blueprint subscription side-effects: ranch/templates/<TemplateFiles>.md + ranch/scripts/<bp>/*.js + ranch/rules/<bp>.json + ranch/nav-buttons-registry.json + spice/<module>/<HubNote>.md + .obsidian/{hotkeys,daily-notes}.json (allowlisted per landmine #12). Wave-1 .claude/ leftover backfill rolled into S2 housekeeping commit. **Whole-suite GREEN at v0.33.0 close:** claude-surface 171/0 (UNCHANGED count — CS-SUB-3 body rewrite was a net-wash: lost 2 inline counters, added CS-SUB-3b's 2; the walker now scans every manifest), helper-cases 511/0 (+3 for claude_surface install path coverage across the three new blueprints), bootstrap 58/0, cli 62/0, install-sh 14/0, migrate 104/0, audit 68/0, cowork-smoke 92/0, renderer 70 cases exit 0. Workshop_version 0.32.0 → 0.33.0 MINOR. **Stage commits:** S0.2 `a523265` (plan lock) → S1.1 `f892235` (subscription drift) → S1.2 `c180b83` (CS-SUB-3 generalize) → S2 `becbaee` (project 1.5.0) + `e043a61` (S2 housekeeping) → S3 `9967502` (daily 0.3.0) + `b9f00c5` (S3 housekeeping) → S4 `9974431` (meetings 0.4.0) + `13c2833` (S4 housekeeping) → S5 v0.33.0 close commit + tag. **NEW FIX-LATER-NOTEs:** (a) `## Skills index` marker block in CLAUDE.md stays at header-only — the renderer doesn't auto-populate from `kind: "skill"` entries; blueprints would need to emit explicit `claude_md_row` entries with `"table": "skills-index"`; wave 3 candidate; (b) S2 implementer flagged plan-author gap: per-blueprint S<N>.7 commit lists in the wave-2 plan didn't anticipate first-time-subscription workshop materialization side-effects (templates/scripts/rules/hub-note/nav-buttons-registry) — addressed inline with per-stage housekeeping commits; future wave plans should bake "first-time subscription => materialization commit follow-up" into the plan template. **NEW lessons (v0.33.0):** (a) **`sauce update` uses git-reset semantics** — runs `git fetch origin main && git reset --hard origin/main` inside the workshop's pantry/ before re-running the installer; the right pattern for self-install verify-before-commit is `node -e 'require("./platform/bootstrap.js").phaseRunInstaller({ vaultPath: process.cwd() })'` which skips the git operations and runs the installer against on-disk state; reinforces landmine #18 (pantry/ is git-managed, never hand-edit; recovery via `sauce update --force`). (d) **`/install`, `/upgrade` are slash commands, NOT CLI verbs** — slash commands invoke skill bodies that shell to `sauce update`; CS-SUB-3 catches authors who confuse the two. ## v0.36.0 sauce homebrew distribution (pantry out of vault) CLOSED 2026-05-13

Distribution + CLI cycle — no platform catalogue changes (mechanism + blueprint counts UNCHANGED). Workshop_version 0.33.1 → 0.36.0 MINOR (skips 0.34.0 / 0.35.0 — the wave-3 cohesion cycle slot at 0.34.0 remains open; 0.36.0 was branched from 0.33.1 to ship the distribution change as a focused, isolatable cycle). **Architecture move:** `pantry/` (the workshop clone that consumer vaults previously kept inside the vault at `<vault>/pantry/`) leaves the synced vault and ships via Homebrew into `$(brew --prefix)/Cellar/sauce/<v>/libexec/`. The `sauce` bin shim is now version-aware and resolves `SAUCE_DIR` in two modes: (1) brew-installed default — `SAUCE_DIR=$libexec` — used by anyone who `brew install willfell/sauce/sauce`; (2) dev mode — `SAUCE_DIR=$(readlink ~/.sauce/active-pantry)` — toggled by `sauce link <pantry>` and reverted by `sauce unlink`. Pure-function mirror lives at `platform/cli/resolve-sauce-dir.js` so the resolution logic is unit-testable (SH1-SH3 cases in run-cli.js).

**NEW CLI verbs (6):**
- `sauce vault add|list|remove` — per-machine registry maintenance against `~/.sauce/vaults.json`. `add` validates the vault path exists + has `ranch/platform-config.json`; idempotent. `list` prints a table with path / workshop_version / last-install-at. `remove` accepts path or registry-id; failure-loud on unknown.
- `sauce reinstall --all | --vault <path>` — re-runs the installer across registered vaults. `--all` walks the registry; `--vault` runs against a single path (also accepts unregistered paths for one-shot installs).
- `sauce migrate-layout` — 8-step state machine that migrates a legacy `<vault>/pantry/` consumer onto the new layout: (1) detect legacy pantry, (2) verify pantry git is clean (refuse-loud on dirty tree), (3) capture pantry HEAD sha for rollback, (4) `git mv pantry/ ../sauce-pantry-tmp/` (out-of-vault), (5) update `ranch/platform-config.json` paths if needed, (6) register vault in `~/.sauce/vaults.json`, (7) attempt `brew install willfell/sauce/sauce` if not present, (8) run `sauce reinstall --vault $PWD`. Rollback on failure restores the pantry/ in-vault layout from the captured sha.
- `sauce doctor` — platform health check: node version (≥18); `~/.sauce/vaults.json` exists + valid JSON; `~/.sauce/active-pantry` symlink resolves (or is absent — both OK); `which sauce` matches brew shim; `$(brew --prefix)/Cellar/sauce/<v>/libexec/platform/cli/sauce-cli.js` exists. Each check prints PASS/FAIL/SKIP with a one-line reason.
- `sauce link <pantry>` — sets `~/.sauce/active-pantry` to point at a working tree (dev mode). Validates the target has `platform/cli/sauce-cli.js`. Atomic via `ln -sfn`.
- `sauce unlink` — removes `~/.sauce/active-pantry`. Idempotent (no-op if absent).

**NEW helper modules:**
- `platform/cli/registry.js` — read/write `~/.sauce/vaults.json`. Atomic writes via tmp-file + rename; sidecar `.bak` preserves prior contents on every write. Schema validation: `{vaults: [{path, registered_at, workshop_version, last_install_at}]}`. Malformed-input guards: missing-file → fresh registry; non-JSON → fail-loud with backup-of-corrupted preserved; non-object/non-array shape → fail-loud.
- `platform/cli/resolve-sauce-dir.js` — pure-function mirror of bin shim logic. Inputs: `{home, libexec, fsExists}`. Returns `{sauceDir, mode}` where mode is `'dev' | 'brew'`. Used by `sauce doctor` to explain which pantry will be used + tested in isolation via SH1-SH3 cases.

**install.js + bootstrap.js additions:**
- `sauce update` now prints a migration hint when legacy-layout symptoms are detected (`<vault>/pantry/` present but `~/.sauce/active-pantry` resolving elsewhere; or no `~/.sauce/vaults.json` entry for the vault).
- `sauce bootstrap` auto-registers the vault in `~/.sauce/vaults.json` on success (`--no-register` opts out for CI / one-shot scenarios).
- `sauce install --register` writes a fresh registry entry without running bootstrap (for vaults migrated by `sauce migrate-layout`).

**NEW test harnesses (2):**
- `platform/test/run-registry.js` — 16 sub-asserts. R1-R4 (add/list/remove + idempotent-add), R5-R8 (atomic-write semantics: tmp-rename, sidecar .bak preserved, no partial-write corruption, concurrent-write safety), R9-R12 (malformed-input guards: missing-file → empty, non-JSON → fail-loud + backup, non-array vaults → fail-loud, missing path field → fail-loud), R13-R16 (schema migration from absent fields, version-bump touch-up of last_install_at, remove-by-id vs remove-by-path, registry survives sauce update of self).
- `platform/test/run-migrate-layout.js` — 18 sub-asserts. ML1-ML8 (each of the 8 state machine steps individually green-path), ML9-ML12 (rollback on each abort-able step: dirty-tree, mv-failure, brew-not-present, install-failure), ML13-ML16 (idempotent re-runs: already-migrated vault → no-op, partially-migrated → resume from last sha, brew already installed → skip step 7, vault already registered → skip step 6), ML17-ML18 (failure-loud guards: vault path doesn't exist, vault has no `pantry/`).

**run-cli.js delta:** 62 → 93 sub-asserts (+31). New cases: V1-V4 (vault add/list/remove + unknown-verb guard), I1-I3 (reinstall --all, --vault, missing-flag guard), M1-M5 (migrate-layout: legacy-detect, clean-tree precondition, register, brew-skip, full E2E), D1-D3 (doctor: green/yellow/red cases), L1-L4 (link valid pantry, link missing target, unlink present, unlink absent), SH1-SH3 (resolve-sauce-dir: brew default, dev mode active, dev mode broken symlink), U1-U2 (update migration-hint: legacy detected, modern layout silent), IR1 (install --register writes registry entry).

**Whole-suite GREEN at v0.36.0 close:** claude-surface 171/0, helper-cases 511/0, bootstrap 58/0, **cli 93/0 (+31)**, install-sh 14/0, migrate 104/0, audit 68/0, cowork-smoke 92/0, renderer 70 cases exit 0, **NEW registry 16/0**, **NEW migrate-layout 18/0**. Total **1145 sub-asserts plus 70 renderer cases** (+65 vs v0.33.0's 1080: +16 registry + 18 migrate-layout + 31 run-cli). Harness count 9 → 11.

**Distribution:** NEW sibling tap repo `willfell/homebrew-sauce` at `git@github-personal:willfell/homebrew-sauce.git`. Initial `Formula/sauce.rb` ships v0.36.0: depends on node; `libexec.install Dir["*"]`; `npm install --omit=dev --prefix libexec`; writes the active-pantry-aware bin shim (HEREDOC); `chmod 0755 bin/sauce`. Test block asserts `sauce help` emits text. User installs via `brew install willfell/sauce/sauce` (tap auto-added).

**Fixes:** the dirty-tree + sync-corruption second-machine failures from pre-v0.36 layout where `pantry/` commits inside the consumer vault would race with iCloud / Obsidian Sync. Pre-v0.36, every `sauce update` would `git pull` inside the synced vault, causing iCloud to checkpoint a partial tree mid-rebase. Post-v0.36, the pantry lives outside the synced vault entirely; only `ranch/`, `spice/`, and `.claude/` materialized artifacts cross the sync boundary.

**Workshop self-install:** `sauce reinstall --vault $PWD` from the workshop dev-repo green; workshop subscription `workshop_version: 0.36.0` mirrors manifest. Tag `v0.36.0` annotated; pushed to `git@github-personal:willfell/sauce.git`.

**Stage commits:** S1-S8 sub-asserts grew incrementally — see commit log between v0.33.1 and the v0.36.0 close commit. S9 (this cycle close): `feat: v0.36.0 — homebrew distribution (pantry out of vault)`.

**NEW landmines / lessons (v0.36.0):** No new numbered landmines this cycle. New lessons captured: (a) **pantry/-in-vault is sync-hostile** — any git working tree inside an iCloud-backed Obsidian vault will eventually race with the sync daemon; move git-managed content outside the sync boundary; (b) **two-resolution-mode bin shims are dev-friendly** — `~/.sauce/active-pantry` symlink toggle preserves brew-installed mode as the default while letting workshop development use a local working tree without re-installing; mirrors Python venv / nvm / pyenv activation idiom; (c) **registry as per-machine source of truth** — `~/.sauce/vaults.json` is the canonical list of installed consumers on this machine; `sauce reinstall --all` becomes trivial; future cycles can add `sauce upgrade --all` for cross-vault version migrations; (d) **migration tooling as a 1st-class CLI verb** — `sauce migrate-layout` codifies the pre-v0.36 → v0.36 transition as an idempotent, resumable, rollback-able state machine; pattern reusable for future layout-changing cycles. Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-13-v0.36.0-sauce-homebrew-distribution-plan.md`.

## v0.36.1 CLOSED 2026-05-12

Patch bundle from v0.36.0 end-of-cycle code review. Two critical (C1, C2), three important (I3, I4, I5), three lower-priority bumps (LP1-LP3).

**C1 — Test harness pollution of `~/.sauce/vaults.json`.** `run-cli.js` cases C11/C12/C13 (pre-existing bootstrap tests) invoked `cli.dispatch(["bootstrap", ...])`, which calls `cmd-bootstrap.js`'s `registry.add(ctx.vaultPath)`. Without `withTempHome`, each run wrote to the developer's real `~/.sauce/vaults.json`. 30 stale `/var/folders/.../beacon-cli-*` entries accumulated pre-fix. Fix: wrapped C11/C12/C13 in `withTempHome` (matches IR1/IR2 pattern from S7); added an end-of-suite invariant in `main()` that snapshots production-registry `mtime + size` pre-run and asserts they're unchanged post-run. Defensive guard: catches any future regression where a new test forgets `withTempHome` around a registry write path. Ran the one-shot cleanup script after the fix landed to drop the accumulated stale entries.

**C2 — `beacon-cli-` mkdtemp prefix rename.** Cosmetic. `withTempVault` mkdtemped under `beacon-cli-XYZ` (legacy name pre-v0.23.0 sauce rebrand). Renamed to `sauce-cli-` so registry log lines, doctor warnings, and any future debug output read consistently.

**I3 — `_auditStrict` is a production no-op.** `platform/cli/cmd-migrate-layout.js:67-73` always returned `{ok:true}` outside tests because `cmd-audit.js` doesn't expose a programmatic strict-check function. Meant `sauce migrate-layout --purge` would always delete the archive on real runs because the audit gate was fake. Fix: dropped the fake gate semantics — production stub now returns `{ok:true, _stub:true}` and the run() function checks `audit._stub` to emit "Audit: skipped (production audit integration deferred — see FIX-LATER-NOTE)" instead of "Audit: OK". `--purge` precondition relaxes to "step 1-7 success" (implicit by virtue of reaching the audit step; any earlier step throws on failure). Test harnesses still inject `ctx._auditStrict` to exercise gate semantics. Wiring to real audit deferred to a future cycle (FIX-LATER-NOTE).

**I4 — `pruneMissing` doesn't validate vault-ness.** `platform/cli/registry.js:50-58` only checked `fs.existsSync(v.path)`. If a user `mv`d a vault elsewhere but the path still existed as some other directory, `pruneMissing` kept it and `reinstall --all` would run the installer against a non-sauce dir. Fix: two-marker check — `ranch/platform-installed.json` (post-install) OR `ranch/platform-config.json` (pre-install) must be present. Updated R5 case to seed a `ranch/platform-config.json` marker so the test path qualifies. NEW R7 case asserts a path that exists but lacks ranch/ markers IS pruned. run-registry.js grows 16 → 19 sub-asserts (+R7 + 2 from R5 marker seeding).

**I5 — `cmd-link` accepts brew-installed pantry as link target.** `cmd-link.js` validated `platform/manifest.json` + `platform/cli/sauce-cli.js`, both of which exist in the brew-installed pantry. So `sauce link $(brew --prefix sauce)/libexec` succeeded, creating a confusing state where `brew upgrade` was defeated (the active-pantry symlink wins). Fix: NEW `_brewLibexec(ctx)` helper that resolves `brew --prefix sauce` and returns `<prefix>/libexec`. Mockable via `ctx._brewPrefix` for unit-testing. cmd-link.js refuses to symlink if target equals brewLibexec. NEW L5 case dispatches via `cli.dispatch(["link", fakeLibexec], {_brewPrefix: () => fakeBrew})` and asserts the refusal error names "brew-installed". `sauce-cli.js` `link` verb dispatch now threads `opts` as ctx (previously `null`) so the test hook reaches cmd-link.

**LP1-LP3:** `package.json` `name: beacon-workshop → sauce`, `version: 0.21.0 → 0.36.1`, description scrubbed of "Beacon vault platform" and legacy `run-install` reference. `platform/manifest.json` `workshop_version: 0.36.0 → 0.36.1`, `date: "2026-05-12"`. `ranch/platform-subscription.json` `workshop_version: 0.36.0 → 0.36.1`.

**Bootstrap-via-brew fix:** commit `9048281` (post-v0.36.0 tag) bundled into v0.36.1 — `sauce bootstrap` now resolves the workshop from `__dirname` rather than `<vault>/pantry/`, which is required for fresh-vault bootstraps from the brew-installed shim.

**Whole-suite preflight delta:** run-cli.js sub-asserts 94 → 96 (+L5 +INV invariant), run-registry.js 16 → 19 (+R7 +R5 marker-seed). Other harnesses unchanged.

**Stage commits:** Single commit close — `feat: v0.36.1 — patch bundle from v0.36.0 cycle review`. Tag `v0.36.1` annotated; pushed to `git@github-personal:willfell/sauce.git` after user-confirmed `TAP_PR_TOKEN` provisioning.

**NEW landmines / lessons (v0.36.1):** No new numbered landmines. New lessons: (a) **test harnesses that touch HOME-rooted state MUST wrap in `withTempHome`** — even read-only-looking dispatch paths can transitively call `registry.add` / `registry.write`; the regression cost was 30 stale entries cluttering doctor output until the fix shipped; (b) **end-of-suite invariants are cheap insurance** — snapshot critical machine-global files (e.g. production registry mtime+size) at suite start, assert unchanged at suite end; catches future drift before it accumulates; (c) **"audit-gated" production code paths must be honest about what they're checking** — a stub that always returns `{ok:true}` for production is worse than no gate at all because it falsely advertises safety; either wire to the real check or relax the precondition to what's actually verifiable; (d) **dev-mode symlinks need refusal lists** — anywhere a CLI accepts a "point at X" arg, enumerate the X values that would silently break upgrades / break consumers and refuse them loudly; cheap to add at construction, expensive to debug six months in.

## v0.37.0 scratch blueprint CLOSED 2026-05-12

NEW `scratch@0.1.0` blueprint — one-click date-stamped quick-capture note from anywhere in Obsidian. Architecture mirrors the `journal` pattern with a three-level extension: `runTemplaterTemplate` nav-button action with `folder_date_pattern: "YYYY/MM-MMMM/YYYY-MM-DD"` (three-level via moment.format slash-as-literal) + `filename_date_pattern: "YYYY-MM-DD-HH-mm"` (timestamped). The scratch template's `<%* %>` block lazily creates a per-day index file `<DayName>-YYYY-MM-DD.md` via `tp.file.create_new` wrapped in try/catch for rapid-double-click race tolerance. Global hub at `spice/scratch/Scratch.md` renders day cards via NEW `ScratchHubCards` CustomJS class over BeaconCards row layout (dataview groups scratch notes by day); per-day index renders scratch list sorted by HH-mm via NEW `ScratchDayList` CustomJS class. Adopts `claude_surface[]` from day one — ships `/scratch` slash command (manifest entry kind=command, dest `.claude/commands/scratch.md`) + `new-scratch` SKILL.md (kind=skill, dest `{{skills_dir}}/new-scratch/SKILL.md` where `skills_dir: ".claude/skills/scratch"`) + Scratch resolvers row (kind=claude_md_row, path `{{module_directory}}` → resolves to `spice/scratch`). `depends_on` declares `platform-claude >=0.1.1` (consumes v0.33.1 row.path fix) + `nav-buttons >=2.6.0` + `customjs-guard >=1.0.0` + `cards >=0.2.4`. Pre-flight S0 verified: (S0.1) three-level folder_date_pattern composes correctly through `space-nav-buttons.js:338-340` since moment treats slashes as literal; (S0.2) `tp.file.create_new` race posture via try/catch on `/already exists|exists/i` is the established pattern; (S0.3) hub `files[]` entry follows project precedent — re-install overwrites user-edited hub (FIX-LATER candidate for `skip_if_exists` flag); (S0.4) USER APPROVAL granted for v0.37.0 clean-separation slot (v0.34.0 wave-3 cohesion still pending). Harness deltas: helper-cases 515 → 530 (+15 — 6 SHC-S cases + 9 TW1 trailing-whitespace lint hits auto-discovered on the 3 new scratch templates pre-S3); renderer 35 → 36 cases (+1 R-SCRATCH-NEW for three-level folder composition + HH-mm-suffixed filename + templater dispatch capture); audit 68 → 74 (+6 SA-S cases for scratch rule_fragment validation — required_frontmatter `type: scratch` + naming_pattern + scope.path_glob match + frontmatter_branch resolution). Whole-suite total **1170 sub-asserts + 36 renderer cases** across 12 harnesses (claude-surface 171, cowork-smoke 92, bootstrap 58, cli 97, install-sh 5, migrate 104, migrate-layout 18, registry 18, doctor-self 3 UNCHANGED). Workshop self-install verified clean via `phaseRunInstaller`; `/audit --claude-surface` reported `dead_path=0, orphan=0, stale_but_valid=4 (pre-existing on audit/install/upgrade/bootstrap mechanism bodies), consumer_edit_at_risk=0, aligned=20` (scratch's 3 entries all `aligned`; install incidentally resolved 2 pre-existing orphans by adding the hub note + sliding into the registry). Stage commits: S0 read-only investigations (no commits); S1.1-S1.7 = `79c2e82` (S1.1 manifest scaffold) + `4172d6f` (S1.2 scratch-note template) + `412894f` (S1.3 day-index template) + `0ebe000` (S1.4 hub template) + `cca5cc1` (S1.6 /scratch slash command) + `72837c1` (S1.7 new-scratch SKILL.md) + `819a3cc` (S1.5 register in workshop catalogue); S2.1-S2.2 = `c1fc783` (ScratchHubCards) + `edbddc1` (ScratchDayList); S3.1-S3.3 = `4409030` (SHC-S harness cases) + `bf08ce7` (renderer case) + `69440bb` (SA-S audit cases); S4.1-S4.3 = `ab266db` (workshop subscription) + `a72c341` (self-install spec-exact files) + `743c0ba` (materialization housekeeping). Mechanism count UNCHANGED at 10; blueprint count `10 → 11` (NEW scratch); helper count UNCHANGED at 13; allowlist UNCHANGED at 17 paths + CLAUDE.md marker carve-out; landmines UNCHANGED at 22; stub md5 invariant UNCHANGED. Workshop_version `0.36.1 → 0.37.0` MINOR. Tag `v0.37.0` annotated; `.github/workflows/release.yml` triggered on tag push auto-bumps `Formula/sauce.rb` in `willfell/homebrew-sauce` tap (~10s, opens PR). Consumer rollout deferred to post-cycle: `brew upgrade sauce && sauce reinstall --vault <path>` per machine, then add `{ "name": "scratch", "version": "0.1.0" }` to each consumer's `ranch/platform-subscription.json` blueprints[] (or `sauce update --force` post-edit). **NEW FIX-LATER-NOTEs:** (a) audit rule-runner `_compileGlob` in `platform/audit/rule-runner.js` is case-sensitive — `scope.path_glob: "Scratch-*.md"` doesn't match a `scratch-*.md` lowercase filename; case-insensitive matcher candidate for a future patch (surfaced when SA-S6 expected a lowercase-prefix violation but the rule's scope skipped the file entirely); (b) plan baselines for run-renderer/run-cli were stale (claimed 70/62 but actual 35/97 post-v0.36.1) — plan-baseline-drift after out-of-order cycle hops is a real risk worth a sanity-check step at S0 of any future cycle; (c) hub `files[]` entry has no `skip_if_exists` flag — re-install will overwrite a user-edited `spice/scratch/Scratch.md`; recommend probing installer schema for `skip_if_exists` support during wave-3 and adding flag to all hub entries (project's `Projects.md`, scratch's `Scratch.md`); (d) 4 pre-existing `stale_but_valid` findings on `audit.md` + `install.md` + `upgrade.md` + `bootstrap.md` command body version comments lag the registry (the body's `<!-- @claude-surface:version X.Y.Z -->` header is 0.0.1 behind the registry's version field) — small housekeeping cycle candidate (separate from scratch); (e) plan body's claude_surface manifest snippet specified `platform-claude >=0.1.0` but task description specified `>=0.1.1` — tighter constraint used since current platform-claude is at 0.1.1 (the v0.33.1 fix lives there); plan body should be updated for consistency in a future plan-edit pass. **NEW lessons (v0.37.0):** (a) **out-of-order cycle hops cause plan-baseline drift** — the v0.35.0 scratch plan was authored when workshop was at v0.33.0 with 9 harnesses; v0.36.0 + v0.36.1 jumped ahead and added 3 NEW harnesses (run-migrate-layout, run-registry, run-doctor-self) plus reshaped run-install-sh (14 → 5) and grew run-cli (+35). Plan baselines became fiction. Plans drafted ahead-of-time need a "sanity-check baselines against current state" step at S0 in any future re-execution. (b) **TW1 trailing-whitespace lint silently auto-discovers** new `platform/blueprints/<bp>/templates/*.md` files without explicit case registration — the +3 sub-asserts that arrived in run-helper-cases between S2.2 close (515) and S3.1 start (518) traced to the three new scratch templates being picked up by an existing wildcard glob. Net delta for S3.1 was +15 sub-asserts (6 cases × 2-3 inline asserts each) despite the plan's "+6" promise; the math reconciles when you separate "new cases" (6) from "total new sub-asserts" (15) — capture both numbers in future plans. (c) **out-of-order ship doesn't break ship order** — v0.36.0 cycle name advanced from 0.33.1 → 0.36.0 directly (skipped 0.34.0 + 0.35.0); v0.37.0 ships AFTER v0.36.1 without filling v0.34.0/v0.35.0 first. Cycle slots are claim-stakes, not strict ordering. v0.34.0 remains reserved for wave-3 cohesion; v0.35.0 is now retired (scratch took its conceptual slot at v0.37.0). (d) **claude_surface[] adoption from day one is cheaper than retrofitting** — scratch shipped with claude_surface[] alongside its initial v0.1.0; no migration cycle needed. Future new blueprints should mirror this pattern (manifest declares command + skill + claude_md_row from the start). (e) **brew release workflow is push-and-forget** — tagging `v0.37.0` and pushing the tag triggers `.github/workflows/release.yml` which auto-bumps `Formula/sauce.rb` in the tap repo within ~10s. No manual formula edits needed; consumer rollout becomes `brew upgrade sauce` + `sauce reinstall --vault <path>`. Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-v0.37.0-scratch-blueprint-plan.md`. Design: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-scratch-blueprint-design.md`.

## v0.38.0 deploy-confidence substrate CLOSED 2026-05-13

Builds the substrate for closing the "is the brewed sauce-after-upgrade actually OK against representative content?" gap. v0.36.x distribution shipped pantry-out-of-vault + brew + registry, but provided no automated assurance that a real-data vault survives `brew upgrade sauce && sauce reinstall`. This cycle wires that loop end-to-end at the bootstrap+seed+audit-clean bar (heavier bars — renderer-stub verification, upgrade-survival — explicitly deferred to follow-on cycles).

**NEW substrate (S1-S4):**
- `platform/seeder/` library (workshop-only; never installed into consumers): `helpers.js` (`emitFrontmatter` alphabetic-sort + ambiguous-YAML quoting, `writeNote` enforces module-directory invariant via `path.posix.normalize` prefix check, `fixedRng` Mulberry32 seeded by SHA256(`sauce-test-vault-v1:<bp>:<anchor>`), `substituteLenient` lenient-replace of `{{var}}`, `ModuleDirectoryEscapeError`); `declarative.js` (loads `seed.json` schema_version=1 kind="declarative"); `programmatic.js` (loads `seed.js`, makes ctx with `daysAgo(n)` + `makeDateLike(d).format(YYYY/MM/DD/MMMM)` + `renderTemplate(relPath, vars)` + `jitterTime(hhmm, ±N, rng)` + `pickFrom(arr, rng)`); `seeder.js` (`seedVault({workshopRoot, vaultPath, blueprints, anchorDate, dryRun})` dispatches declarative→programmatic→`{kind:"unknown", warning:"no seed contribution"}`).
- `platform/cli/cmd-seed.js` — `sauce seed [--vault] [--blueprint] [--anchor-date] [--reset] [--dry-run]`. Tilde-expansion on `--vault`. Vault resolution: `--vault` explicit, OR `registry.list()` single-vault rule, OR error. Wired into `sauce-cli.js` as context-free verb (parallels `vault` / `reinstall` / `link`). Help line in `cmd-help.js`. Dispatcher Usage line + `printUsage()` include `seed`.
- `vault_kind:"test"` NEW field on `ranch/platform-config.json`. Default absent = production-marked. `--reset` codepath gates: refuses non-test vault with `process.exit(2)`; on test vault, `rm -rf <vault>/spice/<module_directory>/` for each target blueprint (resolved from each blueprint's manifest), then falls through to additive `seedVault(...)`.
- `platform/test/run-seed.js` — 31 sub-asserts: HEL-1..4 (frontmatter quoting), MD-1..5 (writeNote module-dir guard + additive skip), LD-1..5 (declarative loader edge cases), PR-1..3b (programmatic loader + materialize), RNG-1..3 (`fixedRng` deterministic across calls + varies by (blueprint, anchor) tuple), DT-1..3 (`makeDateLike` YYYY-MM-DD / MM-MMMM / nested), R-1..3b (`--reset` gate via `execFileSync` subprocess — exit 2 on non-test vault; exit 0 + dir-nuked on test vault; --blueprint selectivity).

**Pilot seed contributions (S6-S8):**
- `platform/blueprints/project/seed/seed.json` α declarative — 3 representative projects (Acme-Migration active, North-Star-Refactor planning, Q1-2026-Audit archived). Each renders `templates/Project.md` with `{{slug}}/{{title}}/{{status}}` vars; template's uppercase `{{NAME}}/{{DATE}}/{{SLUG}}` placeholders survive `substituteLenient` as literals (accepted — audit-bar-ii doesn't validate body content semantically).
- `platform/blueprints/daily/seed/seed.js` β programmatic — 30 daily notes routed `YYYY/MM-MMMM/YYYY-MM-DD.md` ending at `--anchor-date`. RNG-driven `wakeup` jitter (`jitterTime("07:30", 15, rng)`) + `mood` (`pickFrom(["good","neutral","busy"], rng)`). Body template `content/daily-template.md` (best-effort `try/catch`; falls back to minimal stub on template-missing — but the template exists at that path so it renders, leaving Templater `<% %>` literals intact since `substituteLenient` only touches `{{var}}` shape). Determinism smoke: two fresh tmp vaults seeded at same anchor produce byte-identical content (sha256 `c4b3f69ddf6014c7da826240f9439b6c05a68c2eb33ccf34781c22c6934e0461`).
- `platform/blueprints/meetings/seed/seed.json` α declarative — 4 meetings: 2 with `[[Person]]` wikilinks (dangling-link warnings accepted per design — people blueprint not seeded this cycle), 1 standalone, 1 recurring. Renders `templates/Meeting.md` (heavy Templater `<% %>` syntax; literals survive substitution — acceptable).

**Integration smoke harness (S5, S8.5):**
- `platform/test/run-integration-smoke.js` — bar-ii harness, 15 sub-asserts. Bootstraps a fresh `mkdtemp` vault with `--non-interactive --no-register --mechanisms=all`, seeds across all subscribed seedable blueprints with `--anchor-date 2026-05-12`, audits clean (0 errors AND 0 warnings), then post-conditions: per-blueprint file counts (≥3 / ≥30 / ≥4), `--no-register` registry-untouched invariant, installed.json mechanism-version coherence with workshop `platform/manifest.json`. `KEEP_SMOKE_VAULT=1` env-var preserves the tmp vault for inspection. Wired into `release:preflight` chain LAST (after `run-seed.js`). End-to-end runtime ~5-10s. The `--mechanisms=all` flag was an inline correction to S5.1 — the plan implied no extra bootstrap flags but the `installed.json mechanism-version coherence` assert required the subscription to actually contain mechanisms.

**CI (S9, S10):**
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` converted single-job → `[macos-latest, ubuntu-latest]` `fail-fast: false` matrix; both runners run full `npm run release:preflight`; brew-install-smoke step gated `if: matrix.os == 'macos-latest'`.
- `scripts/check-version-sync.js` — fails if `package.json version !== platform/manifest.json workshop_version`. Prepended as first step of `release:preflight`.
- `.github/workflows/release.yml` gains a `preflight` job (macos-latest, full preflight chain); existing `bump-tap` job becomes `needs: preflight`. Tag-pushed-against-red preflight no longer ships a formula bump.

**Docs (S11):**
- `Docs/use.md` gains "Releasing a version" gate-sequence (lockstep version bump + preflight + push + tag + release.yml + merge tap PR + verify) + "Recommended GitHub branch protection (one-time UI setup)" sections.

**Workshop dogfood (S12):**
- `sauce reinstall --vault $PWD` (workshop self-install) clean. Test-vault dogfood (`/Users/willfellhoelter/notes/sauce/test-vault`) deferred — that vault is on pre-v0.36.0 layout with stale `workshop_relative_path: "../beacon"` stub; migration is out-of-scope this cycle.
- `workshop_version` 0.37.0 → 0.38.0; `package.json version` 0.36.1 → 0.38.0 reconciled (closes the intentional preflight-red window between S10.2 and S12.3). Manifest `date: "2026-05-13"`.

**Stage commits on origin/main (chronological):** S1.x (predecessor session: `517dda1` seeder skeleton + decl loader, `6e00df8` code-review fixes, `626b605`/`82c6182`/`fac4ef9` harness +16 sub-asserts, `d7b7e80` wire run-seed into preflight); S2.1 `09b6629` cmd-seed.js dispatcher; S2.2 `077918a` verb-table wiring + help; S2.3 `1ddea03` +3 S-cases to run-cli; S3.1 `09d98f1` programmatic.js; S3.2 `0405d8c` wire into seedVault; S3.3 `1a98f83` +10 PR/RNG/DT cases; S4.1 `d51e4ce` --reset path; S4.2 `3339bc3` +5 R-cases; S5.1 `237cf46` smoke harness skeleton; S6.2 `8f058e9` project α-seed; S7.2 `ef99eb8` daily β-seed; S8.2 `4c2d869` meetings α-seed; S8.5 `503d37f` wire smoke into preflight + `--mechanisms=all` fix; S9.1 `743ba60` CI matrix; S10.1 `3a43654` check-version-sync.js gate; S10.2 `5a700a5` preflight gates on version-sync first; S10.3 `1d1e381` release.yml preflight job; S11.1 `4cef914` use.md release-process; S12.3 `9e1e6ad` workshop_version + package.json reconciled.

**Whole-suite preflight delta:** run-cli 97 → 100 (+3 from S2.3); NEW run-seed 31 sub-asserts; NEW run-integration-smoke 15 sub-asserts. Whole-suite total 1170 → 1216 (+46). 12 → 14 harnesses. Renderer cases unchanged at 36. Mechanisms catalogue UNCHANGED (10). Blueprints catalogue UNCHANGED (11).

**Pacing rhythm:** Subagent-driven-development for S2-S4 (full two-reviewer protocol for implementation tasks: implementer → spec-compliance → code-quality; combined-review shortcut for test-addition tasks per the autonomous-handoff cadence guidance). Controller-direct edits for S5-S12 (deterministic transcription + smoke verification — subagent overhead unjustified for boilerplate file creation). Single session closed the cycle (no recursive handoffs needed). Cross-cutting macOS APFS gotchas: (a) `.gitignore` `/Scripts/` rule matched the lowercase `scripts/check-version-sync.js` due to case-insensitive FS; required `git add -f` to track — Linux CI sees the lowercase path normally; (b) `github-personal` SSH host alias from `CLAUDE.md` isn't configured on this machine — workflow-touching commits pushed via `git push git@github.com:willfell/sauce.git main` (HTTPS origin's OAuth token lacks `workflow` scope; SSH succeeds).

**NEW FIX-LATER-NOTEs:** (a) test-vault at `/Users/willfellhoelter/notes/sauce/test-vault` is on pre-v0.36.0 layout — needs `sauce migrate-layout` pass to become the proper dogfood target; one-off follow-up cycle; (b) `.gitignore` `/Scripts/` rule should be replaced with explicit named files inside `Scripts/` so the lowercase `scripts/` directory no longer collides on APFS; (c) `cmd-seed.js`'s `--reset` path partially-deletes on mid-loop `readBlueprintModuleDir` throw — acceptable for test-vault destructive op but a transactional pre-check pass (validate all manifests resolvable before any rm) would be more robust; (d) smoke's `--mechanisms=all` flag was an inline correction to S5.1 — the plan implied no extra bootstrap flags but the installed.json mechanism-version coherence assert requires the subscription to actually contain mechanisms; future smoke plans should explicit this; (e) the daily β-seed renders `content/daily-template.md` but the template uses Templater `<% %>` syntax which substituteLenient leaves intact — seeded daily notes contain Templater literals. Acceptable for audit-bar-ii but visually confusing if the user opens the seeded vault. A future cycle could either ship a separate `seed-template.md` per blueprint or extend the seeder ctx with a Templater-stub renderer.

**NEW lessons (v0.38.0):**
- (a) **destructive-op gates need test-vault marker fields, not registry membership.** v0.36.0's `~/.sauce/vaults.json` registry was tempting as the gating mechanism but it's per-machine and the user can register anything. A vault-local field (`vault_kind:"test"` in `ranch/platform-config.json`) is the right opt-in: explicit, repo-relative, survives a re-clone, can be reviewed by a human inspecting the vault before deletion happens.
- (b) **deterministic seeds need a controlled RNG seed, not real time.** `fixedRng(blueprint, anchorDate)` produces byte-identical output across runs because the seed is derived from a SHA256 of (blueprint, anchor) — no `Math.random()` calls, no `Date.now()`. The smoke runs against `--anchor-date 2026-05-12` so it's stable across days.
- (c) **smoke harness bootstrap flags must explicitly enable mechanism subscription** if the harness will inspect `installed.json` versions. `--non-interactive` by default produces an empty subscription; `--mechanisms=all` is the bootstrap-of-everything flag the harness needs.
- (d) **intentional preflight-red windows are OK to schedule but must be tight.** Between S10.2 (preflight gates on version-sync first) and S12.3 (reconcile), local `npm run release:preflight` fails immediately on the first line. The plan called this out explicitly; if a tag had been pushed in that window, `release.yml`'s `preflight` job would have failed before `bump-tap` ran, and no formula would have shipped — exactly the gate behavior we wanted.
- (e) **OAuth-token push restrictions for `.github/workflows/` files.** HTTPS origin's GitHub OAuth token without the `workflow` scope can't push commits that modify `.github/workflows/*.yml`. SSH-based push (or a Personal Access Token with `workflow` scope) is required. Document this in cycle-close protocol for any cycle that touches CI.

Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-v0.38.0-deploy-confidence-substrate-plan.md`. Spec: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-deploy-confidence-substrate-design.md`. Result: `Docs/plans/2026-05-13-v0.38.0-result.md`. Handoff: `Docs/prompts/2026-05-13-v0.38.0-execution-handoff.md` (autonomous-execution variant).

## v0.38.1 people α-seed CLOSED 2026-05-13

First application of the v0.38.0 deploy-confidence substrate to a non-pilot blueprint. Three declarative entries — Alice / Bob / Carol — land in `platform/blueprints/people/seed/seed.json`, materializing as `spice/people/<Name>.md` with `tags: [person]`, `company`, and `title` frontmatter. The names match the `[[Person]]` wikilinks already shipped in `platform/blueprints/meetings/seed/seed.json`, retiring v0.38.0's known-limitation dangling-link warnings on the meetings α-seed.

`platform/test/run-integration-smoke.js` grows `15 → 17 pass · 0 fail` via `+2` expectations (`smoke-bp-people-exists` + `smoke-bp-people-count>=3`); the harness's seed total line moves `37 → 40 notes across 4 blueprints` and the smoke-4 regex `total: \d+ notes` still matches. Workshop self-install via `sauce reinstall --vault $PWD` clean post-bump.

**Workshop dogfood (T3):** `sauce reinstall --vault $PWD` clean. Workshop materialization touched `ranch/bootstrap-last-install.log` + `ranch/claude-surface-registry.json` + `ranch/platform-installed.json` — bundled into T4 cycle-close commit per handoff guidance.

**Versions (T3 lockstep):** `workshop_version 0.38.0 → 0.38.1`, `package.json version 0.38.0 → 0.38.1`, `platform/blueprints/people/manifest.json version 0.1.1 → 0.1.2`. Manifest `date: "2026-05-13"`. Tag `v0.38.1` annotated, pushed; `.github/workflows/release.yml` preflight → bump-tap chain triggered to auto-bump `Formula/sauce.rb` in `willfell/homebrew-sauce`.

**Stage commits on origin/main:** T1 `721dfb1` people seed.json; T2 `6e7e314` smoke +2 expectations; T3 `7912ff9` version bump lockstep. T4 close bundle commit follows this archive write.

**Whole-suite preflight delta:** `run-integration-smoke.js 15 → 17 pass`. All other harnesses UNCHANGED. Whole-suite total `1216 → 1218 sub-asserts + 36 renderer cases` across 14 harnesses. Mechanisms catalogue UNCHANGED (10). Blueprints catalogue UNCHANGED at 11 entries; `people` is the only PATCH-bumped blueprint.

**Pacing rhythm:** Controller-direct execution (no subagents, no reviewers). Test-pass output is the spec gate for T1 (`3 notes created, 0 skipped` + tmp-vault `ls` shows Alice.md / Bob.md / Carol.md) and T2 (smoke 15 → 17). Per the handoff's cadence guidance — for a tightly-specified substrate-application cycle of this size, controller-direct is the fastest path. Subagent dispatch would have added overhead without quality gain.

**Lessons (v0.38.1):**
- (a) **The substrate scales as designed.** Adding a new blueprint seed is now ≈3 file edits (seed.json + smoke expectations + version triple) plus a tmp-vault smoke validation. No design judgment, no architectural decisions, no plan beyond the handoff doc.
- (b) **Templater-literal frontmatter is harmless and accepted.** Seeded `Alice.md` shows the seed-managed frontmatter block (`company: Acme`, `tags: [person]`, `title: Engineering Manager`) followed by the template's own Templater-literal block (`<% tp.file.title %>` etc.). substituteLenient only replaces `{{var}}` shape, not Templater syntax. v0.38.0 daily β-seed accepted this posture; v0.38.1 carries it forward without fighting it.
- (c) **Wikilink resolution is basename-keyed.** Obsidian's link resolver matches `[[Alice]]` against any vault file with basename `Alice.md` — no path-prefix matching needed. The meetings seed's dangling-wikilink warnings retire automatically the moment people files exist at any vault location; ours land at `spice/people/Alice.md` per the module-directory invariant.
- (d) **No-handoff-needed cycles still benefit from a written handoff.** This cycle's `Docs/prompts/2026-05-13-v0.38.1-people-seed-handoff.md` was the operational spec — file paths quoted, JSON content embedded, expected smoke counts predicted. A single session closed the cycle without clarification questions or re-planning. The handoff is the proof that v0.38.0 left a substrate that an agent can apply mechanically.

Plan: (none — handoff doc IS the plan). Handoff: `Docs/prompts/2026-05-13-v0.38.1-people-seed-handoff.md`. Result: `Docs/plans/2026-05-13-v0.38.1-result.md`.

## v0.40.0 scratch day-hub redesign CLOSED 2026-05-13

`scratch@0.1.0 → 0.2.0` MINOR. Nav-button now opens-or-creates today's day-hub `Scratch-Day-YYYY-MM-DD.md` instead of always creating a timestamped scratch. The day-hub is the per-day surface — embeds `ScratchNewButton` (NEW helper using `customJS.AccentButton`) for creating `Scratch-YYYY-MM-DD-HH-mm.md` siblings + `ScratchDayList` for browsing existing ones. Resolves three stacked bugs in v0.37.0's shipping shape: (a) wikilink collision between scratch day-index filename `<dddd>-YYYY-MM-DD.md` and daily-blueprint filename `dddd-YYYY-MM-DD.md` — scratch back-link `[[Tuesday-2026-05-12|Back to day]]` was resolving to the daily note; new filename `Scratch-Day-YYYY-MM-DD.md` is collision-free, (b) `ScratchHubCards` target path pointed at the colliding day-index and dead-ended; rewritten to target `Scratch-Day-*` path, (c) no "today's scratches" landing surface — day-hub now is that surface. **No nav-buttons mechanism delta** — initial design proposed `nav-buttons@2.6.0 → 2.7.0` MINOR with new `if_exists` field, but `space-nav-buttons.js:348-352` already does open-if-exists for `runTemplaterTemplate`; the behavior was dormant for scratch because the v0.1.0 nav-button's filename had an `HH-mm` suffix making each click unique. Dropping `HH-mm` from the nav-button's `filename_date_pattern` activates the dormant branch for free. Templates: `Scratch Day Hub.md` (NEW), `Scratch.md` (lazy-create `<%* %>` block retired, back-link wikilink rewritten), `Scratch Day.md` (RETIRED). Helpers: `ScratchNewButton` (NEW — uses `customJS.AccentButton.render` after S2 code review caught that the originally-coded `beacon-accent-button` CSS class didn't exist), `ScratchHubCards` (path rewrite). Manifest: customjs_classes +1, files +1 new −1 retired, depends_on +1 (`accent-button >=0.1.0`), nav_buttons[0] template_source + filename composition + id rename `scratch-new → scratch-day-hub`, rule_fragments +1 (`scratch-day-hub` validator). Leaf fragment's path_glob narrowed `Scratch-*.md → Scratch-2*.md` after S3 review caught `_compileGlob` doesn't exclude `-` in `*` — broader glob would fire false-positive violations on every day-hub note (works until year 3000). Harness deltas: helper-cases 590 → 593 (+SHC-S7 + retired-lazy + Scratch-Day back-link asserts in SHC-S2), renderer 36 cases UNCHANGED (R-SCRATCH-NEW renamed to R-SCRATCH-DAYHUB), audit 74 → 77 (+SA-S7 day-hub valid +SA-S8 wrong-type violation; SCRATCH_FRAGMENT path_glob narrowed in parallel with manifest). Whole-suite preflight green across 12 harnesses at S5 close. Cycle scoped scratch-only — initial S6 plan proposed absorbing in-flight v0.39.0 project@1.5.0 → 1.6.0 work, but project@1.6.0 manifest references a never-created `helpers/project-action-buttons.js` (v0.39.0 stopped at S6.7; planned S7 file-creation never executed). v0.39.0 closure deferred to a separate future cycle. Migration deferred — orphan `<dddd>-YYYY-MM-DD.md` files in existing vaults left for hand-cleanup; surface area per vault is tiny. Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-13-v0.40.0-scratch-day-hub-redesign-plan.md`. Design: `Docs/plans/2026-05-13-scratch-day-hub-redesign-design.md`.

## v0.41.0 convenience-cohesion CLOSED 2026-05-13

`convenience@0.1.0 → 0.2.0` MINOR absorbs three consumer-default behaviors. (a) `platform/manifest.json` `app_settings` += `defaultViewMode: "preview"` + `livePreview: false` — every newly opened note starts in reading view; `Cmd+E` flips per-note. Inherits `applyAppSettings` platform-as-overrider semantics. (b) `convenience` `hotkeys[]` +1 entry `Cmd+T → daily-notes:goto-today` — inherits new-tab semantics from already-shipped `alwaysOpenInNewTab: true`. After brew upgrade + headspace reinstall: all 7 entries materialize cleanly (verified via `sauce audit --entity-create` zero findings + `cat ranch/entity-create-registry.json` shows 7 entries: meeting/person/project/budget/paycheck/invoice/scratch). Commit: `700958f`. Test gap surfaced for future cycle: workshop dogfood is not a faithful proxy for consumer install — file-source-missing errors degrade silently at workshop but block install completion at fresh consumer vaults. Brainstorm input for v0.46.3+.

- **v0.63.3 summary (to-do follow-up PATCH — minimal-template redesign, CLOSED 2026-05-19):** PATCH redesigning the daily-template body to a minimal form per user feedback after v0.63.2 smoke. `Today To-Do.md` and `_renderTomorrowTemplate` both strip the pre-existing `## Tasks` heading, `- [ ]` placeholder, and `## Notes` heading — leaving just frontmatter + the two dataviewjs blocks (SpaceNavButtons + ToDoLeafActions). User reported the pre-existing `- [ ]` placeholder visually obscured the migrated task right below it in Obsidian's rendering. Two logic changes to keep migration working with the new minimal body: (a) `parseTasks` gains a frontmatter-aware fallback — if no `## Tasks` heading exists, it scans the whole body after the closing `---` of frontmatter, so free-form unchecked tasks added directly under the dataviewjs blocks are findable. (b) `applyMigration` gains an end-of-file append path — when tomorrow has no `## Tasks` heading (the new minimal default), migrated content is appended at the end with a blank-line separator. The prior "return tomorrow unchanged" silent-fail behavior is gone. Also corrects v0.63.2's post_install Notice text drift (FLN-todo-17 closed). to-do 0.3.2 → 0.3.3 PATCH; workshop 0.63.2 → 0.63.3; package.json + subscription lockstep; FA6 + TD-HC-1 pins bumped 0.3.2 → 0.3.3. Whole-suite preflight green (22 harnesses; run-todo-modal now has 22 sub-asserts — added TM-6 for no-`## Tasks` tomorrow append + TM-7 for minimal-template parseTasks with frontmatter-skip).
- **v0.63.2 summary (to-do follow-up PATCH — three UX/recovery fixes from headspace smoke, CLOSED 2026-05-19):** PATCH bundling three issues found during the v0.63.1 smoke. (a) `ToDoMigrateModal._renderTomorrowTemplate` was missing the `ToDoLeafActions` dataviewjs block, so auto-created tomorrow notes (when user clicks Migrate before tomorrow exists) didn't have the inline All-To-Dos / Migrate buttons that Templater-created notes have. Fix: add the missing block to the inline template. (b) Modal silently created tomorrow's note + showed only a Notice toast; user had no immediate signal of WHERE the migrated tasks went and couldn't easily navigate to verify. Fix: `_submit` now calls `app.workspace.openLinkText(tomorrowFile.path, '')` after successful writes, so tomorrow opens in the active pane immediately. (c) `ToDoLeafActions.openAllToDos` previously just called `openLinkText` — but Obsidian creates an empty placeholder if the target file is missing, AND the installer treats `{{module_directory}}`-scoped destinations as idempotent (skip-if-exists), so a user-deleted-then-recreated-via-button All-ToDos.md stayed empty forever. Fix: `openAllToDos` now detects missing OR empty OR aggregator-block-missing files and writes the canonical hub body inline before opening; emits a Notice when restoration happens. to-do 0.3.1 → 0.3.2 PATCH; workshop 0.63.1 → 0.63.2; package.json + subscription lockstep; FA6 + TD-HC-1 version pins bumped 0.3.1 → 0.3.2. Whole-suite preflight green (22 harnesses, TM-1..5 still pass — the _renderTomorrowTemplate change adds a block but doesn't affect parser/applier static helpers). FLN-todo-14 (deleted hub recovery) closed inline; FLN-todo-15 (tomorrow-template missing leaf block) closed inline; new FLN-todo-16: `_renderTomorrowTemplate` still duplicates the daily-template body — drift between this and `Today To-Do.md` will recur each time the daily template changes (e.g., if a `ToDoSomethingElseActions` is added in a future cycle). Long-term refactor: share a single body partial via a new platform primitive (continuation of FLN-todo-1).
- **v0.63.1 summary (to-do follow-up PATCH — customJS instance fix + nav-button → AccentButton scope rebalance, CLOSED 2026-05-19):** PATCH addressing two issues found during v0.63.0 headspace smoke. (1) `todo-migrate-init.js` used `new customJS.ToDoMigrateModal().open()` but the customjs plugin auto-instantiates each registered class — so `customJS.ToDoMigrateModal` is already a singleton instance, NOT the class constructor. The `new` call fails with `customJS.ToDoMigrateModal is not a constructor`. Fix: drop `new`; call `customJS.ToDoMigrateModal.open()` directly. (2) v0.63.0 declared All-To-Dos + Migrate as global `nav_buttons[]` per the brainstorm decision, which surfaces them on EVERY note via `SpaceNavButtons` — the design's expected consequence but the user found it undesirable for these two specifically (All-To-Dos is contextual-ish; Migrate is strictly contextual). Fix: remove `todo-all` + `todo-migrate` from `nav_buttons[]`, add NEW `ToDoLeafActions` CustomJS class that renders both as AccentButtons in a centered row, embed via a new dataviewjs block in `Today To-Do.md`. Mirrors `ScratchLeafActions` pattern. Buttons now visible ONLY on `ToDo-YYYY-MM-DD.md` daily notes. The `todo-today` "create today" nav-button stays global (correct quick-create semantics). to-do blueprint 0.3.0 → 0.3.1 PATCH; workshop_version 0.63.0 → 0.63.1; package.json + subscription lockstep. - **v0.49.2 summary (Kanban Card vault-scan source-board fix — REAL fix, CLOSED 2026-05-16):** PATCH validated at headspace via user's hands-on smoke. v0.49.1's earlier attempt was based on a wrong reading of the obsidian-kanban plugin's flow (v0.49.1 assumed `tp.config.active_file` would be the kanban board, but reading the kanban plugin's main.js v2.0.51 source revealed it calls `templaterPlugin.append_template_to_active_file(template)` AFTER opening the new file as the active leaf — so `active_file` IS the new file in this flow). v0.49.2's real fix: `Template, Kanban Card.md` detects source board by SCANNING THE VAULT for kanban-plugin board files containing `[[<new-card-title>]]` (kanban appends this link to the board's body when adding a card), picking the board with the most recent mtime. Both `<%* %>` blocks (workstream picker + auto-promote `tp.file.move`) use this `_findSourceKanbanBoard(title)` async helper. Robust to: vault-root file placement (kanban-plugin's per-board `new-note-folder` is IGNORED when `new-note-template` is set), kanban's active-leaf switching to the new file, multiple open kanban boards. **User-validated 2026-05-16:** "everything is now functioning as it should" — confirmed via hands-on creation of project `testit` + nested task `more-test/tasks/ya/ya` + sub-task-board `more-test/tasks/testing it/board/sub/sub`. project blueprint 1.9.1 → 1.9.2 PATCH; workshop_version 0.49.1 → 0.49.2. Brew formula auto-bumped via release.yml; tap PR #29 merged 2026-05-16T07:06:19Z (then v0.49.2 → tap PR #30 merged same day); brew upgrade verified. Workshop dogfood reinstall clean (28 history entries on v0.49.2 alone). NEW FLN-v49.2-1 (Mobile): user reports "startup template is failing but can't see logs on phone" — suggests `ProjectTaskCreateListenerInit` may not fire on Obsidian Mobile (customjs startupScriptNames semantics may differ desktop vs mobile, OR a downstream call throws silently); desktop verified working, so this is a mobile-platform follow-up triage candidate; not blocking. Result: `Docs/plans/2026-05-16-v0.49.2-result.md`.
- **v0.49.1 summary (Kanban Card source-board detection fix, SUPERSEDED by v0.49.2 same-day):** PATCH attempted to fix the v0.48.0/v0.49.0 follow-on symptom where new kanban card files landed at vault root with no workstream picker. The fix used `tp.config.active_file` as primary source-board detection, based on the (wrong) assumption that Templater records the user's active file at command invocation. Reading the obsidian-kanban plugin's actual source revealed the flow: kanban creates the empty new file, OPENS it as the active leaf, then calls `templaterPlugin.append_template_to_active_file(template)`. By template-run time, `tp.config.active_file` IS the new file. v0.49.1's "fix" didn't help; user reported the symptom persisted ("another no.md" still at vault root). Superseded by v0.49.2's vault-scan approach.
- **v0.49.1 summary (original — pre-supersede):** PATCH fixing the user-reported symptom where new task cards landed at vault root (`nono.md`) with no workstream picker after clicking "+ Add a card" on a project's kanban board. **Root cause:** the obsidian-kanban plugin's per-board `new-note-folder` setting is IGNORED when `new-note-template` is set — kanban delegates folder placement to Templater's `create-new-note-from-template` command, which uses Templater's default folder (vault root) rather than kanban's `new-note-folder`. The v0.48.0/v0.49.0 listener was never reached because kanban-plugin invokes Templater directly via its internal integration (the listener watches for vault.create on `^spice/projects/<slug>/tasks/<file>.md$`, which doesn't match a vault-root-created file). **Fix:** Template, Kanban Card.md's source-board detection now prefers `tp.config.active_file?.path` (Templater-recorded active file at invocation time = the kanban board the user clicked) over `tp.config.target_file?.path` (the new file at its initial location). Both the workstream picker (top `<%* %>` block) and the auto-promote `tp.file.move` (bottom `<%* %>` block) use this fixed detection. Auto-promote logic generalizes: derives `projectDir` from `tp.config.active_file?.path` rather than gating on `/tasks/` in the new file's path; moves the new file to `<projectDir>/tasks/<title>/<title>.md` regardless of where Templater placed it (vault root, project root, anywhere). project blueprint 1.9.0 → 1.9.1 PATCH; workshop_version 0.49.0 → 0.49.1; package.json + subscription lockstep. Workshop dogfood reinstall clean (1 content overwrite: Template, Kanban Card.md). Whole-suite preflight green; existing KC-1..3 source-string sub-asserts continue to pass (they check sentinel + create-new + empty-toast strings which all remain). Brew tag chain auto-bumped tap formula on push.

Three independent fixes designed as one cohesive cycle. **(1) L2 listener registration:** NEW `ProjectTaskCreateListenerInit` customjs class (5-LOC bootstrap that delegates to v0.48.0's `ProjectTaskCreateListener.init()`) registered via customjs plugin's `startupScriptNames[]` field. Wired into installItem at install.js:993 immediately after `applyTemplaterStartupTemplates`. v0.48.0's Templater wiring + Template, Project Task Create Listener.md retained as belt-and-suspenders backstop (idempotent init via `_initialized` flag in the unchanged v0.48.0 listener class). **(2) BUG-7 Create Board gate widening:** `project-nav-buttons.js:597` 1-line edit — `if (ctx.context === "task-hub")` → `if (ctx.context === "task-hub" || ctx.context === "task-note")`. Open Board / Create Board AccentButton now renders inside task notes too. Preserves v0.46.0's design (the `_createTaskBoard` helper remained intact); the gate widening just exposes existing functionality on more contexts. **(3) BUG-8 sentinel relocation:** entity-create's load-bearing sentinel relocated from outside-block HTML comment `<!-- entity-create:<id> -->` (visible in source/edit mode of Obsidian) to inside-block JS comment `// entity-create:<id> — installer-managed; do not delete this comment` (invisible in BOTH source AND reading modes). Touches all 7 entity-create-using hub source files (Projects.md / Meeting Hub.md / People.md / Scratch Day Hub.md / Budgets.md / Paychecks.md / Invoices.md) — all turned out to be Flavor B (standalone outside-block marker; v0.46.0 installer was supposed to inject the dataviewjs block at install time). install.js `injectAccentButtonBlock` becomes verify-only per Choice A (hub source files own the AccentButton dataviewjs block; installer ZERO-MUTATION just confirms presence via inside-block sentinel regex; emits warning if missing). S4b mid-stage code-review fix: pushErr step disambiguated to `entity_create_block_error` (vs pushWarn's `entity_create_block_missing`). Workshop_version 0.48.0 → 0.49.0. - **v0.48.0 summary (project kanban "+ Add a card" workstream fix + icons polish, CLOSED-WITH-KNOWN-ISSUE 2026-05-15):** MINOR cycle shipping the `ProjectTaskCreateListener` CustomJS class (~65 LOC) + Templater startup_templates wiring + NEW `applyTemplaterStartupTemplates` installer helper (~190 LOC mirroring `applyTemplaterFolderTemplates` at install.js:3727) intended to fix BUG-5 (kanban "+ Add a card" doesn't trigger Templater post-paste at projects). Bundled FLN-a/b/c icons polish: `icons@0.1.0 → 0.1.1` PATCH (resolve() captures `this._tier1` to local once at top — was triple-call via getter; `__cache → _cache` rename for sauce single-underscore convention; `this._tier1` refs in resolve() body 3 → 1) + `entity-create@0.2.0 → 0.2.1` PATCH (plusIcon SVG 16x16 → 15x15 for size consistency with Tier 1 vendored icons). Workshop preflight green across 15 harnesses; +10 sub-asserts (PTCL-1..5 listener path-regex + idempotency + command-id literal; KC-1..3 Kanban Card source-string asserts; ICN-1 icons regression guard threshold ≤3; smoke-prj-customjs-startup post-bootstrap data.json startup_templates[] verification). **Known issue (S10 escalation gate fired):** at headspace re-validation after brew upgrade + reinstall + Cmd-R reload, the listener never subscribed at boot. No `[ProjectTaskCreateListener] subscribed to vault.create` console log. Templater's `startup_templates[]` mechanism did not activate the listener at the consumer install. Net effect: BUG-5 fix is dormant at headspace; kanban "+ Add a card" still doesn't trigger workstream picker. The listener class + data.json wiring + test harness coverage are correct and reusable; only the registration vector failed. **Disposition:** close v0.48.0 as-is; v0.49.0 follow-up swaps registration to L2 customjs `startupScriptNames[]` (design committed `9095f33` immediately after v0.48.0 close); the listener class is unchanged in v0.49.0; v0.48.0's wiring stays as belt-and-suspenders backstop (idempotent init via `_initialized` flag). v0.48.0 also surfaced two pre-existing bugs unrelated to v0.48.0: BUG-7 (HIGH; `project-nav-buttons.js:597` gates Create Board AccentButton on task-hub context only — pre-existing since v0.39.0+) + BUG-8 (cosmetic; `<!-- entity-create:<id> -->` HTML markers visible in source/edit mode of all 7 entity-create-using hub files — added in v0.46.0). Both bundled into v0.49.0 scope. **S0 verification gate retrospect:** S0 had 4 sub-steps; Steps 1.1+1.2 retired headlessly; Steps 1.3+1.4 deferred per user defensive-hedge (option C: bump _postCreateDelayMs 50→200ms + skip live smoke). The 200ms hedge turned out to be irrelevant — the actual failure was earlier in the chain (Templater startup_templates didn't fire AT ALL). Lesson: a defensive-hedge on Mechanism B doesn't help if Mechanism A (registration itself) fails. v0.49.0 elevates S0 to a HARD-FAIL gate before any code lands. ## v0.71.1 customjs-class-file-contract CLOSED 2026-05-21

**Workshop:** 0.71.0 → 0.71.1
**Mechanism:** activity-feed 0.5.0 → 0.5.1

**Headline:** v0.71.0 S2 introduced a file-scope `_humanCase` helper above the `ActivityFeed` class declaration. customJS loads each .js file under `eval(`(${file})`)` — a file-scope helper preceding the class causes `SyntaxError: Unexpected token 'class'`, customJS silently fails to register the class, and downstream consumers (SpaceDailyDashboard) emit "ActivityFeed mechanism unavailable" on the daily note. Bug shipped to all vaults at v0.71.0 but was masked where customJS still held the v0.4.1 instance in memory.

**Fix:** `_humanCase` moved inside `ActivityFeed` as an instance method; call site uses `this._humanCase(key)`. NEW regression test `AF-V0710-CUSTOMJS-1` (4 sub-asserts) loads the file under customJS's `(${file})` contract.

**Lessons:**
1. customJS class-file contract: each .js file under `jsFolder` must be a single class expression. NO file-scope helpers; every helper lives inside the class. Code-quality review for v0.5.0 flagged file-scope `_humanCase` as a "new precedent" minor issue but missed the contract violation.
2. Consumer subscription bump still manual (FLN-v67-7 unresolved). Each vault's `ranch/platform-subscription.json` activity-feed pin needs bumping 0.5.0 → 0.5.1 before `sauce update`.
3. FLN-v68-3 Edit-tool revert pattern recurred multiple times during this patch. Mitigation: grep-verify immediately after every Edit AND before staging; commit ASAP in a single batch.

See `Docs/plans/2026-05-21-v0.71.1-customjs-class-file-contract-result.md` for the full record.

**Headline:** Surfaced kanban-board column-moves in the daily-dashboard Activity panel. Each card carries `status:` + `status_prev:` + `status_changed_at:` written by `KanbanStatusSync.syncAllBoards(dv, today)` (parses each `kanban-plugin: board` page, slugifies the column header, writes only-on-change via `app.fileManager.processFrontMatter`). The dashboard called `syncAllBoards` BEFORE `ActivityFeed.render`, then passed `tsKeys: ["created_at", "status_changed_at"]` to surface today's moves alongside today's creates. NEW `_renderKanbanDrillInList` emitted per-card transition lines (move/create/archive precedence: archive > move > create).

**v0.72.1 follow-up (same day):** added a once-per-day in-singleton cache to `syncAllBoards` after the user reported a 283-card write storm on accuris' first run of the day. Subsequent re-renders within the same customjs-singleton lifetime returned the cached `{synced, archived, boards}` result instantly. Did NOT fix the underlying re-render cause (smart-connections embedding queue suspected).

See `Docs/plans/2026-05-21-activity-feed-kanban-progress-design.md` + `-plan.md`.

## v0.73.0 kanban-progress-architectural-cleanup MINOR CLOSED 2026-05-22

**Workshop:** 0.72.1 → 0.73.0
**Mechanism:** `kanban-status-sync` 0.1.1 → 0.2.0 (NEW `KanbanStatusSyncInit` customjs startup-script class); `activity-feed` 0.6.0 → 0.7.0 (persisted `<details>` state + audit pass).
**Blueprint:** `daily` 0.12.0 → 0.13.0 (removed inline syncAllBoards call; `_renderSection` honors persisted state).

**Headline:** Four-part architectural cleanup of the kanban-progress surface plus an efficiency audit pass on activity-feed.

- **Part A (kanban-status-sync@0.2.0):** moved the `syncAllBoards` trigger out of `SpaceDailyDashboard.render` and into a NEW customjs startup-script class `KanbanStatusSyncInit`. The init class is registered in `customjs.startupScriptNames[]` via the manifest's `customjs_startup_scripts[]` field, runs once at vault boot (Dataview-ready retry-with-backoff, max 30s), and is a cache-hit on subsequent invocations (per the v0.72.1 once-per-day cache). Removes our code from the hot path of every Dataview re-execution — closes the "scroll resets to top of daily" UX regression triggered by external plugins (e.g., smart-connections embedding queue) invalidating the dataviewjs subscription.
- **Part B (activity-feed@0.7.0 + daily@0.13.0):** persisted `<details>` open/closed state across re-renders via `ranch/cache/dashboard-section-state.json`. Namespaced keys: `sauce-daily-dashboard:<section>` for top-level Tasks/Meetings/Activity panels; `sauce-activity-feed:<bucketKey>` for inner activity-feed groups. Best-effort read/write; missing file or malformed JSON falls back to default-open semantics. Last-write-wins on toggle. New helpers `_readSectionState` + `_writeSectionStateKey` (dashboard), `_readGroupState` + `_writeGroupStateKey` (activity-feed). `render()` becomes `async`; the await is guarded behind a real `app.vault.adapter` check so the Node harness path stays synchronous.
- **Part C (Obsidian command):** Cmd+P → "Sauce: Re-sync kanban boards" (id `kanban-status-sync:resync-now`) registered inside `KanbanStatusSyncInit._registerResyncCommand`. Bypasses the once-per-day cache (nulls `_lastSyncDay`/`_lastSyncResult` on the singleton) and forces a fresh sweep. Surfaces the result via Notice.
- **Part D (activity-feed audit):** precedence comments (metaBuilder vs getSubtitle), short-circuit annotation in `_query.inWindow`, manifest description trim. THREE new runtime asserts in `run-activity-feed.js`: AF-V073-1 (tsKeys with 3 distinct keys, any-in-window semantics), AF-V073-2 (groupPreviewBuilder fires for `defaultClosed[]` groups + post-bucket key matches), AF-V073-3 (native-Date `_resolveTimeWindow` fallback reachable from Node harness). `kanban-status-sync` harness gains `Pass 1b` (`KSS-INIT-1..9`) covering manifest + init-class source lint.

**Deploy:** workshop tag v0.73.0 pushed; homebrew-sauce tap PR #92 merged; `brew upgrade sauce` to 0.73.0; pins bumped + `sauce update --force` against all 4 consumer vaults (barebones / accuris-sauce / ero-sauce / headspace-sauce). Byte-level cmp verified `activity-feed.js`, `space-daily-dashboard.js`, and `kanban-status-sync-init.js` match workshop sources across all 4 consumers. `KanbanStatusSyncInit` present in `startupScriptNames[]` for each.

**Lessons / carry-forwards:**
1. The render-time-sync pattern shipped in v0.72.0 was a structural mistake. Even with a perfect cache, putting work inside the dataviewjs hot path makes it vulnerable to external re-render triggers we can't control. Future: any persistent bookkeeping that touches frontmatter should run at startup, not at render.
2. `app` is shimmed as `{}` in the test harness — type-of guards must check for a real `vault.adapter`, not just `app`, when deciding whether to enter an await path. Otherwise an async function yields on the first await and breaks sync harness assertions.
3. Landmine #16 strikes again — workshop self-install short-circuited until the version bump landed. Plan accommodated this by running self-install AFTER the cycle-close bumps.
4. AF-V065 (description-mentions-"0.2.0") was a stale regression-protection assertion that the trim made false. Replaced with a forward-looking assertion (description mentions current-shipping `v0.7.0`).
5. `groupPreviewBuilder` gating decision (manifest-`defaultClosed[]` only, not state-closed) documented inline in `_renderFramedGroup` AND in this entry — stable rule going forward.

See `Docs/plans/2026-05-22-v0.73.0-kanban-progress-architectural-cleanup-{design,plan,result}.md`.

## v0.74.0 cowork-orchestrator-rigor MINOR CLOSED 2026-05-22

**Workshop:** 0.73.0 → 0.74.0
**Blueprint:** cowork 0.12.0 → 0.13.0
**Engagement-types:** personal / w2-fte / consulting 0.1.0 → 0.2.0 (additive `tripwire_aspects[]`)
**Mechanism bumps:** none
**Harnesses:** +14 HC-V0740-1..14 in `run-helper-cases.js`; `V071-VERSION` renamed `V074-VERSION` in `run-cowork-smoke.js`; stale HC-V0710-3b/3c/4 + V068-ONBOARD-GUARD fixed (follow-up `b69572f`). 22-harness count unchanged.

**Headline:** Three workstreams bundling body-shape enforcement, a tripwire-aspects model for non-finance engagements, and filesystem-first write tooling with a unified onboarding entry-point.

- **Workstream A (body-shape enforcement):** six `write-run-note-*` skills gain three new sections — `## Title composition` (deterministic `{engagement_id} — {cadence} — {date}` formula), `## Adaptive body skeleton` (five structural markers including `summary:` frontmatter + Obsidian admonitions), `## Pre-write self-check` (five-item checklist; on miss returns `failed:contract-violation:<field>` and halts). The cowork manifest grows `title: required` on all 6 atomic-note rule_fragments. Four orchestrators (`morning-briefing`, `eod-review`, `weekly-review`, `monthly-review`) handle `failed:contract-violation:*` and produce skeleton-compliant stub notes on empty-prompt branches.

- **Workstream B (tripwire-aspects model):** three engagement-type JSON files gain `tripwire_aspects[]` arrays: personal=`["cc_drift"]`; w2-fte=`["calendar_drift", "queue_growth"]` (+ midday in `supported_cadences`); consulting=`["cc_drift", "calendar_drift", "queue_growth"]` (+ midday in `supported_cadences`). `cowork:midday-tripwire` orchestrator rewrites its preflight gate from `engagement_type === "personal"` to `tripwire_aspects.length > 0`, enabling it for all three types. Severity vocabulary flattens from `yellow|red` to `warn|alert`. `cowork:gather-calendar` gains a `drift-check` mode; `cowork:gather-projects` gains a `tripwire-delta` mode.

- **Workstream C (filesystem-first + single-command onboarding):** all six `write-run-note-*` skills migrate from `mcp__obsidian__create_note` to `Bash mkdir -p` + `Write` + `wc -c` byte-count assertion. `cowork:check-vault-routing` gains a `{ required: ["filesystem"] }` mode. `cowork:onboard-scheduled-jobs` receives a full rewrite: 5-step conversational flow (Welcome → preflight auto-delegate → bulk-defaults question → per-cadence preambles → filesystem Read+Write copy with byte-count assertion + re-runnable detection → final summary). This is now the single entry-point post-`brew upgrade sauce`.

**Commits (S0–S11):** `ef5995e` (S1), `b4b344e` + `8063e16` + `2b1da92` (S2 + follow-ups), `89c7a17` (S3), `087b78d` (S3b), `7d81225` + `21cd1c9` (S4), `271640f` (S5), `3b96bcc` (S6), `1ce2cd1` (S7), `bc60491` + `b69572f` (S8 + follow-up), `1c64b4b` + `b60bbe7` (S9 + follow-up), `9ff9060` + `e796933` (S10 + follow-up), S11 this commit.

**Smoke:** `sauce update` clean — 70 new history entries; `workshop_version: 0.74.0`; cowork surface markers verified. Preflight GREEN across all 22 harnesses. Visual smoke (Obsidian) pending user-side post-tag: `brew upgrade sauce` → bump cowork pins 0.12.0 → 0.13.0 per vault (manual; FLN-v67-7) → `sauce update` → `cowork:onboard-scheduled-jobs` (new conversational flow) → next scheduled fire passes all 5 self-checks; midday-tripwire fires on w2-fte + consulting engagements.

**Lessons:**
1. Cross-cycle test-assertion staleness: when a contract changes, audit ALL `HC-*` assertions for stale literals BEFORE preflight, not after. (HC-V0710-3b/3c/4 hit this pattern; v0.71.0 also hit with `discriminator_tags` errata-3.)
2. Plan-doc references to deprecated install commands (`./install.sh` vs `sauce update`) cause friction. Plan boilerplate for S10 dogfood should reference the canonical command.
3. S10 dogfood install touches 28+ consumer-mirror files (`.claude/skills/cowork/*`, engagement-templates, rules) beyond the 3 ranch state files the plan named. Future S10 plans should enumerate the full mirror set.
4. `ranch/platform-subscription.json` has a top-level `workshop_version` field independent of the `pinned` map — both must be bumped at S9.
5. Plan-time pin-location assumptions for FA4/FA6 cowork version tests were stale; actual pin lived in FA5 regex. Grep-verify before writing the plan step.
6. Count assertions should use safety margin (`>= N-1`) rather than exact equality to tolerate file-set drift (15 prompt files vs expected 14).

See `Docs/plans/2026-05-22-v0.74.0-cowork-orchestrator-rigor-{design,plan,result}.md`.

## v0.75.0 cowork-skill-routing-hardening MINOR CLOSED 2026-05-22

**Workshop:** 0.74.0 → 0.75.0
**Blueprint:** cowork 0.13.0 → 0.14.0
**Engagement-types:** personal / w2-fte / consulting 0.2.0 → 0.3.0 (additive `render_aspects.semantic_related`)
**Mechanism bumps:** smart-connections-bridge NEW 0.1.0
**Harnesses:** HC-V0750-A1..A5 in `run-cowork-smoke.js`; HC-V0750-B1..B12 in new `run-smart-connections-bridge.js`; HC-V0750-D1..D7 in `run-cli.js`; stale S1 READ-form assertions fixed (follow-up `0e75874`). 22 → 23 harnesses.

**Headline:** Four workstreams bundled. (A) Closes the v0.74.0 multi-context-Claude-Code skill-routing gap: three layers of hardening — READ-form rewrites (~60 refs across 6 orchestrator files), post-write structural verification in 5 orchestrators (delete-on-miss + `failed:contract-violation:<field>`), and enriched scheduled-tasks prompts in `onboard-scheduled-jobs` Step 6. A fail-closed audit harness check (HC-V0750-A1) prevents naked `Use Skill cowork:` regressions in future cycles. (B) New `smart-connections-bridge@0.1.0` mechanism: Node CLI sc-bridge over `.smart-env/multi/*.ajson` with `{{module_directory}}` substitution + `TaylorAI/bge-micro-v2` embeddings; new `gather-semantic-related` sub-skill renders `> [!example]+ 🧩 Related context` callout; integrated in morning-briefing (per-event), eod-review (daily note), weekly-review (aggregated). Engagement-aware via `render_aspects.semantic_related`; graceful degradation when SC index absent. (C) `onboard-scheduled-jobs` Step 2 path-resolution fix — uses `workshop_manifest_path` instead of unreachable in-vault path. (D) FLN-v67-7 closed: `sauce update --bump-pins` flag replaces 4-vault manual pin-bump loop.

- **Workstream A (skill-routing hardening, 3 layers):** Layer 1 rewrites all `Use Skill cowork:<X>` references (both cases) in 6 orchestrator SKILL.md bodies to the explicit `READ ... SKILL.md in full ... then apply ...` form. Layer 2 adds a post-write verification step to each of the 5 atomic-note-writing orchestrators: re-reads the written file, regex-scans structural markers + frontmatter, deletes on miss, returns `failed:contract-violation:<field>`. Layer 3 enriches the scheduled-tasks prompts registered by `onboard-scheduled-jobs` Step 6 to explicitly instruct cron-agent sessions to READ sub-skill SKILL.md bodies before acting. HC-V0750-A1 is a fail-closed regression guard using a case-insensitive grep.

- **Workstream B (smart-connections-bridge + gather-semantic-related):** New mechanism `smart-connections-bridge@0.1.0` wraps the SC `.smart-env/multi/*.ajson` corpus with ops: `index-status`, `semantic-search` (cosine similarity, top-K), `find-related` (find-related on a vault note path). Uses `@xenova/transformers` with `TaylorAI/bge-micro-v2` (384-dim; same model as SC). New `cowork:gather-semantic-related` sub-skill calls sc-bridge and renders the callout + lag-line in Synopsis. Morning-briefing adds per-event semantic search; eod-review + weekly-review add find-related. Three engagement-type JSON files gain `render_aspects.semantic_related: "include"` (opt-out via `"skip"`). `.local/` added to `audit/sanctioned-dirs.js`; CS-MIG-1 counts bumped 41→42 / 32→33.

- **Workstream C (engagement-types path fix):** `onboard-scheduled-jobs` Step 2 now resolves engagement-type manifests via `workshop_manifest_path` instead of an in-vault path that doesn't exist at consumer install time.

- **Workstream D (`sauce update --bump-pins`):** New flag reads the brew-installed workshop's `platform/manifest.json`, derives target blueprint/mechanism versions, writes `ranch/platform-subscription.json`, and proceeds with the normal update — replacing the manual per-vault edit ritual. Workshop-self-vault detection is a v0.75.1 carry-forward (the workshop's own `platform-installed.json` lacks a `workshop_path` field, causing a no-op for workshop dogfood runs).

**Commits (S0–S17):** `e54021b` + `643b40b` + `f6992cb` (S1 + follow-ups), `43a98ce` (S2), `7728d4f` (S3), `ecb0f7c` + `0e75874` (S4 + follow-up), `9e4f839` (S5), `8e3d898` (S6), `ef83e4d` (S7), `0799b3a` (S8), `f68f0d2` (S9), `7a2bd85` (S10), `4ee7662` (S11), `f2d4bb6` (S12), `9a50278` (S13), `d73d2b6` (S14), `b974bac` (S15), `9005bc3` (S16 dogfood), S17 this commit.

**Smoke:** Preflight GREEN across all 23 harnesses. `sauce update` dogfood (S16) clean — `workshop_version: 0.75.0`; cowork@0.14.0; smart-connections-bridge@0.1.0 installed. Visual smoke (Obsidian) pending post-tag: `brew upgrade sauce` → `sauce update --bump-pins` per consumer vault → `sauce update` → `cowork:onboard-scheduled-jobs` (or manual Layer 3 recipe) → morning-briefing produces `> [!example]+ 🧩 Related context` callout when SC indexed; degrades gracefully when absent.

**Lessons:**
1. Cross-cycle test-assertion staleness reactivated by READ-form rewrites. Audit ALL `HC-*` assertions for stale literals BEFORE preflight — same lesson as v0.74.0. Consider a pre-preflight gate in `build-test-verify.md`.
2. Workshop install path differs from consumer install path: orchestrators drop `skills/orchestrators/` segment on install; sub-skills keep `skills/` segment. Document the two-path invariant in `code-conventions.md`.
3. Case-insensitive grep needed for `Use Skill` audits. HC-V0750-A1 now uses `-i`; 12 lowercase variants would have escaped a case-sensitive grep.
4. Synthetic test vectors must match real embedding dimensionality. BGE-micro-v2 is 384-dim; 4-dim fixtures returned garbage cosine similarity.
5. `@xenova/transformers` caches in `node_modules/.cache/`, not `~/.cache/huggingface/`. Check both Python and JS paths when debugging cache behavior.
6. `ranch/platform-installed.json` carries `module_directory: null` per-blueprint — not suitable as a source for blueprint metadata. Canonical source: per-blueprint `manifest.json` in `platform/blueprints/<bp>/`.
7. `sauce update --bump-pins` is a no-op for workshop-self-vault (missing `workshop_path` field). Workshop dogfood stays on `node platform/test/run-install.js .`.
8. New mechanisms installing to novel top-level directories must add that directory to `audit/sanctioned-dirs.js` in the same stage (sc-bridge → `.local/`).
9. CS-MIG-1 hardcoded sub-skill counts must be bumped whenever a new sub-skill is added — grep `run-claude-surface.js` in the same commit as the manifest `skills[]` entry.

See `Docs/plans/2026-05-22-v0.75.0-cowork-skill-routing-hardening-{design,plan,result}.md`.
